"""SmartInvest 4.8 - production-style core infrastructure and configuration.

This module deliberately contains no investment logic.  It provides:

* validated, environment-driven configuration;
* idempotent application logging;
* optional-dependency discovery with diagnostics;
* validated domain data structures;
* safe JSON conversion for NumPy, pandas and dataclass values; and
* thread-safe, transactional SQLite persistence with batch price writes.

Python 3.10+ is recommended.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import importlib
import importlib.metadata
import importlib.util
import json
import logging
import math
import os
import sqlite3
import sys
import threading
from contextlib import contextmanager
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field, is_dataclass
from datetime import date, datetime, timezone
from enum import Enum
from logging.handlers import RotatingFileHandler
from pathlib import Path
from types import ModuleType
from typing import Any, Final, Iterator, Mapping, Optional, Sequence

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd


APP_NAME: Final = "SmartInvest48"
UTC = timezone.utc


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Exceptions
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


class SmartInvestError(Exception):
    """Base exception for failures raised by the SmartInvest core."""


class ConfigurationError(SmartInvestError, ValueError):
    """Raised when application configuration is invalid."""


class PersistenceError(SmartInvestError):
    """Raised when a database operation cannot be completed."""


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Logging
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


class _UtcFormatter(logging.Formatter):
    converter = staticmethod(lambda timestamp: datetime.fromtimestamp(timestamp, UTC).timetuple())


def setup_logger(
    name: str = APP_NAME,
    log_file: str | os.PathLike[str] | None = "smartinvest_4_8.log",
    *,
    console_level: int = logging.INFO,
    file_level: int = logging.DEBUG,
    max_bytes: int = 5 * 1024 * 1024,
    backup_count: int = 3,
    propagate: bool = False,
) -> logging.Logger:
    """Create or reconfigure an idempotent SmartInvest logger.

    Only handlers installed by this function are replaced.  This avoids
    duplicate output when the module is reloaded while preserving handlers
    that an embedding application may have attached itself.
    """

    if max_bytes <= 0:
        raise ValueError("max_bytes must be greater than zero")
    if backup_count < 0:
        raise ValueError("backup_count cannot be negative")

    app_logger = logging.getLogger(name)
    app_logger.setLevel(min(console_level, file_level if log_file else console_level))
    app_logger.propagate = propagate

    for handler in list(app_logger.handlers):
        if getattr(handler, "_smartinvest_handler", False):
            app_logger.removeHandler(handler)
            handler.close()

    formatter = _UtcFormatter(
        "%(asctime)sZ | %(levelname)-8s | %(name)s | "
        "%(threadName)s | %(message)s",
        datefmt="%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S",
    )

    console = logging.StreamHandler(sys.stdout)
    console.setLevel(console_level)
    console.setFormatter(formatter)
    console._smartinvest_handler = True  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
    app_logger.addHandler(console)

    if log_file:
        destination = Path(log_file).expanduser()
        try:
            destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            rotating = RotatingFileHandler(
                destination,
                maxBytes=max_bytes,
                backupCount=backup_count,
                encoding="utf-8",
                delay=True,
            )
            rotating.setLevel(file_level)
            rotating.setFormatter(formatter)
            rotating._smartinvest_handler = True  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
            app_logger.addHandler(rotating)
        except OSError as exc:
            app_logger.warning(
                "File logging is unavailable at %s: %s",
                destination,
                exc,
            )

    return app_logger


logger = setup_logger()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Optional dependencies
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class DependencyStatus:
    import_name: str
    distribution_name: str
    available: bool
    version: str | None = None
    error: str | None = None


class DependencyRegistry:
    """Discover and lazily import optional packages.

    Discovery uses ``find_spec`` and therefore avoids importing heavy packages
    (TensorFlow, XGBoost, etc.) during startup.  ``load`` performs the real
    import and records an actionable diagnostic if it fails.
    """

    def __init__(self, dependencies: Mapping[str, str]) -> None:
        self._dependencies = dict(dependencies)
        self._statuses: dict[str, DependencyStatus] = {}
        self._modules: dict[str, ModuleType] = {}
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self.refresh()

    def refresh(self) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            self._statuses.clear()
            for import_name, distribution_name in self._dependencies.items():
                try:
                    found = importlib.util.find_spec(import_name) is not None
                    version = (
                        importlib.metadata.version(distribution_name)
                        if found
                        else None
                    )
                    self._statuses[import_name] = DependencyStatus(
                        import_name, distribution_name, found, version
                    )
                except importlib.metadata.PackageNotFoundError:
                    # A package can be importable without distribution metadata
                    # (for example, a vendored or editable installation).
                    self._statuses[import_name] = DependencyStatus(
                        import_name, distribution_name, True
                    )
                except (ImportError, ValueError) as exc:
                    self._statuses[import_name] = DependencyStatus(
                        import_name,
                        distribution_name,
                        False,
                        error=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
                    )

    def status(self, import_name: str) -> DependencyStatus:
        try:
            return self._statuses[import_name]
        except KeyError as exc:
            raise KeyError(f"Unknown optional dependency: {import_name}") from exc

    def is_available(self, import_name: str) -> bool:
        return self.status(import_name).available

    def load(self, import_name: str) -> ModuleType:
        with self._lock:
            if import_name in self._modules:
                return self._modules[import_name]
            status = self.status(import_name)
            if not status.available:
                detail = f" ({status.error})" if status.error else ""
                raise ImportError(
                    f"Optional dependency '{status.distribution_name}' is not "
                    f"installed{detail}."
                )
            try:
                module = importlib.import_module(import_name)
            except Exception as exc:
                self._statuses[import_name] = DependencyStatus(
                    status.import_name,
                    status.distribution_name,
                    False,
                    status.version,
                    f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
                )
                raise ImportError(
                    f"Optional dependency '{status.distribution_name}' was found "
                    f"but could not be imported: {exc}"
                ) from exc
            self._modules[import_name] = module
            return module

    def report(self) -> dict[str, DependencyStatus]:
        return dict(self._statuses)


OPTIONAL_DEPENDENCIES: Final = {
    "yfinance": "yfinance",
    "sklearn": "scikit-learn",
    "xgboost": "xgboost",
    "lightgbm": "lightgbm",
    "hmmlearn": "hmmlearn",
    "tensorflow": "tensorflow",
    "plotly": "plotly",
    "textblob": "textblob",
    "vaderSentiment": "vaderSentiment",
    "requests": "requests",
}

dependencies = DependencyRegistry(OPTIONAL_DEPENDENCIES)

# Backwards-compatible capability flags.  Use dependencies.load("package") at
# the point where the package is actually needed.
YFINANCE_AVAILABLE = dependencies.is_available("yfinance")
SKLEARN_AVAILABLE = dependencies.is_available("sklearn")
XGBOOST_AVAILABLE = dependencies.is_available("xgboost")
LIGHTGBM_AVAILABLE = dependencies.is_available("lightgbm")
HMMLEARN_AVAILABLE = dependencies.is_available("hmmlearn")
TF_AVAILABLE = dependencies.is_available("tensorflow")
PLOTLY_AVAILABLE = dependencies.is_available("plotly")
TEXTBLOB_AVAILABLE = dependencies.is_available("textblob")
VADER_AVAILABLE = dependencies.is_available("vaderSentiment")
REQUESTS_AVAILABLE = dependencies.is_available("requests")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configuration
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


DEFAULT_TICKERS: Final[tuple[str, ...]] = (
    "AAPL",
    "MSFT",
    "GOOGL",
    "AMZN",
    "NVDA",
    "JPM",
    "JNJ",
    "XOM",
    "BRK-B",
    "SPY",
    "QQQ",
    "GLD",
    "TLT",
    "BTC-USD",
    "ETH-USD",
)


def _env_int(env: Mapping[str, str], name: str, default: int) -> int:
    raw = env.get(name)
    if raw is None or not raw.strip():
        return default
    try:
        return int(raw)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise ConfigurationError(f"{name} must be an integer, got {raw!r}") from exc


def _env_float(env: Mapping[str, str], name: str, default: float) -> float:
    raw = env.get(name)
    if raw is None or not raw.strip():
        return default
    try:
        value = float(raw)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise ConfigurationError(f"{name} must be numeric, got {raw!r}") from exc
    if not math.isfinite(value):
        raise ConfigurationError(f"{name} must be finite")
    return value


def _core_normalise_tickers(values: Sequence[str]) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    tickers: list[str] = []
    seen: set[str] = set()
    for raw in values:
        ticker = str(raw).strip().upper()
        if not ticker:
            continue
        if any(char.isspace() for char in ticker):
            raise ConfigurationError(f"Ticker cannot contain whitespace: {ticker!r}")
        if ticker not in seen:
            tickers.append(ticker)
            seen.add(ticker)
    if not tickers:
        raise ConfigurationError("At least one ticker must be configured")
    return tuple(tickers)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class SecureConfig:
    """Validated runtime configuration with secrets sourced from the environment."""

    fred_api_key: str = field(default="", repr=False)
    news_api_key: str = field(default="", repr=False)
    alpha_vantage_key: str = field(default="", repr=False)
    default_tickers: tuple[str, ...] = DEFAULT_TICKERS
    benchmark: str = "SPY"
    lookback_days: int = 365 * 3
    risk_free_rate: float = 0.045
    database_path: Path = Path("smartinvest_4_8.db")
    log_path: Path | None = Path("smartinvest_4_8.log")

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        object.__setattr__(
            self, "default_tickers", _core_normalise_tickers(self.default_tickers)
        )
        benchmark = self.benchmark.strip().upper()
        if not benchmark or any(char.isspace() for char in benchmark):
            raise ConfigurationError("benchmark must be a non-empty ticker")
        object.__setattr__(self, "benchmark", benchmark)

        if self.lookback_days < 30:
            raise ConfigurationError("lookback_days must be at least 30")
        if self.lookback_days > 365 * 50:
            raise ConfigurationError("lookback_days cannot exceed 50 years")
        if not math.isfinite(self.risk_free_rate):
            raise ConfigurationError("risk_free_rate must be finite")
        if not -0.25 <= self.risk_free_rate <= 1.0:
            raise ConfigurationError("risk_free_rate must be between -0.25 and 1.0")

        database_path = Path(self.database_path).expanduser()
        if database_path.exists() and database_path.is_dir():
            raise ConfigurationError("database_path points to a directory")
        object.__setattr__(self, "database_path", database_path)
        if self.log_path is not None:
            object.__setattr__(self, "log_path", Path(self.log_path).expanduser())

    @classmethod
    def from_env(
        cls, env: Mapping[str, str] | None = None
    ) -> "SecureConfig":
        source = os.environ if env is None else env
        raw_tickers = source.get("SMARTINVEST_TICKERS", "")
        tickers = (
            tuple(raw_tickers.split(","))
            if raw_tickers.strip()
            else DEFAULT_TICKERS
        )
        log_value = source.get("SMARTINVEST_LOG_PATH", "smartinvest_4_8.log")
        return cls(
            fred_api_key=source.get("FRED_API_KEY", "").strip(),
            news_api_key=source.get("NEWS_API_KEY", "").strip(),
            alpha_vantage_key=source.get("ALPHA_VANTAGE_KEY", "").strip(),
            default_tickers=tickers,
            benchmark=source.get("SMARTINVEST_BENCHMARK", "SPY"),
            lookback_days=_env_int(
                source, "SMARTINVEST_LOOKBACK_DAYS", 365 * 3
            ),
            risk_free_rate=_env_float(
                source, "SMARTINVEST_RISK_FREE_RATE", 0.045
            ),
            database_path=Path(
                source.get("SMARTINVEST_DB_PATH", "smartinvest_4_8.db")
            ),
            log_path=Path(log_value) if log_value.strip() else None,
        )

    def missing_api_keys(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
        missing = []
        if not self.fred_api_key:
            missing.append("FRED_API_KEY")
        if not self.news_api_key:
            missing.append("NEWS_API_KEY")
        if not self.alpha_vantage_key:
            missing.append("ALPHA_VANTAGE_KEY")
        return tuple(missing)

    # Read-only aliases preserve compatibility with the original uppercase API.
    @property
    def FRED_API_KEY(self) -> str:
        return self.fred_api_key

    @property
    def NEWS_API_KEY(self) -> str:
        return self.news_api_key

    @property
    def ALPHA_VANTAGE_KEY(self) -> str:
        return self.alpha_vantage_key

    @property
    def DEFAULT_TICKERS(self) -> list[str]:
        return list(self.default_tickers)

    @property
    def BENCHMARK(self) -> str:
        return self.benchmark

    @property
    def LOOKBACK_DAYS(self) -> int:
        return self.lookback_days

    @property
    def RISK_FREE_RATE(self) -> float:
        return self.risk_free_rate


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Domain data structures
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _core_finite_float(name: str, value: float) -> float:
    number = float(value)
    if not math.isfinite(number):
        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be finite")
    return number


def _unit_interval(name: str, value: float) -> float:
    number = _core_finite_float(name, value)
    if not 0.0 <= number <= 1.0:
        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be between 0 and 1")
    return number


def _non_empty(name: str, value: str) -> str:
    cleaned = str(value).strip()
    if not cleaned:
        raise ValueError(f"{name} cannot be empty")
    return cleaned


class RegimeName(str, Enum):
    BULL = "bull"
    BEAR = "bear"
    SIDEWAYS = "sideways"
    HIGH_VOLATILITY = "high_volatility"
    CRISIS = "crisis"
    UNKNOWN = "unknown"


@dataclass(slots=True)
class MarketRegime:
    name: str
    confidence: float
    characteristics: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    recommended_adjustments: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        self.name = _non_empty("name", self.name).lower()
        self.confidence = _unit_interval("confidence", self.confidence)
        self.characteristics = {
            str(key): _core_finite_float(f"characteristics[{key!r}]", value)
            for key, value in self.characteristics.items()
        }
        self.recommended_adjustments = {
            str(key): _core_finite_float(f"recommended_adjustments[{key!r}]", value)
            for key, value in self.recommended_adjustments.items()
        }


@dataclass(slots=True)
class Strategy:
    name: str
    strategy_type: str
    regime_target: str
    allocations: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    expected_return: str = "medium"
    expected_volatility: str = "medium"
    description: str = ""
    rationale: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    score: float = 0.0

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        self.name = _non_empty("name", self.name)
        self.strategy_type = _non_empty("strategy_type", self.strategy_type).lower()
        self.regime_target = _non_empty("regime_target", self.regime_target).lower()
        valid_bands = {"low", "medium", "high"}
        self.expected_return = self.expected_return.lower()
        self.expected_volatility = self.expected_volatility.lower()
        if self.expected_return not in valid_bands:
            raise ValueError("expected_return must be low, medium or high")
        if self.expected_volatility not in valid_bands:
            raise ValueError("expected_volatility must be low, medium or high")
        self.allocations = {
            _non_empty("allocation ticker", ticker).upper(): _core_finite_float(
                f"allocations[{ticker!r}]", weight
            )
            for ticker, weight in self.allocations.items()
        }
        if any(weight < 0 for weight in self.allocations.values()):
            raise ValueError("allocation weights cannot be negative")
        if self.allocations and not math.isclose(
            sum(self.allocations.values()), 1.0, rel_tol=1e-6, abs_tol=1e-6
        ):
            raise ValueError("allocation weights must sum to 1.0")
        self.score = _core_finite_float("score", self.score)
        self.rationale = [str(item).strip() for item in self.rationale if str(item).strip()]


@dataclass(slots=True)
class EnsemblePrediction:
    ticker: str
    horizon_days: int
    xgboost_pred: float | None = None
    rf_pred: float | None = None
    lstm_pred: float | None = None
    rl_pred: float | None = None
    ensemble_pred: float = 0.0
    confidence: float = 0.0
    model_weights: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    direction: str = "neutral"

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        self.ticker = _non_empty("ticker", self.ticker).upper()
        if self.horizon_days <= 0:
            raise ValueError("horizon_days must be greater than zero")
        for attribute in (
            "xgboost_pred",
            "rf_pred",
            "lstm_pred",
            "rl_pred",
        ):
            value = getattr(self, attribute)
            if value is not None:
                setattr(self, attribute, _core_finite_float(attribute, value))
        self.ensemble_pred = _core_finite_float("ensemble_pred", self.ensemble_pred)
        self.confidence = _unit_interval("confidence", self.confidence)
        self.direction = self.direction.lower()
        if self.direction not in {"up", "down", "neutral"}:
            raise ValueError("direction must be up, down or neutral")
        self.model_weights = {
            str(name): _unit_interval(f"model_weights[{name!r}]", weight)
            for name, weight in self.model_weights.items()
        }
        if self.model_weights and not math.isclose(
            sum(self.model_weights.values()), 1.0, rel_tol=1e-6, abs_tol=1e-6
        ):
            raise ValueError("model_weights must sum to 1.0")


@dataclass(slots=True)
class ScenarioResult:
    scenario_name: str
    description: str
    portfolio_return: float
    max_loss: float
    hedging_suggestions: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    affected_positions: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    recovery_time_estimate: str = ""

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        self.scenario_name = _non_empty("scenario_name", self.scenario_name)
        self.portfolio_return = _core_finite_float(
            "portfolio_return", self.portfolio_return
        )
        self.max_loss = _core_finite_float("max_loss", self.max_loss)
        self.affected_positions = {
            str(ticker).upper(): _core_finite_float(
                f"affected_positions[{ticker!r}]", value
            )
            for ticker, value in self.affected_positions.items()
        }


@dataclass(slots=True)
class RiskReport:
    ticker: str
    var_95: float
    var_99: float
    cvar_95: float
    max_drawdown: float
    volatility_annual: float
    beta: float
    correlation_risk: float
    risk_level: str
    main_risk_sources: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    sortino_ratio: float = 0.0

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        self.ticker = _non_empty("ticker", self.ticker).upper()
        for attribute in (
            "var_95",
            "var_99",
            "cvar_95",
            "max_drawdown",
            "volatility_annual",
            "beta",
            "correlation_risk",
            "sortino_ratio",
        ):
            setattr(self, attribute, _core_finite_float(attribute, getattr(self, attribute)))
        if self.volatility_annual < 0:
            raise ValueError("volatility_annual cannot be negative")
        self.risk_level = _non_empty("risk_level", self.risk_level).lower()


@dataclass(slots=True)
class XAIExplanation:
    ticker: str
    action: str
    allocation_pct: float
    reasons: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    sentiment_score: float = 0.0
    momentum_score: float = 0.0
    value_score: float = 0.0
    risk_score: float = 0.0
    confidence: float = 0.0

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        self.ticker = _non_empty("ticker", self.ticker).upper()
        self.action = _non_empty("action", self.action).lower()
        if self.action not in {"increase", "decrease", "hold", "buy", "sell"}:
            raise ValueError("Unsupported XAI action")
        self.allocation_pct = _core_finite_float("allocation_pct", self.allocation_pct)
        for attribute in (
            "sentiment_score",
            "momentum_score",
            "value_score",
            "risk_score",
        ):
            setattr(self, attribute, _core_finite_float(attribute, getattr(self, attribute)))
        self.confidence = _unit_interval("confidence", self.confidence)


@dataclass(slots=True)
class BacktestResult:
    strategy_name: str
    period: str
    assets: list[str]
    total_return: float
    cagr: float
    max_drawdown: float
    sharpe_ratio: float
    sortino_ratio: float
    calmar_ratio: float
    win_rate: float
    trades: int
    equity_curve: pd.Series = field(default_factory=lambda: pd.Series(dtype=float))
    monthly_returns: pd.Series = field(default_factory=lambda: pd.Series(dtype=float))
    stop_loss_enabled: bool = False
    stop_loss_triggers: int = 0
    stop_loss_events: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        self.strategy_name = _non_empty("strategy_name", self.strategy_name)
        self.period = _non_empty("period", self.period)
        self.assets = list(_core_normalise_tickers(self.assets))
        for attribute in (
            "total_return",
            "cagr",
            "max_drawdown",
            "sharpe_ratio",
            "sortino_ratio",
            "calmar_ratio",
        ):
            setattr(self, attribute, _core_finite_float(attribute, getattr(self, attribute)))
        self.win_rate = _unit_interval("win_rate", self.win_rate)
        if self.trades < 0 or self.stop_loss_triggers < 0:
            raise ValueError("trade and stop-loss counts cannot be negative")
        if not isinstance(self.equity_curve, pd.Series):
            self.equity_curve = pd.Series(self.equity_curve, dtype=float)
        if not isinstance(self.monthly_returns, pd.Series):
            self.monthly_returns = pd.Series(self.monthly_returns, dtype=float)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# JSON serialization
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def to_jsonable(value: Any) -> Any:
    """Convert common analytical values to strict JSON-compatible objects."""

    if value is pd.NA or value is pd.NaT:
        return None
    if value is None or isinstance(value, (str, bool, int)):
        return value
    if isinstance(value, float):
        return value if math.isfinite(value) else None
    if isinstance(value, Enum):
        return to_jsonable(value.value)
    if isinstance(value, (datetime, date, pd.Timestamp)):
        if isinstance(value, datetime) and value.tzinfo is None:
            value = value.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
        return value.isoformat()
    if isinstance(value, Path):
        return str(value)
    if isinstance(value, np.generic):
        return to_jsonable(value.item())
    if isinstance(value, np.ndarray):
        return [to_jsonable(item) for item in value.tolist()]
    if isinstance(value, pd.Series):
        return {
            "index": [to_jsonable(item) for item in value.index.tolist()],
            "values": [to_jsonable(item) for item in value.tolist()],
            "name": to_jsonable(value.name),
        }
    if isinstance(value, pd.DataFrame):
        return [
            {str(key): to_jsonable(item) for key, item in row.items()}
            for row in value.to_dict(orient="records")
        ]
    if is_dataclass(value) and not isinstance(value, type):
        return to_jsonable(asdict(value))
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return {str(key): to_jsonable(item) for key, item in value.items()}
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple, set, frozenset)):
        return [to_jsonable(item) for item in value]
    raise TypeError(f"Object of type {type(value).__name__} is not JSON serializable")


def json_dumps(value: Any) -> str:
    return json.dumps(
        to_jsonable(value),
        ensure_ascii=False,
        allow_nan=False,
        separators=(",", ":"),
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# SQLite persistence
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


class Database:
    """Thread-safe SQLite persistence using short-lived connections.

    Each operation receives its own connection, while writes are serialized
    inside this process. SQLite WAL mode and ``busy_timeout`` improve behaviour
    when other processes read or write the same database.
    """

    SCHEMA_VERSION: Final = 1

    def __init__(
        self,
        db_path: str | os.PathLike[str] = "smartinvest_4_8.db",
        *,
        timeout: float = 30.0,
        batch_size: int = 1_000,
    ) -> None:
        self.db_path = Path(db_path).expanduser().resolve()
        if timeout <= 0:
            raise ValueError("timeout must be greater than zero")
        if batch_size <= 0:
            raise ValueError("batch_size must be greater than zero")
        if self.db_path.exists() and self.db_path.is_dir():
            raise ValueError("db_path points to a directory")
        self.timeout = float(timeout)
        self.batch_size = int(batch_size)
        self._write_lock = threading.RLock()
        try:
            self.db_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            self._init_tables()
        except (OSError, sqlite3.Error) as exc:
            raise PersistenceError(
                f"Could not initialize database at {self.db_path}: {exc}"
            ) from exc

    def _connect(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
        conn = sqlite3.connect(
            str(self.db_path),
            timeout=self.timeout,
            check_same_thread=False,
            isolation_level=None,
        )
        conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON")
        conn.execute(f"PRAGMA busy_timeout = {int(self.timeout * 1000)}")
        return conn

    @contextmanager
    def _transaction(self, *, write: bool = False) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:
        lock = self._write_lock if write else _NullLock()
        with lock:
            conn = self._connect()
            try:
                conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE" if write else "BEGIN")
                yield conn
                conn.commit()
            except Exception:
                conn.rollback()
                raise
            finally:
                conn.close()

    def _init_tables(self) -> None:
        schema = """
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS price_cache (
                ticker TEXT NOT NULL,
                date TEXT NOT NULL,
                open REAL,
                high REAL,
                low REAL,
                close REAL NOT NULL,
                volume REAL,
                PRIMARY KEY (ticker, date)
            );
            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_price_cache_date
                ON price_cache(date);

            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS regime_history (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                timestamp TEXT NOT NULL,
                regime TEXT NOT NULL,
                confidence REAL NOT NULL,
                details TEXT NOT NULL
            );
            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_regime_history_timestamp
                ON regime_history(timestamp);

            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS strategy_log (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                timestamp TEXT NOT NULL,
                strategy_name TEXT NOT NULL,
                regime TEXT NOT NULL,
                expected_return TEXT NOT NULL,
                score REAL NOT NULL,
                allocations TEXT NOT NULL
            );

            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS backtest_results (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                timestamp TEXT NOT NULL,
                strategy TEXT NOT NULL,
                period TEXT NOT NULL,
                cagr REAL NOT NULL,
                sharpe REAL NOT NULL,
                max_drawdown REAL NOT NULL,
                details TEXT NOT NULL
            );

            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS scenario_log (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                timestamp TEXT NOT NULL,
                scenario TEXT NOT NULL,
                portfolio_return REAL NOT NULL,
                details TEXT NOT NULL
            );
        """
        with self._write_lock:
            conn = self._connect()
            try:
                conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL")
                conn.execute("PRAGMA synchronous = NORMAL")
                conn.executescript(schema)
                conn.execute(f"PRAGMA user_version = {self.SCHEMA_VERSION}")
            finally:
                conn.close()

    @staticmethod
    def _timestamp() -> str:
        return datetime.now(UTC).isoformat()

    @staticmethod
    def _optional_number(value: Any) -> float | None:
        if value is None or pd.isna(value):
            return None
        number = float(value)
        return number if math.isfinite(number) else None

    def cache_prices(self, ticker: str, frame: pd.DataFrame) -> int:
        """Validate and UPSERT OHLCV rows; return the number of rows submitted."""

        symbol = _non_empty("ticker", ticker).upper()
        if not isinstance(frame, pd.DataFrame):
            raise TypeError("frame must be a pandas DataFrame")
        if frame.empty:
            return 0

        columns = {str(column).strip().lower(): column for column in frame.columns}
        if "close" not in columns:
            raise ValueError("Price frame must contain a Close column")

        rows: list[tuple[Any, ...]] = []
        for index, row in frame.iterrows():
            timestamp = pd.Timestamp(index)
            if pd.isna(timestamp):
                raise ValueError("Price frame contains an invalid date index")
            close = self._optional_number(row[columns["close"]])
            if close is None or close <= 0:
                raise ValueError(f"Invalid Close value for {symbol} at {index!r}")
            date_key = (
                timestamp.tz_convert(UTC).isoformat()
                if timestamp.tzinfo is not None
                else timestamp.isoformat()
            )
            rows.append(
                (
                    symbol,
                    date_key,
                    self._optional_number(row.get(columns.get("open"))),
                    self._optional_number(row.get(columns.get("high"))),
                    self._optional_number(row.get(columns.get("low"))),
                    close,
                    self._optional_number(row.get(columns.get("volume"))),
                )
            )

        statement = """
            INSERT INTO price_cache
                (ticker, date, open, high, low, close, volume)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            ON CONFLICT(ticker, date) DO UPDATE SET
                open = excluded.open,
                high = excluded.high,
                low = excluded.low,
                close = excluded.close,
                volume = excluded.volume
        """
        try:
            with self._transaction(write=True) as conn:
                for start in range(0, len(rows), self.batch_size):
                    conn.executemany(statement, rows[start : start + self.batch_size])
        except sqlite3.Error as exc:
            raise PersistenceError(f"Could not cache prices for {symbol}: {exc}") from exc
        return len(rows)

    def get_cached_prices(
        self,
        ticker: str,
        *,
        start: datetime | date | str | None = None,
        end: datetime | date | str | None = None,
    ) -> pd.DataFrame:
        symbol = _non_empty("ticker", ticker).upper()
        query = """
            SELECT date, open, high, low, close, volume
            FROM price_cache WHERE ticker = ?
        """
        parameters: list[Any] = [symbol]
        if start is not None:
            query += " AND date >= ?"
            parameters.append(
                start.isoformat() if isinstance(start, (date, datetime)) else str(start)
            )
        if end is not None:
            query += " AND date <= ?"
            parameters.append(
                end.isoformat() if isinstance(end, (date, datetime)) else str(end)
            )
        query += " ORDER BY date"
        try:
            with self._transaction() as conn:
                records = conn.execute(query, parameters).fetchall()
        except sqlite3.Error as exc:
            raise PersistenceError(
                f"Could not read cached prices for {symbol}: {exc}"
            ) from exc
        if not records:
            return pd.DataFrame(
                columns=["Open", "High", "Low", "Close", "Volume"],
                index=pd.DatetimeIndex([], name="Date"),
            )
        result = pd.DataFrame([dict(record) for record in records])
        result["date"] = pd.to_datetime(result["date"], utc=True)
        result = result.set_index("date")
        result.index.name = "Date"
        return result.rename(
            columns={
                "open": "Open",
                "high": "High",
                "low": "Low",
                "close": "Close",
                "volume": "Volume",
            }
        )

    def _insert_log(self, statement: str, parameters: Sequence[Any]) -> int:
        try:
            with self._transaction(write=True) as conn:
                cursor = conn.execute(statement, tuple(parameters))
                if cursor.lastrowid is None:
                    raise PersistenceError("SQLite did not return a row id")
                return int(cursor.lastrowid)
        except sqlite3.Error as exc:
            raise PersistenceError(f"Could not persist log record: {exc}") from exc

    def log_regime(self, regime: MarketRegime) -> int:
        return self._insert_log(
            """
            INSERT INTO regime_history
                (timestamp, regime, confidence, details)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
            """,
            (
                self._timestamp(),
                regime.name,
                regime.confidence,
                json_dumps(
                    {
                        "characteristics": regime.characteristics,
                        "recommended_adjustments": regime.recommended_adjustments,
                    }
                ),
            ),
        )

    def log_strategy(self, strategy: Strategy, regime: str) -> int:
        return self._insert_log(
            """
            INSERT INTO strategy_log
                (timestamp, strategy_name, regime, expected_return, score, allocations)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """,
            (
                self._timestamp(),
                strategy.name,
                _non_empty("regime", regime).lower(),
                strategy.expected_return,
                strategy.score,
                json_dumps(strategy.allocations),
            ),
        )

    def log_backtest(self, result: BacktestResult) -> int:
        details = {
            "total_return": result.total_return,
            "sortino_ratio": result.sortino_ratio,
            "calmar_ratio": result.calmar_ratio,
            "win_rate": result.win_rate,
            "trades": result.trades,
            "assets": result.assets,
            "equity_curve": result.equity_curve,
            "monthly_returns": result.monthly_returns,
            "stop_loss_enabled": result.stop_loss_enabled,
            "stop_loss_triggers": result.stop_loss_triggers,
            "stop_loss_events": result.stop_loss_events,
        }
        return self._insert_log(
            """
            INSERT INTO backtest_results
                (timestamp, strategy, period, cagr, sharpe, max_drawdown, details)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """,
            (
                self._timestamp(),
                result.strategy_name,
                result.period,
                result.cagr,
                result.sharpe_ratio,
                result.max_drawdown,
                json_dumps(details),
            ),
        )

    def log_scenario(self, result: ScenarioResult) -> int:
        return self._insert_log(
            """
            INSERT INTO scenario_log
                (timestamp, scenario, portfolio_return, details)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
            """,
            (
                self._timestamp(),
                result.scenario_name,
                result.portfolio_return,
                json_dumps(result),
            ),
        )

    def health_check(self) -> bool:
        try:
            with self._transaction() as conn:
                return conn.execute("SELECT 1").fetchone()[0] == 1
        except sqlite3.Error as exc:
            logger.error("Database health check failed: %s", exc)
            return False


class _NullLock:
    def __enter__(self) -> "_NullLock":
        return self

    def __exit__(self, *_: Any) -> None:
        return None


__all__ = [
