#MODUL 2


"""SmartInvest 4.8 - Module 2: robust market-data engine.

The engine provides validated daily OHLCV acquisition, two-level caching,
bounded retry/rate limiting, safe concurrent downloads, deterministic opt-in
synthetic data, technical feature engineering and compact market overviews.

It is designed to work with ``smartinvest_core.py`` (Module 1).  Synthetic
prices are disabled by default and are never written to the persistent cache.
Yahoo Finance data is intended for research; verify licensing and suitability
before using it in a production trading system.
"""


import hashlib
import logging
import math
import random
import re
import threading
import time
from collections import OrderedDict
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from enum import Enum
from functools import wraps
from typing import Any, Callable, Final, ParamSpec, TypeVar

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

# This distribution is a single-file build. Core names are defined above.


UTC: Final = timezone.utc
LOGGER = logging.getLogger(f"{APP_NAME}.market_data")
_TICKER_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^[A-Z0-9^.=/_:-]{1,32}$")
_REQUIRED_OHLC = ("Open", "High", "Low", "Close")
_STANDARD_COLUMNS = ("Open", "High", "Low", "Close", "Volume")
_P = ParamSpec("_P")
_R = TypeVar("_R")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Exceptions and provenance
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


class MarketDataError(SmartInvestError):
    """Base exception for Module 2."""


class DataUnavailableError(MarketDataError):
    """Raised when neither live nor acceptable cached data is available."""


class DataValidationError(MarketDataError, ValueError):
    """Raised when market data fails structural or quality validation."""


class DownloadError(MarketDataError):
    """Raised when a remote market-data request fails."""


class DataSource(str, Enum):
    MEMORY = "memory"
    CACHE = "cache"
    CACHE_STALE = "cache_stale"
    LIVE = "live"
    SYNTHETIC = "synthetic"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class DataQualityReport:
    """Diagnostics produced by the most recent OHLCV validation."""

    ticker: str
    input_rows: int
    output_rows: int
    dropped_rows: int
    duplicate_rows: int
    first_date: str | None
    last_date: str | None

    @property
    def dropped_ratio(self) -> float:
        return self.dropped_rows / self.input_rows if self.input_rows else 0.0


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Validated configuration
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _positive_int(name: str, value: int) -> int:
    if isinstance(value, bool) or int(value) != value or value <= 0:
        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be a positive integer")
    return int(value)


def _positive_float(name: str, value: float) -> float:
    number = float(value)
    if not math.isfinite(number) or number <= 0:
        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be a positive finite number")
    return number


def _normalise_windows(name: str, windows: tuple[int, ...] | list[int]) -> tuple[int, ...]:
    values = tuple(_positive_int(f"{name} item", item) for item in windows)
    if not values:
        raise ValueError(f"{name} cannot be empty")
    if len(values) != len(set(values)):
        raise ValueError(f"{name} cannot contain duplicates")
    return values


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class CacheConfig:
    """Freshness, coverage and bounded in-memory-cache settings."""

    max_staleness_bdays: int = 2
    min_coverage_ratio: float = 0.80
    memory_ttl_seconds: float = 900.0
    memory_max_entries: int = 256
    trading_days_per_week: float = 5.0

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.max_staleness_bdays < 0:
            raise ValueError("max_staleness_bdays cannot be negative")
        if not 0.0 <= self.min_coverage_ratio <= 1.0:
            raise ValueError("min_coverage_ratio must be between 0 and 1")
        if not math.isfinite(self.memory_ttl_seconds) or self.memory_ttl_seconds < 0:
            raise ValueError("memory_ttl_seconds must be finite and non-negative")
        _positive_int("memory_max_entries", self.memory_max_entries)
        if not 1.0 <= self.trading_days_per_week <= 7.0:
            raise ValueError("trading_days_per_week must be between 1 and 7")


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class DownloadConfig:
    """Remote-download policy. Synthetic fallback is intentionally opt-in."""

    max_attempts: int = 3
    base_delay_seconds: float = 0.75
    max_delay_seconds: float = 8.0
    timeout_seconds: float = 15.0
    calls_per_second: float = 4.0
    auto_adjust: bool = True
    repair_prices: bool = True
    allow_stale_cache: bool = True
    allow_synthetic_fallback: bool = False

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        _positive_int("max_attempts", self.max_attempts)
        _positive_float("base_delay_seconds", self.base_delay_seconds)
        _positive_float("max_delay_seconds", self.max_delay_seconds)
        _positive_float("timeout_seconds", self.timeout_seconds)
        _positive_float("calls_per_second", self.calls_per_second)
        if self.max_delay_seconds < self.base_delay_seconds:
            raise ValueError(
                "max_delay_seconds cannot be smaller than base_delay_seconds"
            )


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class QualityConfig:
    """OHLCV validation thresholds."""

    max_invalid_row_ratio: float = 0.05
    reject_future_days: int = 1
    require_volume: bool = False

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not 0.0 <= self.max_invalid_row_ratio <= 1.0:
            raise ValueError("max_invalid_row_ratio must be between 0 and 1")
        if self.reject_future_days < 0:
            raise ValueError("reject_future_days cannot be negative")


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class FeatureConfig:
    """Windows and numerical conventions for technical features."""

    return_windows: tuple[int, ...] = (1, 5, 20, 60)
    sma_windows: tuple[int, ...] = (5, 10, 20, 50, 200)
    vol_windows: tuple[int, ...] = (20, 60)
    rsi_window: int = 14
    macd_fast: int = 12
    macd_slow: int = 26
    macd_signal: int = 9
    bb_window: int = 20
    bb_std: float = 2.0
    atr_window: int = 14
    volume_window: int = 20
    annualization_factor: int = 252

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        object.__setattr__(
            self,
            "return_windows",
            _normalise_windows("return_windows", self.return_windows),
        )
        object.__setattr__(
            self,
            "sma_windows",
            _normalise_windows("sma_windows", self.sma_windows),
        )
        object.__setattr__(
            self,
            "vol_windows",
            _normalise_windows("vol_windows", self.vol_windows),
        )
        for name in (
            "rsi_window",
            "macd_fast",
            "macd_slow",
            "macd_signal",
            "bb_window",
            "atr_window",
            "volume_window",
            "annualization_factor",
        ):
            _positive_int(name, getattr(self, name))
        if self.macd_fast >= self.macd_slow:
            raise ValueError("macd_fast must be smaller than macd_slow")
        _positive_float("bb_std", self.bb_std)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Retry and rate limiting
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def retry_with_backoff(
    *,
    max_attempts: int,
    base_delay: float,
    max_delay: float,
    retry_if: Callable[[BaseException], bool],
    sleeper: Callable[[float], None] = time.sleep,
) -> Callable[[Callable[_P, _R]], Callable[_P, _R]]:
    """Retry only explicitly classified transient failures."""

    _positive_int("max_attempts", max_attempts)
    _positive_float("base_delay", base_delay)
    _positive_float("max_delay", max_delay)

    def decorator(function: Callable[_P, _R]) -> Callable[_P, _R]:
        @wraps(function)
        def wrapped(*args: _P.args, **kwargs: _P.kwargs) -> _R:
            last_error: BaseException | None = None
            for attempt in range(1, max_attempts + 1):
                try:
                    return function(*args, **kwargs)
                except BaseException as exc:
                    if isinstance(exc, (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit)):
                        raise
                    last_error = exc
                    if attempt >= max_attempts or not retry_if(exc):
                        raise
                    exponential = min(max_delay, base_delay * (2 ** (attempt - 1)))
                    delay = min(max_delay, exponential + random.uniform(0.0, 0.3))
                    LOGGER.warning(
                        "%s failed on attempt %d/%d: %s; retrying in %.2fs",
                        function.__name__,
                        attempt,
                        max_attempts,
                        exc,
                        delay,
                    )
                    sleeper(delay)
            assert last_error is not None
            raise last_error

        return wrapped

    return decorator


class RateLimiter:
    """Thread-safe fixed-interval limiter for outbound requests."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        calls_per_second: float = 4.0,
        *,
        monotonic: Callable[[], float] = time.monotonic,
        sleeper: Callable[[float], None] = time.sleep,
    ) -> None:
        self._minimum_interval = 1.0 / _positive_float(
            "calls_per_second", calls_per_second
        )
        self._monotonic = monotonic
        self._sleeper = sleeper
        self._last_call = 0.0
        self._lock = threading.Lock()

    def wait(self) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            now = self._monotonic()
            delay = self._minimum_interval - (now - self._last_call)
            if delay > 0:
                self._sleeper(delay)
            self._last_call = self._monotonic()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Market-data engine
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


class MarketDataEngine:
    """Acquire, validate, cache and transform daily market data."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        db: Database,
        cache_cfg: CacheConfig | None = None,
        feat_cfg: FeatureConfig | None = None,
        download_cfg: DownloadConfig | None = None,
        quality_cfg: QualityConfig | None = None,
        *,
        downloader: Callable[..., pd.DataFrame] | None = None,
        now: Callable[[], datetime] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        required_db_methods = ("get_cached_prices", "cache_prices")
        missing = [name for name in required_db_methods if not callable(getattr(db, name, None))]
        if missing:
            raise TypeError(
                "db does not implement the Module 1 database API: "
                + ", ".join(missing)
            )

        self.db = db
        self.cache_cfg = cache_cfg or CacheConfig()
        self.feat_cfg = feat_cfg or FeatureConfig()
        self.download_cfg = download_cfg or DownloadConfig()
        self.quality_cfg = quality_cfg or QualityConfig()
        self._downloader = downloader
        self._now = now or (lambda: datetime.now(UTC))
        self._rate_limiter = RateLimiter(self.download_cfg.calls_per_second)

        self._cache: OrderedDict[tuple[str, int], tuple[float, pd.DataFrame]] = (
            OrderedDict()
        )
        self._cache_lock = threading.RLock()
        self._key_locks: dict[tuple[str, int], threading.Lock] = {}
        self._key_locks_lock = threading.Lock()
        self._diagnostics_lock = threading.Lock()
        self._quality_reports: dict[str, DataQualityReport] = {}
        self._last_errors: dict[str, str] = {}

    @property
    def last_errors(self) -> dict[str, str]:
        with self._diagnostics_lock:
            return dict(self._last_errors)

    @property
    def quality_reports(self) -> dict[str, DataQualityReport]:
        with self._diagnostics_lock:
            return dict(self._quality_reports)

    @staticmethod
    def _normalise_ticker(ticker: str) -> str:
        symbol = str(ticker).strip().upper()
        if not _TICKER_PATTERN.fullmatch(symbol):
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid ticker: {ticker!r}")
        return symbol

    @staticmethod
    def _normalise_days(days: int) -> int:
        return _positive_int("days", days)

    def _utc_now(self) -> datetime:
        current = self._now()
        if current.tzinfo is None:
            current = current.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
        return current.astimezone(UTC)

    def _lock_for(self, key: tuple[str, int]) -> threading.Lock:
        with self._key_locks_lock:
            return self._key_locks.setdefault(key, threading.Lock())

    @staticmethod
    def _copy_frame(frame: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
        copied = frame.copy(deep=True)
        copied.attrs = dict(frame.attrs)
        return copied

    def _memory_get(
        self,
        key: tuple[str, int],
        *,
        allow_synthetic: bool,
    ) -> pd.DataFrame | None:
        with self._cache_lock:
            cached = self._cache.get(key)
            if cached is None:
                return None
            inserted_at, frame = cached
            if time.monotonic() - inserted_at >= self.cache_cfg.memory_ttl_seconds:
                self._cache.pop(key, None)
                return None
            self._cache.move_to_end(key)
            if frame.attrs.get("is_synthetic", False) and not allow_synthetic:
                return None
            result = self._copy_frame(frame)
            result.attrs["cache_layer"] = DataSource.MEMORY.value
            return result

    def _memory_put(self, key: tuple[str, int], frame: pd.DataFrame) -> None:
        stored = self._copy_frame(frame)
        with self._cache_lock:
            self._cache[key] = (time.monotonic(), stored)
            self._cache.move_to_end(key)
            while len(self._cache) > self.cache_cfg.memory_max_entries:
                self._cache.popitem(last=False)

    def clear_memory_cache(self, ticker: str | None = None) -> int:
        """Clear all memory entries, or only entries for one ticker."""

        with self._cache_lock:
            if ticker is None:
                count = len(self._cache)
                self._cache.clear()
                return count
            symbol = self._normalise_ticker(ticker)
            keys = [key for key in self._cache if key[0] == symbol]
            for key in keys:
                self._cache.pop(key, None)
            return len(keys)

    def _business_days_since(self, timestamp: pd.Timestamp) -> int:
        if timestamp.tzinfo is None:
            timestamp = timestamp.tz_localize(UTC)
        last_day = timestamp.tz_convert(UTC).date()
        today = self._utc_now().date()
        if last_day > today:
            return 0
        return int(np.busday_count(last_day, today))

    def _coverage_ratio(self, frame: pd.DataFrame, days: int) -> float:
        expected = max(
            1,
            math.ceil(days * self.cache_cfg.trading_days_per_week / 7.0),
        )
        return min(1.0, frame.index.normalize().nunique() / expected)

    @staticmethod
    def _flatten_columns(frame: pd.DataFrame, ticker: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
        result = frame.copy()
        if not isinstance(result.columns, pd.MultiIndex):
            return result

        # yfinance has returned both (field, ticker) and (ticker, field)
        # layouts across call styles. Select the requested symbol if present.
        for level in range(result.columns.nlevels):
            values = {
                str(value).upper()
                for value in result.columns.get_level_values(level)
            }
            if ticker in values:
                result = result.xs(ticker, axis=1, level=level, drop_level=True)
                break
        if isinstance(result.columns, pd.MultiIndex):
            result.columns = [
                next(
                    (
                        str(part)
                        for part in column
                        if str(part).lower()
                        in {"open", "high", "low", "close", "adj close", "volume"}
                    ),
                    str(column[0]),
                )
                for column in result.columns
            ]
        return result

    def _validate_ohlcv(
        self,
        frame: pd.DataFrame,
        ticker: str,
        *,
        allow_empty: bool = False,
    ) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Return a normalized UTC-indexed OHLCV frame or raise."""

        symbol = self._normalise_ticker(ticker)
        if not isinstance(frame, pd.DataFrame):
            raise DataValidationError("Market data must be a pandas DataFrame")
        if frame.empty:
            if allow_empty:
                return pd.DataFrame(columns=_STANDARD_COLUMNS)
            raise DataValidationError(f"{symbol}: market data is empty")

        cleaned = self._flatten_columns(frame, symbol)
        canonical = {
            str(column).strip().lower().replace("_", " "): column
            for column in cleaned.columns
        }
        rename: dict[Any, str] = {}
        for standard in _STANDARD_COLUMNS:
            source = canonical.get(standard.lower())
            if source is not None:
                rename[source] = standard
        cleaned = cleaned.rename(columns=rename)

        required = set(_REQUIRED_OHLC)
        if self.quality_cfg.require_volume:
            required.add("Volume")
        missing = sorted(required.difference(cleaned.columns))
        if missing:
            raise DataValidationError(
                f"{symbol}: missing required OHLCV columns: {', '.join(missing)}"
            )
        if "Volume" not in cleaned.columns:
            cleaned["Volume"] = np.nan
        cleaned = cleaned.loc[:, list(_STANDARD_COLUMNS)].copy()

        input_rows = len(cleaned)
        index = pd.to_datetime(cleaned.index, errors="coerce", utc=True)
        valid_index = ~index.isna()
        cleaned = cleaned.loc[valid_index].copy()
        cleaned.index = index[valid_index]
        cleaned.index.name = "Date"

        for column in _STANDARD_COLUMNS:
            cleaned[column] = pd.to_numeric(cleaned[column], errors="coerce")

        duplicate_rows = int(cleaned.index.duplicated(keep="last").sum())
        cleaned = cleaned.loc[~cleaned.index.duplicated(keep="last")]
        cleaned = cleaned.sort_index()

        prices = cleaned.loc[:, list(_REQUIRED_OHLC)]
        finite_prices = np.isfinite(prices).all(axis=1)
        structurally_valid = (
            finite_prices
            & (cleaned["Open"] > 0)
            & (cleaned["High"] > 0)
            & (cleaned["Low"] > 0)
            & (cleaned["Close"] > 0)
            & (cleaned["High"] >= cleaned["Low"])
            & (cleaned["High"] >= cleaned["Open"])
            & (cleaned["High"] >= cleaned["Close"])
            & (cleaned["Low"] <= cleaned["Open"])
            & (cleaned["Low"] <= cleaned["Close"])
        )
        if self.quality_cfg.require_volume:
            structurally_valid &= (
                np.isfinite(cleaned["Volume"]) & (cleaned["Volume"] >= 0)
            )
        else:
            invalid_volume = cleaned["Volume"].notna() & (
                ~np.isfinite(cleaned["Volume"]) | (cleaned["Volume"] < 0)
            )
            cleaned.loc[invalid_volume, "Volume"] = np.nan

        latest_allowed = pd.Timestamp(
            self._utc_now().date()
            + timedelta(days=self.quality_cfg.reject_future_days),
            tz=UTC,
        )
        structurally_valid &= cleaned.index.normalize() <= latest_allowed
        cleaned = cleaned.loc[structurally_valid].copy()

        dropped_rows = input_rows - len(cleaned)
        report = DataQualityReport(
            ticker=symbol,
            input_rows=input_rows,
            output_rows=len(cleaned),
            dropped_rows=dropped_rows,
            duplicate_rows=duplicate_rows,
            first_date=cleaned.index[0].isoformat() if not cleaned.empty else None,
            last_date=cleaned.index[-1].isoformat() if not cleaned.empty else None,
        )
        with self._diagnostics_lock:
            self._quality_reports[symbol] = report

        if cleaned.empty:
            raise DataValidationError(
                f"{symbol}: no rows remain after OHLCV validation"
            )
        if report.dropped_ratio > self.quality_cfg.max_invalid_row_ratio:
            raise DataValidationError(
                f"{symbol}: rejected {dropped_rows}/{input_rows} rows "
                f"({report.dropped_ratio:.1%}), exceeding the configured "
                f"{self.quality_cfg.max_invalid_row_ratio:.1%} limit"
            )
        if dropped_rows:
            LOGGER.warning(
                "%s: removed %d/%d invalid or duplicate OHLCV rows",
                symbol,
                dropped_rows,
                input_rows,
            )
        return cleaned

    def _load_from_db_cache(self, ticker: str, days: int) -> pd.DataFrame | None:
        cutoff = self._utc_now() - timedelta(days=days)
        try:
            frame = self.db.get_cached_prices(ticker, start=cutoff)
        except PersistenceError as exc:
            LOGGER.warning("%s: persistent-cache read failed: %s", ticker, exc)
            return None
        if frame is None or frame.empty:
            return None
        try:
            validated = self._validate_ohlcv(frame, ticker)
        except DataValidationError as exc:
            LOGGER.warning("%s: persistent cache is invalid: %s", ticker, exc)
            return None

        staleness = self._business_days_since(validated.index[-1])
        coverage = self._coverage_ratio(validated, days)
        fresh = (
            staleness <= self.cache_cfg.max_staleness_bdays
            and coverage >= self.cache_cfg.min_coverage_ratio
        )
        validated.attrs.update(
            {
                "source": (
                    DataSource.CACHE.value
                    if fresh
                    else DataSource.CACHE_STALE.value
                ),
                "ticker": ticker,
                "stale": not fresh,
                "staleness_business_days": staleness,
                "coverage_ratio": coverage,
                "is_synthetic": False,
            }
        )
        return validated

    def _load_yfinance(self) -> Any:
        try:
            return dependencies.load("yfinance")
        except ImportError as exc:
            raise DataUnavailableError(
                "yfinance is not installed or could not be imported"
            ) from exc

    @staticmethod
    def _is_transient_download_error(exc: BaseException) -> bool:
        return isinstance(exc, DownloadError)

    def _download_once(
        self,
        ticker: str,
        start: datetime,
        end: datetime,
    ) -> pd.DataFrame:
        self._rate_limiter.wait()
        try:
            if self._downloader is not None:
                frame = self._downloader(ticker=ticker, start=start, end=end)
            else:
                yf = self._load_yfinance()
                frame = yf.download(
                    ticker,
                    start=start,
                    end=end,
                    interval="1d",
                    progress=False,
                    threads=False,
                    auto_adjust=self.download_cfg.auto_adjust,
                    repair=self.download_cfg.repair_prices,
                    timeout=self.download_cfg.timeout_seconds,
                    multi_level_index=False,
                )
        except DataUnavailableError:
            raise
        except Exception as exc:
            raise DownloadError(
                f"{ticker}: remote request failed: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
            ) from exc
        if frame is None or frame.empty:
            raise DownloadError(f"{ticker}: remote source returned no rows")
        return frame

    def _download_with_retry(
        self,
        ticker: str,
        start: datetime,
        end: datetime,
    ) -> pd.DataFrame:
        retried = retry_with_backoff(
            max_attempts=self.download_cfg.max_attempts,
            base_delay=self.download_cfg.base_delay_seconds,
            max_delay=self.download_cfg.max_delay_seconds,
            retry_if=self._is_transient_download_error,
        )(self._download_once)
        raw = retried(ticker, start, end)
        # Validation errors are deterministic and are intentionally not retried.
        return self._validate_ohlcv(raw, ticker)

    def _decorate(
        self,
        frame: pd.DataFrame,
        *,
        ticker: str,
        source: DataSource,
        days: int,
        stale: bool,
        synthetic: bool = False,
    ) -> pd.DataFrame:
        result = self._copy_frame(frame)
        result.attrs.update(
            {
                "source": source.value,
                "ticker": ticker,
                "requested_days": days,
                "stale": stale,
                "is_synthetic": synthetic,
                "prepared_at": self._utc_now().isoformat(),
            }
        )
        return result

    def _synthetic_data(self, ticker: str, days: int) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Generate deterministic test data without mutating NumPy global state."""

        digest = hashlib.sha256(ticker.encode("utf-8")).digest()
        seed = int.from_bytes(digest[:8], "big", signed=False)
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        frequency = "D" if self.cache_cfg.trading_days_per_week >= 6.5 else "B"
        dates = pd.date_range(
            end=pd.Timestamp(self._utc_now().date(), tz=UTC),
            periods=max(2, days),
            freq=frequency,
            name="Date",
        )
        close_to_close = rng.normal(0.0003, 0.015, len(dates))
        close = 100.0 * np.exp(np.cumsum(close_to_close))
        open_price = np.r_[close[0], close[:-1]] * (
            1.0 + rng.normal(0.0, 0.0025, len(dates))
        )
        intraday_range = rng.uniform(0.004, 0.025, len(dates))
        high = np.maximum(open_price, close) * (1.0 + intraday_range / 2.0)
        low = np.minimum(open_price, close) * (1.0 - intraday_range / 2.0)
        frame = pd.DataFrame(
            {
                "Open": open_price,
                "High": high,
                "Low": low,
                "Close": close,
                "Volume": rng.integers(
                    1_000_000, 50_000_001, size=len(dates), endpoint=False
                ),
            },
            index=dates,
        )
        return self._decorate(
            frame,
            ticker=ticker,
            source=DataSource.SYNTHETIC,
            days=days,
            stale=False,
            synthetic=True,
        )

    def _fallback(
        self,
        *,
        ticker: str,
        days: int,
        cached: pd.DataFrame | None,
        error: BaseException,
        allow_synthetic: bool,
    ) -> pd.DataFrame:
        if (
            cached is not None
            and not cached.empty
            and self.download_cfg.allow_stale_cache
        ):
            LOGGER.warning(
                "%s: live data unavailable (%s); using stale persistent cache",
                ticker,
                error,
            )
            return self._decorate(
                cached,
                ticker=ticker,
                source=DataSource.CACHE_STALE,
                days=days,
                stale=True,
            )
        if allow_synthetic:
            LOGGER.warning(
                "%s: live and cached data unavailable; returning explicitly "
                "enabled synthetic data",
                ticker,
            )
            return self._synthetic_data(ticker, days)
        raise DataUnavailableError(
            f"{ticker}: no usable live or cached market data: {error}"
        ) from error

    def fetch_ticker(
        self,
        ticker: str,
        days: int = 730,
        force_refresh: bool = False,
        *,
        allow_synthetic: bool | None = None,
    ) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Fetch one ticker and return a defensive DataFrame copy.

        ``DataFrame.attrs['source']`` is one of ``cache``, ``cache_stale``,
        ``live`` or ``synthetic``. Synthetic fallback must be enabled either in
        ``DownloadConfig`` or for this individual call.
        """

        symbol = self._normalise_ticker(ticker)
        requested_days = self._normalise_days(days)
        key = (symbol, requested_days)
        synthetic_allowed = (
            self.download_cfg.allow_synthetic_fallback
            if allow_synthetic is None
            else bool(allow_synthetic)
        )

        if not force_refresh:
            memory = self._memory_get(key, allow_synthetic=synthetic_allowed)
            if memory is not None:
                return memory

        with self._lock_for(key):
            if not force_refresh:
                memory = self._memory_get(key, allow_synthetic=synthetic_allowed)
                if memory is not None:
                    return memory

            cached = self._load_from_db_cache(symbol, requested_days)
            if (
                not force_refresh
                and cached is not None
                and cached.attrs.get("source") == DataSource.CACHE.value
            ):
                result = self._decorate(
                    cached,
                    ticker=symbol,
                    source=DataSource.CACHE,
                    days=requested_days,
                    stale=False,
                )
                self._memory_put(key, result)
                return self._copy_frame(result)

            now = self._utc_now()
            cache_has_coverage = (
                cached is not None
                and not cached.empty
                and float(cached.attrs.get("coverage_ratio", 0.0))
                >= self.cache_cfg.min_coverage_ratio
            )
            start = (
                cached.index[-1].to_pydatetime() - timedelta(days=1)
                if cache_has_coverage and not force_refresh
                else now - timedelta(days=requested_days)
            )
            # yfinance treats end as exclusive; tomorrow includes today's
            # completed bar when the provider has published it.
            end = datetime.combine(
                now.date() + timedelta(days=1),
                datetime.min.time(),
                tzinfo=UTC,
            )
            try:
                fresh = self._download_with_retry(symbol, start, end)
                if cached is not None and not cached.empty:
                    merged = pd.concat([cached, fresh], axis=0)
                    merged = merged.loc[~merged.index.duplicated(keep="last")]
                    merged = merged.sort_index()
                else:
                    merged = fresh
                cutoff = pd.Timestamp(now - timedelta(days=requested_days))
                merged = merged.loc[merged.index >= cutoff]
                merged = self._validate_ohlcv(merged, symbol)
                result = self._decorate(
                    merged,
                    ticker=symbol,
                    source=DataSource.LIVE,
                    days=requested_days,
                    stale=False,
                )
                try:
                    self.db.cache_prices(symbol, fresh)
                except (PersistenceError, ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
                    LOGGER.warning(
                        "%s: live data returned but persistent-cache write failed: %s",
                        symbol,
                        exc,
                    )
            except (DataUnavailableError, DownloadError, DataValidationError) as exc:
                result = self._fallback(
                    ticker=symbol,
                    days=requested_days,
                    cached=cached,
                    error=exc,
                    allow_synthetic=synthetic_allowed,
                )

            self._memory_put(key, result)
            with self._diagnostics_lock:
                self._last_errors.pop(symbol, None)
            return self._copy_frame(result)

    def fetch_multiple(
        self,
        tickers: list[str] | tuple[str, ...],
        days: int = 730,
        max_workers: int = 8,
        *,
        force_refresh: bool = False,
        allow_synthetic: bool | None = None,
        strict: bool = False,
    ) -> dict[str, pd.DataFrame]:
        """Fetch unique tickers concurrently.

        In non-strict mode failures are recorded in ``last_errors``. In strict
        mode the first completed failure is re-raised after worker cleanup.
        """

        requested_days = self._normalise_days(days)
        workers = _positive_int("max_workers", max_workers)
        symbols = list(dict.fromkeys(self._normalise_ticker(t) for t in tickers))
        if not symbols:
            return {}
        workers = min(workers, len(symbols))
        results: dict[str, pd.DataFrame] = {}
        errors: dict[str, BaseException] = {}

        with ThreadPoolExecutor(
            max_workers=workers,
            thread_name_prefix="market-data",
        ) as pool:
            futures = {
                pool.submit(
                    self.fetch_ticker,
                    symbol,
                    requested_days,
                    force_refresh,
                    allow_synthetic=allow_synthetic,
                ): symbol
                for symbol in symbols
            }
            for future in as_completed(futures):
                symbol = futures[future]
                try:
                    results[symbol] = future.result()
                except Exception as exc:
                    errors[symbol] = exc
                    LOGGER.error("%s: fetch failed: %s", symbol, exc)

        with self._diagnostics_lock:
            for symbol in symbols:
                self._last_errors.pop(symbol, None)
            self._last_errors.update(
                {
                    symbol: f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
                    for symbol, exc in errors.items()
                }
            )
        if strict and errors:
            symbol = next(iter(errors))
            raise DataUnavailableError(
                f"Market-data batch failed for {symbol}: {errors[symbol]}"
            ) from errors[symbol]
        return {symbol: results[symbol] for symbol in symbols if symbol in results}

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Feature engineering
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @staticmethod
    def _feature_input(frame: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
        if not isinstance(frame, pd.DataFrame) or frame.empty:
            raise DataValidationError("Feature input must be a non-empty DataFrame")
        if "Close" not in frame.columns:
            raise DataValidationError("Feature input is missing Close")
        prepared = frame.copy()
        prepared.index = pd.to_datetime(prepared.index, errors="coerce", utc=True)
        prepared = prepared.loc[~prepared.index.isna()]
        prepared = prepared.loc[~prepared.index.duplicated(keep="last")].sort_index()
        for column in _STANDARD_COLUMNS:
            if column in prepared.columns:
                prepared[column] = pd.to_numeric(prepared[column], errors="coerce")
        if not np.isfinite(prepared["Close"].dropna()).all():
            raise DataValidationError("Feature input contains non-finite Close values")
        if (prepared["Close"].dropna() <= 0).any():
            raise DataValidationError("Feature input contains non-positive Close values")
        if prepared["Close"].notna().sum() < 2:
            raise DataValidationError("Feature input needs at least two valid closes")
        return prepared

    def compute_features(
        self,
        frame: pd.DataFrame,
        cfg: FeatureConfig | None = None,
        *,
        drop_incomplete: bool = True,
    ) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Compute leakage-safe trailing technical features.

        All values at time *t* use information available no later than *t*.
        The caller must shift features separately when the prediction target
        requires features known strictly before the close at *t*.
        """

        config = cfg or self.feat_cfg
        data = self._feature_input(frame)
        close = data["Close"]
        features = pd.DataFrame(index=data.index)

        for window in config.return_windows:
            features[f"returns_{window}d"] = close.pct_change(
                window, fill_method=None
            )

        for window in config.sma_windows:
            average = close.rolling(window, min_periods=window).mean()
            features[f"sma_{window}"] = average
            features[f"price_vs_sma_{window}"] = close.div(average).sub(1.0)

        daily_return = close.pct_change(fill_method=None)
        for window in config.vol_windows:
            features[f"vol_{window}d"] = (
                daily_return.rolling(window, min_periods=window).std(ddof=1)
                * math.sqrt(config.annualization_factor)
            )
        if len(config.vol_windows) >= 2:
            short_window, long_window = config.vol_windows[:2]
            denominator = features[f"vol_{long_window}d"].replace(0.0, np.nan)
            features["vol_ratio"] = features[f"vol_{short_window}d"].div(
                denominator
            )

        # Wilder RSI.
        delta = close.diff()
        gain = delta.clip(lower=0.0)
        loss = -delta.clip(upper=0.0)
        average_gain = gain.ewm(
            alpha=1.0 / config.rsi_window,
            adjust=False,
            min_periods=config.rsi_window,
        ).mean()
        average_loss = loss.ewm(
            alpha=1.0 / config.rsi_window,
            adjust=False,
            min_periods=config.rsi_window,
        ).mean()
        relative_strength = average_gain.div(average_loss.replace(0.0, np.nan))
        rsi = 100.0 - 100.0 / (1.0 + relative_strength)
        rsi = rsi.mask((average_loss == 0) & (average_gain > 0), 100.0)
        rsi = rsi.mask((average_loss == 0) & (average_gain == 0), 50.0)
        features[f"rsi_{config.rsi_window}"] = rsi

        ema_fast = close.ewm(
            span=config.macd_fast,
            adjust=False,
            min_periods=config.macd_fast,
        ).mean()
        ema_slow = close.ewm(
            span=config.macd_slow,
            adjust=False,
            min_periods=config.macd_slow,
        ).mean()
        features["macd"] = ema_fast - ema_slow
        features["macd_signal"] = features["macd"].ewm(
            span=config.macd_signal,
            adjust=False,
            min_periods=config.macd_signal,
        ).mean()
        features["macd_hist"] = features["macd"] - features["macd_signal"]

        middle_band = close.rolling(
            config.bb_window, min_periods=config.bb_window
        ).mean()
        band_std = close.rolling(
            config.bb_window, min_periods=config.bb_window
        ).std(ddof=0)
        upper_band = middle_band + config.bb_std * band_std
        lower_band = middle_band - config.bb_std * band_std
        features["bb_width"] = (upper_band - lower_band).div(
            middle_band.replace(0.0, np.nan)
        )
        features["bb_position"] = (close - lower_band).div(
            (upper_band - lower_band).replace(0.0, np.nan)
        )

        if {"High", "Low"}.issubset(data.columns):
            previous_close = close.shift(1)
            true_range = pd.concat(
                (
                    data["High"] - data["Low"],
                    (data["High"] - previous_close).abs(),
                    (data["Low"] - previous_close).abs(),
                ),
                axis=1,
            ).max(axis=1)
            atr = true_range.ewm(
                alpha=1.0 / config.atr_window,
                adjust=False,
                min_periods=config.atr_window,
            ).mean()
            features[f"atr_{config.atr_window}"] = atr
            features[f"atr_pct_{config.atr_window}"] = atr.div(close)

        if "Volume" in data.columns:
            average_volume = data["Volume"].rolling(
                config.volume_window,
                min_periods=config.volume_window,
            ).mean()
            features["volume_ratio"] = data["Volume"].div(
                average_volume.replace(0.0, np.nan)
            )

        features = features.replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan)
        if drop_incomplete:
            features = features.dropna(how="any")
        features.attrs = {
            "source": frame.attrs.get("source", "unknown"),
            "ticker": frame.attrs.get("ticker"),
            "feature_schema_version": 2,
            "drop_incomplete": drop_incomplete,
        }
        return features

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Overview
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @staticmethod
    def _finite_or_none(value: Any) -> float | None:
        try:
            number = float(value)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            return None
        return number if math.isfinite(number) else None

    def get_market_overview(
        self,
        tickers: list[str] | tuple[str, ...],
        days: int = 252,
        *,
        risk_free_rate: float = 0.0,
        include_synthetic: bool = False,
        max_workers: int = 8,
    ) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
        """Return finite, JSON-safe summary metrics for successfully fetched data."""

        annual_rate = float(risk_free_rate)
        if not math.isfinite(annual_rate) or annual_rate <= -1.0:
            raise ValueError("risk_free_rate must be finite and greater than -1")
        data = self.fetch_multiple(
            tickers,
            days,
            max_workers,
            allow_synthetic=include_synthetic,
        )
        overview: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
        daily_risk_free = (1.0 + annual_rate) ** (
            1.0 / self.feat_cfg.annualization_factor
        ) - 1.0

        for ticker, frame in data.items():
            synthetic = bool(frame.attrs.get("is_synthetic", False))
            if synthetic and not include_synthetic:
                LOGGER.warning("%s: excluded synthetic data from overview", ticker)
                continue
            close = pd.to_numeric(frame["Close"], errors="coerce").dropna()
            if len(close) < 20:
                LOGGER.warning("%s: insufficient history for overview", ticker)
                continue
            returns = close.pct_change(fill_method=None).dropna()
            if returns.empty:
                continue
            one_month_steps = min(21, len(close) - 1)
            one_year_steps = min(252, len(close) - 1)
            recent_volatility = returns.tail(20).std(ddof=1) * math.sqrt(
                self.feat_cfg.annualization_factor
            )
            annualized_excess = (
                returns.tail(one_year_steps).mean() - daily_risk_free
            ) * self.feat_cfg.annualization_factor
            sharpe = (
                annualized_excess / recent_volatility
                if math.isfinite(recent_volatility) and recent_volatility > 0
                else None
            )
            overview[ticker] = {
                "price": self._finite_or_none(close.iloc[-1]),
                "return_1y": self._finite_or_none(
                    close.iloc[-1] / close.iloc[-1 - one_year_steps] - 1.0
                ),
                "return_1m": self._finite_or_none(
                    close.iloc[-1] / close.iloc[-1 - one_month_steps] - 1.0
                ),
                "volatility_20d_annualized": self._finite_or_none(
                    recent_volatility
                ),
                "sharpe_approx": self._finite_or_none(sharpe),
                "source": frame.attrs.get("source", "unknown"),
                "is_stale": bool(frame.attrs.get("stale", False)),
                "is_synthetic": synthetic,
                "last_date": close.index[-1].isoformat(),
                "observations": int(len(close)),
            }
        return overview

    "CacheConfig",

    "MarketDataError",
    "QualityConfig",

    "retry_with_backoff",
]




#MODUL 3









import importlib
import logging
import math
import threading
from collections import Counter, deque
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import date, datetime, timezone
from types import MappingProxyType
from typing import Any, Final, Mapping, Sequence

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

# This distribution is a single-file build. Core names are defined above.


UTC: Final = timezone.utc
LOGGER = logging.getLogger(f"{APP_NAME}.regime")
REGIME_NAMES: Final = (
    "bull",
    "bear",
    "sideways",
    "high_volatility",
    "crisis",
)
SAFETY_REGIMES: Final = frozenset({"high_volatility", "crisis"})
_EPSILON: Final = 1e-12


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Exceptions and result diagnostics
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


class RegimeDetectionError(SmartInvestError):
    """Base exception for Module 3."""


class RegimeDataError(RegimeDetectionError, ValueError):
    """Raised when the supplied price history is unusable."""


class RegimeModelError(RegimeDetectionError):
    """Raised when an explicitly required HMM cannot be used."""


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class StateProfile:
    state: int
    label: str
    observations: int
    mean_daily_return: float
    annualized_volatility: float
    mean_drawdown: float


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ModelFitReport:
    fitted: bool
    reason: str
    observations: int
    states: int
    converged: bool
    best_log_likelihood: float | None
    best_log_likelihood_per_observation: float | None
    attempts: int
    trained_through: str | None
    state_profiles: tuple[StateProfile, ...] = ()


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class RegimeDiagnostics:
    as_of: str
    raw_regime: str
    confirmed_regime: str
    method: str
    raw_confidence: float
    confirmed_confidence: float
    hmm_state: int | None = None
    hmm_posterior: float | None = None
    state_probabilities: Mapping[str, float] = field(
        default_factory=lambda: MappingProxyType({})
    )
    history_size: int = 0
    stale_input: bool = False
    synthetic_input: bool = False


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class _Signal:
    as_of: pd.Timestamp
    regime: str
    confidence: float


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class _Metrics:
    as_of: pd.Timestamp
    return_20d: float
    return_60d: float
    volatility_20d: float
    volatility_60d: float
    current_drawdown: float
    max_drawdown_252d: float
    price_vs_sma_200d: float
    volatility_ratio: float


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Validated configuration
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _regime_finite(name: str, value: float) -> float:
    number = float(value)
    if not math.isfinite(number):
        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be finite")
    return number


def _regime_positive(name: str, value: float) -> float:
    number = _regime_finite(name, value)
    if number <= 0:
        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be greater than zero")
    return number


def _regime_positive_integer(name: str, value: int) -> int:
    if isinstance(value, bool) or int(value) != value or value <= 0:
        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be a positive integer")
    return int(value)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class RegimeConfig:
    """Rule thresholds and regime-change policy."""

    annualization_factor: int = 252
    short_return_window: int = 20
    long_return_window: int = 60
    short_volatility_window: int = 20
    long_volatility_window: int = 60
    trend_window: int = 200
    drawdown_window: int = 252
    minimum_history: int = 80

    crisis_volatility: float = 0.40
    crisis_drawdown: float = -0.18
    crisis_short_return: float = -0.12
    high_volatility: float = 0.30
    volatility_spike_ratio: float = 1.50
    bull_short_return: float = 0.035
    bull_long_return: float = 0.075
    bear_short_return: float = -0.035
    bear_long_return: float = -0.075
    sideways_short_return: float = 0.020
    sideways_long_return: float = 0.045

    smoothing_window: int = 5
    switch_confirmations: int = 3
    switch_vote_share: float = 0.58
    stale_confidence_penalty: float = 0.10
    allow_synthetic_input: bool = False

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        for name in (
            "annualization_factor",
            "short_return_window",
            "long_return_window",
            "short_volatility_window",
            "long_volatility_window",
            "trend_window",
            "drawdown_window",
            "minimum_history",
            "smoothing_window",
            "switch_confirmations",
        ):
            _regime_positive_integer(name, getattr(self, name))
        if self.short_return_window >= self.long_return_window:
            raise ValueError(
                "short_return_window must be smaller than long_return_window"
            )
        if self.short_volatility_window >= self.long_volatility_window:
            raise ValueError(
                "short_volatility_window must be smaller than "
                "long_volatility_window"
            )
        if self.minimum_history <= self.long_return_window:
            raise ValueError(
                "minimum_history must exceed the longest return window"
            )
        if self.switch_confirmations > self.smoothing_window:
            raise ValueError(
                "switch_confirmations cannot exceed smoothing_window"
            )
        if not 0.5 <= self.switch_vote_share <= 1.0:
            raise ValueError("switch_vote_share must be between 0.5 and 1")
        if not 0.0 <= self.stale_confidence_penalty < 1.0:
            raise ValueError(
                "stale_confidence_penalty must be between 0 and 1"
            )
        for name in (
            "crisis_volatility",
            "high_volatility",
            "volatility_spike_ratio",
            "bull_short_return",
            "bull_long_return",
            "sideways_short_return",
            "sideways_long_return",
        ):
            _regime_positive(name, getattr(self, name))
        for name in (
            "crisis_drawdown",
            "crisis_short_return",
            "bear_short_return",
            "bear_long_return",
        ):
        if _regime_finite(name, getattr(self, name)) >= 0:
                raise ValueError(f"{name} must be negative")
        if self.crisis_volatility <= self.high_volatility:
            raise ValueError(
                "crisis_volatility must exceed high_volatility"
            )


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class HMMConfig:
    """Optional Gaussian-HMM training policy."""

    enabled: bool = True
    required: bool = False
    n_components: int = 5
    n_iter: int = 300
    tolerance: float = 1e-4
    covariance_floor: float = 1e-4
    restarts: int = 3
    random_seed: int = 42
    minimum_observations: int = 252
    maximum_observations: int = 1_500
    posterior_weight: float = 0.70
    rule_weight: float = 0.30
    refit_after_new_rows: int = 20

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not 3 <= self.n_components <= 5:
            raise ValueError("n_components must be between 3 and 5")
        for name in (
            "n_iter",
            "restarts",
            "minimum_observations",
            "maximum_observations",
            "refit_after_new_rows",
        ):
            _regime_positive_integer(name, getattr(self, name))
        _regime_positive("tolerance", self.tolerance)
        _regime_positive("covariance_floor", self.covariance_floor)
        if self.maximum_observations < self.minimum_observations:
            raise ValueError(
                "maximum_observations cannot be below minimum_observations"
            )
        if self.minimum_observations < self.n_components * 20:
            raise ValueError(
                "minimum_observations must be at least 20 per HMM state"
            )
        if not 0.0 <= self.posterior_weight <= 1.0:
            raise ValueError("posterior_weight must be between 0 and 1")
        if not 0.0 <= self.rule_weight <= 1.0:
            raise ValueError("rule_weight must be between 0 and 1")
        if not math.isclose(
            self.posterior_weight + self.rule_weight,
            1.0,
            abs_tol=1e-9,
        ):
            raise ValueError("posterior_weight and rule_weight must sum to 1")


_DEFAULT_ADJUSTMENTS: Final = MappingProxyType(
    {
        "bull": MappingProxyType(
            {"equities": 0.10, "bonds": -0.05, "gold": -0.03, "cash": -0.02}
        ),
        "bear": MappingProxyType(
            {"equities": -0.15, "bonds": 0.08, "gold": 0.05, "cash": 0.02}
        ),
        "sideways": MappingProxyType(
            {"equities": 0.00, "bonds": 0.02, "gold": 0.01, "cash": -0.03}
        ),
        "high_volatility": MappingProxyType(
            {"equities": -0.10, "bonds": 0.04, "gold": 0.06, "cash": 0.00}
        ),
        "crisis": MappingProxyType(
            {"equities": -0.20, "bonds": 0.10, "gold": 0.08, "cash": 0.02}
        ),
    }
)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Detector
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


class MarketRegimeDetector:
    """Hybrid explainable/HMM detector with date-aware regime hysteresis."""

    REGIMES = list(REGIME_NAMES)

    def __init__(
        self,
        n_components: int | None = None,
        *,
        regime_config: RegimeConfig | None = None,
        hmm_config: HMMConfig | None = None,
        adjustments: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, float]] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.regime_config = regime_config or RegimeConfig()
        base_hmm = hmm_config or HMMConfig()
        if n_components is not None:
            base_hmm = HMMConfig(
                **{
                    field_name: getattr(base_hmm, field_name)
                    for field_name in HMMConfig.__dataclass_fields__
                    if field_name != "n_components"
                },
                n_components=n_components,
            )
        self.hmm_config = base_hmm
        self.n_components = base_hmm.n_components
        self._adjustments = self._validate_adjustments(
            adjustments or _DEFAULT_ADJUSTMENTS
        )

        self.model: Any | None = None
        self._trained = False
        self._center: np.ndarray | None = None
        self._scale: np.ndarray | None = None
        self._state_labels: dict[int, str] = {}
        self._state_profiles: tuple[StateProfile, ...] = ()
        self._trained_through: pd.Timestamp | None = None
        self._trained_rows = 0
        self._fit_report = ModelFitReport(
            fitted=False,
            reason="not fitted",
            observations=0,
            states=self.n_components,
            converged=False,
            best_log_likelihood=None,
            best_log_likelihood_per_observation=None,
            attempts=0,
            trained_through=None,
        )
        self._last_diagnostics: RegimeDiagnostics | None = None
        self._history: deque[_Signal] = deque(
            maxlen=self.regime_config.smoothing_window
        )
        self._confirmed_name: str | None = None
        self._lock = threading.RLock()

    @property
    def trained(self) -> bool:
        with self._lock:
            return self._trained

    @property
    def fit_report(self) -> ModelFitReport:
        with self._lock:
            return self._fit_report

    @property
    def last_diagnostics(self) -> RegimeDiagnostics | None:
        with self._lock:
            return self._last_diagnostics

    @property
    def state_profiles(self) -> tuple[StateProfile, ...]:
        with self._lock:
            return self._state_profiles

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_adjustments(
        values: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, float]],
    ) -> dict[str, dict[str, float]]:
        result: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
        missing = set(REGIME_NAMES).difference(values)
        if missing:
            raise ValueError(
                "adjustments missing regimes: " + ", ".join(sorted(missing))
            )
        for regime in REGIME_NAMES:
            row = {
                str(asset): _regime_finite(
                    f"adjustments[{regime!r}][{asset!r}]", change
                )
                for asset, change in values[regime].items()
            }
            if not row:
                raise ValueError(f"adjustments[{regime!r}] cannot be empty")
            if not math.isclose(sum(row.values()), 0.0, abs_tol=1e-8):
                raise ValueError(
                    f"adjustments[{regime!r}] must sum to zero"
                )
            result[regime] = row
        return result

    @staticmethod
    def _copy_attrs(source: pd.DataFrame, target: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
        target.attrs = dict(source.attrs)
        return target

    def _prepare_prices(
        self,
        frame: pd.DataFrame,
        *,
        as_of: datetime | date | str | pd.Timestamp | None = None,
        minimum_rows: int | None = None,
    ) -> pd.DataFrame:
        if not isinstance(frame, pd.DataFrame) or frame.empty:
            raise RegimeDataError("Price history must be a non-empty DataFrame")
        if frame.attrs.get("is_synthetic", False) and not (
            self.regime_config.allow_synthetic_input
        ):
            raise RegimeDataError(
                "Synthetic market data is disabled for regime detection"
            )
        if "Close" not in frame.columns:
            raise RegimeDataError("Price history is missing the Close column")

        data = frame.copy()
        index = pd.to_datetime(data.index, errors="coerce", utc=True)
        valid_index = ~index.isna()
        data = data.loc[valid_index].copy()
        data.index = index[valid_index]
        data.index.name = "Date"
        data = data.loc[~data.index.duplicated(keep="last")].sort_index()
        if as_of is not None:
            try:
                cutoff = pd.Timestamp(as_of)
            except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
                raise RegimeDataError(f"Invalid as_of value: {as_of!r}") from exc
            cutoff = (
                cutoff.tz_localize(UTC)
                if cutoff.tzinfo is None
                else cutoff.tz_convert(UTC)
            )
            data = data.loc[data.index <= cutoff]
        if data.empty:
            raise RegimeDataError("No price rows exist at or before as_of")

        data["Close"] = pd.to_numeric(data["Close"], errors="coerce")
        finite_positive = np.isfinite(data["Close"]) & (data["Close"] > 0)
        invalid_count = int((~finite_positive).sum())
        if invalid_count:
            LOGGER.warning(
                "Dropped %d rows with invalid Close values", invalid_count
            )
            data = data.loc[finite_positive].copy()
        needed = minimum_rows or self.regime_config.minimum_history
        if len(data) < needed:
            raise RegimeDataError(
                f"Regime detection needs at least {needed} valid rows; "
                f"received {len(data)}"
            )
        return self._copy_attrs(frame, data)

    @staticmethod
    def _drawdown_series(close: pd.Series) -> pd.Series:
        running_peak = close.cummax()
        return close.div(running_peak).sub(1.0)

    def _metrics(self, data: pd.DataFrame) -> _Metrics:
        cfg = self.regime_config
        close = data["Close"]
        returns = close.pct_change(fill_method=None)

        def period_return(window: int) -> float:
            if len(close) <= window:
                return float(close.iloc[-1] / close.iloc[0] - 1.0)
            return float(close.iloc[-1] / close.iloc[-1 - window] - 1.0)

        def annualized_volatility(window: int) -> float:
            sample = returns.tail(window).dropna()
            if len(sample) < 2:
                return 0.0
            value = float(
                sample.std(ddof=1) * math.sqrt(cfg.annualization_factor)
            )
            return value if math.isfinite(value) else 0.0

        drawdowns = self._drawdown_series(close)
        current_drawdown = float(drawdowns.iloc[-1])
        window_close = close.tail(cfg.drawdown_window)
        max_drawdown = float(self._drawdown_series(window_close).min())
        trend_sample = close.tail(cfg.trend_window)
        trend_average = float(trend_sample.mean())
        price_vs_trend = (
            float(close.iloc[-1] / trend_average - 1.0)
            if trend_average > 0
            else 0.0
        )
        short_vol = annualized_volatility(cfg.short_volatility_window)
        long_vol = annualized_volatility(cfg.long_volatility_window)
        vol_ratio = short_vol / max(long_vol, _EPSILON)

        return _Metrics(
            as_of=data.index[-1],
            return_20d=period_return(cfg.short_return_window),
            return_60d=period_return(cfg.long_return_window),
            volatility_20d=short_vol,
            volatility_60d=long_vol,
            current_drawdown=current_drawdown,
            max_drawdown_252d=max_drawdown,
            price_vs_sma_200d=price_vs_trend,
            volatility_ratio=vol_ratio,
        )

    def _build_observations(
        self,
        data: pd.DataFrame,
    ) -> tuple[np.ndarray, pd.DatetimeIndex]:
        cfg = self.regime_config
        close = data["Close"]
        log_return = np.log(close).diff()
        annualized_volatility = (
            log_return.rolling(
                cfg.short_volatility_window,
                min_periods=cfg.short_volatility_window,
            ).std(ddof=1)
            * math.sqrt(cfg.annualization_factor)
        )
        drawdown = self._drawdown_series(close)
        observations = pd.DataFrame(
            {
                "log_return": log_return,
                "annualized_volatility": annualized_volatility,
                "drawdown": drawdown,
            },
            index=data.index,
        ).replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan)
        observations = observations.dropna(how="any")
        return observations.to_numpy(dtype=float), observations.index

    @staticmethod
    def _robust_scale_fit(values: np.ndarray) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
        center = np.median(values, axis=0)
        q25, q75 = np.percentile(values, [25, 75], axis=0)
        scale = q75 - q25
        standard_deviation = np.std(values, axis=0, ddof=1)
        scale = np.where(scale > _EPSILON, scale, standard_deviation)
        scale = np.where(scale > _EPSILON, scale, 1.0)
        return center.astype(float), scale.astype(float)

    @staticmethod
    def _transform(
        values: np.ndarray,
        center: np.ndarray,
        scale: np.ndarray,
    ) -> np.ndarray:
        # Clipping protects EM from isolated corrupt/extreme observations while
        # retaining enough tail information for crisis-state identification.
        return np.clip((values - center) / scale, -12.0, 12.0)

    def _load_gaussian_hmm(self) -> type[Any]:
        try:
            dependencies.load("hmmlearn")
            hmm_module = importlib.import_module("hmmlearn.hmm")
            return hmm_module.GaussianHMM
        except (ImportError, AttributeError) as exc:
            raise RegimeModelError(
                "hmmlearn is unavailable; rule-based detection remains usable"
            ) from exc

    def _profile_states(
        self,
        raw_observations: np.ndarray,
        states: np.ndarray,
    ) -> tuple[dict[int, str], tuple[StateProfile, ...]]:
        summaries: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}
        for state in range(self.n_components):
            mask = states == state
            if not mask.any():
                raise RegimeModelError(
                    f"HMM state {state} has no assigned observations"
                )
            state_values = raw_observations[mask]
            summaries[state] = {
                "observations": float(mask.sum()),
                "return": float(np.mean(state_values[:, 0])),
                "volatility": float(np.mean(state_values[:, 1])),
                "drawdown": float(np.mean(state_values[:, 2])),
            }

        def zscore(key: str) -> dict[int, float]:
            array = np.array(
                [summaries[state][key] for state in summaries],
                dtype=float,
            )
            std = float(array.std(ddof=0))
            mean = float(array.mean())
            return {
                state: (
                    (summaries[state][key] - mean) / std
                    if std > _EPSILON
                    else 0.0
                )
                for state in summaries
            }

        return_z = zscore("return")
        volatility_z = zscore("volatility")
        drawdown_z = zscore("drawdown")
        remaining = set(summaries)
        labels: dict[int, str] = {}

        def assign(label: str, score: Mapping[int, float], *, highest: bool) -> None:
            if not remaining:
                return
            state = (
                max(remaining, key=lambda item: score[item])
                if highest
                else min(remaining, key=lambda item: score[item])
            )
            labels[state] = label
            remaining.remove(state)

        crisis_score = {
            state: volatility_z[state] - return_z[state] - drawdown_z[state]
            for state in summaries
        }
        assign("crisis", crisis_score, highest=True)
        assign("high_volatility", volatility_z, highest=True)
        assign("bull", return_z, highest=True)
        assign("bear", return_z, highest=False)
        for state in remaining:
            labels[state] = "sideways"

        # With fewer than five states, safety overrides still provide all five
        # output regimes even if the HMM cannot dedicate one state to each.
        profiles = tuple(
            StateProfile(
                state=state,
                label=labels[state],
                observations=int(summaries[state]["observations"]),
                mean_daily_return=summaries[state]["return"],
                annualized_volatility=summaries[state]["volatility"],
                mean_drawdown=summaries[state]["drawdown"],
            )
            for state in sorted(summaries)
        )
        return labels, profiles

    def fit(
        self,
        frame: pd.DataFrame,
        *,
        as_of: datetime | date | str | pd.Timestamp | None = None,
    ) -> bool:
        """Fit the optional HMM without using rows after ``as_of``.

        Returns ``False`` when HMM use is optional and fitting is unavailable.
        If ``HMMConfig.required`` is true, model failures are raised.
        """

        with self._lock:
            if not self.hmm_config.enabled:
                self._fit_report = ModelFitReport(
                    False,
                    "HMM disabled by configuration",
                    0,
                    self.n_components,
                    False,
                    None,
                    None,
                    0,
                    None,
                )
                return False
            try:
                data = self._prepare_prices(
                    frame,
                    as_of=as_of,
                    minimum_rows=max(
                        self.regime_config.minimum_history,
                        self.hmm_config.minimum_observations
                        + self.regime_config.short_volatility_window,
                    ),
                )
                raw, observation_dates = self._build_observations(data)
                if len(raw) < self.hmm_config.minimum_observations:
                    raise RegimeModelError(
                        "HMM needs at least "
                        f"{self.hmm_config.minimum_observations} observations; "
                        f"received {len(raw)}"
                    )
                if len(raw) > self.hmm_config.maximum_observations:
                    raw = raw[-self.hmm_config.maximum_observations :]
                    observation_dates = observation_dates[
                        -self.hmm_config.maximum_observations :
                    ]
                center, scale = self._robust_scale_fit(raw)
                transformed = self._transform(raw, center, scale)
                GaussianHMM = self._load_gaussian_hmm()

                best_model: Any | None = None
                best_score = -math.inf
                converged_candidates = 0
                for attempt in range(self.hmm_config.restarts):
                    candidate = GaussianHMM(
                        n_components=self.n_components,
                        covariance_type="diag",
                        n_iter=self.hmm_config.n_iter,
                        tol=self.hmm_config.tolerance,
                        min_covar=self.hmm_config.covariance_floor,
                        random_state=self.hmm_config.random_seed + attempt,
                    )
                    try:
                        candidate.fit(transformed)
                        converged = bool(
                            getattr(candidate, "monitor_", None)
                            and candidate.monitor_.converged
                        )
                        score = float(candidate.score(transformed))
                        if converged and math.isfinite(score):
                            converged_candidates += 1
                            if score > best_score:
                                best_model = candidate
                                best_score = score
                    except Exception as exc:
                        LOGGER.debug(
                            "HMM restart %d/%d failed: %s",
                            attempt + 1,
                            self.hmm_config.restarts,
                            exc,
                        )
                if best_model is None:
                    raise RegimeModelError(
                        "No converged finite HMM candidate was produced"
                    )

                states = np.asarray(best_model.predict(transformed), dtype=int)
                labels, profiles = self._profile_states(raw, states)
                self.model = best_model
                self._center = center
                self._scale = scale
                self._state_labels = labels
                self._state_profiles = profiles
                self._trained = True
                self._trained_through = observation_dates[-1]
                self._trained_rows = len(raw)
                self._fit_report = ModelFitReport(
                    fitted=True,
                    reason=f"{converged_candidates} converged candidate(s)",
                    observations=len(raw),
                    states=self.n_components,
                    converged=True,
                    best_log_likelihood=best_score,
                    best_log_likelihood_per_observation=best_score / len(raw),
                    attempts=self.hmm_config.restarts,
                    trained_through=observation_dates[-1].isoformat(),
                    state_profiles=profiles,
                )
                return True
            except Exception as exc:
                retained_previous = bool(
                    self._trained
                    and self.model is not None
                    and self._center is not None
                    and self._scale is not None
                )
                if not retained_previous:
                    self.model = None
                    self._trained = False
                    self._center = None
                    self._scale = None
                    self._state_labels = {}
                    self._state_profiles = ()
                    self._trained_through = None
                    self._trained_rows = 0
                self._fit_report = ModelFitReport(
                    fitted=retained_previous,
                    reason=(
                        f"refit failed; retained previous model: "
                        f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
                        if retained_previous
                        else f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
                    ),
                    observations=self._trained_rows if retained_previous else 0,
                    states=self.n_components,
                    converged=retained_previous,
                    best_log_likelihood=None,
                    best_log_likelihood_per_observation=None,
                    attempts=self.hmm_config.restarts,
                    trained_through=(
                        self._trained_through.isoformat()
                        if retained_previous and self._trained_through is not None
                        else None
                    ),
                    state_profiles=(
                        self._state_profiles if retained_previous else ()
                    ),
                )
                if self.hmm_config.required:
                    raise RegimeModelError(str(exc)) from exc
                LOGGER.warning(
                    "HMM fit unavailable; %s: %s",
                    (
                        "retaining previous model"
                        if retained_previous
                        else "using rules"
                    ),
                    exc,
                )
                return retained_previous

    def needs_refit(
        self,
        frame: pd.DataFrame,
        *,
        as_of: datetime | date | str | pd.Timestamp | None = None,
    ) -> bool:
        """Return whether enough new valid observations exist to refit the HMM."""

        with self._lock:
            if not self._trained or self._trained_through is None:
                return True
            try:
                data = self._prepare_prices(frame, as_of=as_of)
            except RegimeDataError:
                return False
            new_rows = int((data.index > self._trained_through).sum())
            return new_rows >= self.hmm_config.refit_after_new_rows

    def _rule_based(self, metrics: _Metrics) -> tuple[str, float]:
        cfg = self.regime_config

        crisis_from_drawdown = (
            metrics.volatility_20d >= cfg.crisis_volatility
            and metrics.current_drawdown <= cfg.crisis_drawdown
        )
        crisis_from_shock = (
            metrics.return_20d <= cfg.crisis_short_return
            and metrics.volatility_20d >= cfg.high_volatility
        )
        if crisis_from_drawdown or crisis_from_shock:
            severity = max(
                metrics.volatility_20d / cfg.crisis_volatility,
                abs(metrics.current_drawdown)
                / max(abs(cfg.crisis_drawdown), _EPSILON),
                abs(metrics.return_20d)
                / max(abs(cfg.crisis_short_return), _EPSILON),
            )
            return "crisis", min(0.98, 0.78 + 0.10 * min(severity - 1.0, 2.0))

        volatility_spike = (
            metrics.volatility_20d >= cfg.high_volatility
            or (
                metrics.volatility_ratio >= cfg.volatility_spike_ratio
                and metrics.volatility_20d >= cfg.high_volatility * 0.75
            )
        )
        if volatility_spike:
            severity = max(
                metrics.volatility_20d / cfg.high_volatility,
                metrics.volatility_ratio / cfg.volatility_spike_ratio,
            )
            return "high_volatility", min(
                0.95, 0.72 + 0.10 * min(severity - 1.0, 2.0)
            )

        # Continuous trend score reduces hard threshold discontinuities.
        short_scale = max(cfg.bull_short_return, _EPSILON)
        long_scale = max(cfg.bull_long_return, _EPSILON)
        trend_score = (
            0.40 * math.tanh(metrics.return_20d / short_scale)
            + 0.40 * math.tanh(metrics.return_60d / long_scale)
            + 0.20 * math.tanh(metrics.price_vs_sma_200d / 0.05)
        )

        clear_bull = (
            metrics.return_20d >= cfg.bull_short_return
            and metrics.return_60d >= cfg.bull_long_return
        )
        clear_bear = (
            metrics.return_20d <= cfg.bear_short_return
            and metrics.return_60d <= cfg.bear_long_return
        )
        sideways = (
            abs(metrics.return_20d) <= cfg.sideways_short_return
            and abs(metrics.return_60d) <= cfg.sideways_long_return
            and abs(metrics.price_vs_sma_200d) <= 0.04
        )

        if clear_bull:
            return "bull", min(0.93, 0.56 + 0.34 * abs(trend_score))
        if clear_bear:
            return "bear", min(0.93, 0.56 + 0.34 * abs(trend_score))
        if sideways:
            distance = max(
                abs(metrics.return_20d)
                / max(cfg.sideways_short_return, _EPSILON),
                abs(metrics.return_60d)
                / max(cfg.sideways_long_return, _EPSILON),
            )
