            return "sideways", max(0.55, 0.78 - 0.16 * min(distance, 1.0))
        if trend_score >= 0.35:
            return "bull", min(0.90, 0.54 + 0.30 * abs(trend_score))
        if trend_score <= -0.35:
            return "bear", min(0.90, 0.54 + 0.30 * abs(trend_score))
        # Weak ambiguous trends remain sideways rather than defaulting to bull.
        return "sideways", max(0.50, 0.62 - 0.15 * abs(trend_score))

    def _hmm_signal(
        self,
        data: pd.DataFrame,
        metrics: _Metrics,
        rule_name: str,
        rule_confidence: float,
    ) -> tuple[str, float, int, float, Mapping[str, float]] | None:
        if (
            not self._trained
            or self.model is None
            or self._center is None
            or self._scale is None
        ):
            return None
        if (
            self._trained_through is not None
            and self._trained_through > metrics.as_of
        ):
            LOGGER.debug(
                "Skipping HMM inference at %s because the model was trained "
                "through %s",
                metrics.as_of,
                self._trained_through,
            )
            return None
        raw, _ = self._build_observations(data)
        if not len(raw):
            return None
        transformed = self._transform(raw, self._center, self._scale)
        try:
            posterior = np.asarray(
                self.model.predict_proba(transformed)[-1],
                dtype=float,
            )
            state = int(np.argmax(posterior))
            hmm_name = self._state_labels.get(state, "sideways")
            state_probability = float(posterior[state])
        except Exception as exc:
            LOGGER.warning("HMM inference failed; using rules: %s", exc)
            return None

        # Absolute safety thresholds always override relative HMM labels.
        if rule_name in SAFETY_REGIMES:
            final_name = rule_name
            confidence = max(rule_confidence, state_probability * 0.90)
        elif hmm_name == rule_name:
            final_name = rule_name
            confidence = (
                self.hmm_config.posterior_weight * state_probability
                + self.hmm_config.rule_weight * rule_confidence
            )
            confidence = min(0.97, confidence + 0.05)
        else:
            # In disagreement, require a reasonably decisive posterior;
            # otherwise the explainable rule engine wins at lower confidence.
            if hmm_name in SAFETY_REGIMES:
                # Relative HMM state rankings alone must never declare an
                # absolute safety regime.
                final_name = rule_name
            elif state_probability >= 0.72:
                final_name = hmm_name
            else:
                final_name = rule_name
            confidence = (
                self.hmm_config.posterior_weight * state_probability
                + self.hmm_config.rule_weight * rule_confidence
            ) * 0.82

        label_probabilities: dict[str, float] = {
            name: 0.0 for name in REGIME_NAMES
        }
        for model_state, probability in enumerate(posterior):
            label = self._state_labels.get(model_state, "sideways")
            label_probabilities[label] += float(probability)
        return (
            final_name,
            min(0.99, max(0.05, confidence)),
            state,
            state_probability,
            MappingProxyType(label_probabilities),
        )

    def _build_regime(
        self,
        name: str,
        confidence: float,
        metrics: _Metrics,
        *,
        raw_name: str,
        smoothed: bool,
        hmm_used: bool,
        stale_input: bool,
    ) -> MarketRegime:
        adjusted_confidence = confidence
        if stale_input:
            adjusted_confidence -= self.regime_config.stale_confidence_penalty
        adjusted_confidence = min(0.99, max(0.01, adjusted_confidence))
        return MarketRegime(
            name=name,
            confidence=adjusted_confidence,
            characteristics={
                "return_20d": round(metrics.return_20d, 6),
                "return_60d": round(metrics.return_60d, 6),
                "volatility_20d": round(metrics.volatility_20d, 6),
                "volatility_60d": round(metrics.volatility_60d, 6),
                "current_drawdown": round(metrics.current_drawdown, 6),
                "max_drawdown_252d": round(metrics.max_drawdown_252d, 6),
                "price_vs_sma_200d": round(metrics.price_vs_sma_200d, 6),
                "volatility_ratio": round(metrics.volatility_ratio, 6),
                "raw_confidence": round(confidence, 6),
                "smoothed": float(smoothed),
                "hmm_used": float(hmm_used),
                "raw_regime_agrees": float(name == raw_name),
                # Compatibility alias: now correctly means current drawdown.
                "drawdown": round(metrics.current_drawdown, 6),
            },
            recommended_adjustments=dict(self._adjustments[name]),
        )

    def _smooth(
        self,
        raw_name: str,
        raw_confidence: float,
        metrics: _Metrics,
    ) -> tuple[str, float, bool]:
        signal = _Signal(metrics.as_of.normalize(), raw_name, raw_confidence)

        # Repeated detection for the same market date replaces the observation
        # instead of creating artificial confirmation votes.
        if self._history and self._history[-1].as_of == signal.as_of:
            self._history[-1] = signal
        else:
            self._history.append(signal)

        if self._confirmed_name is None:
            self._confirmed_name = raw_name
            return raw_name, raw_confidence, False

        # Risk escalation is immediate. Exiting a safety regime is deliberately
        # subject to normal confirmation.
        if raw_name in SAFETY_REGIMES:
            self._confirmed_name = raw_name
            if raw_name == "crisis":
                # A crisis supersedes earlier lower-severity votes.
                self._history.clear()
                self._history.append(signal)
            return raw_name, raw_confidence, False

        if raw_name == self._confirmed_name:
            return raw_name, raw_confidence, False

        weighted_votes: dict[str, float] = {}
        confirmations = Counter(item.regime for item in self._history)
        for recency, item in enumerate(self._history, start=1):
            weight = recency * max(item.confidence, 0.05)
            weighted_votes[item.regime] = (
                weighted_votes.get(item.regime, 0.0) + weight
            )
        total_weight = sum(weighted_votes.values())
        candidate = max(weighted_votes, key=weighted_votes.get)
        candidate_share = weighted_votes[candidate] / max(total_weight, _EPSILON)

        may_switch = (
            candidate == raw_name
            and confirmations[candidate]
            >= self.regime_config.switch_confirmations
            and candidate_share >= self.regime_config.switch_vote_share
        )
        if may_switch:
            self._confirmed_name = candidate
            return candidate, min(0.97, max(raw_confidence, candidate_share)), False

        confirmed = self._confirmed_name
        confirmed_share = weighted_votes.get(confirmed, 0.0) / max(
            total_weight, _EPSILON
        )
        confidence = max(
            0.35,
            min(raw_confidence - 0.10, 0.45 + 0.40 * confirmed_share),
        )
        LOGGER.debug(
            "Regime held by hysteresis: raw=%s confirmed=%s "
            "candidate_share=%.3f history=%s",
            raw_name,
            confirmed,
            candidate_share,
            [item.regime for item in self._history],
        )
        return confirmed, confidence, True

    def detect(
        self,
        frame: pd.DataFrame,
        *,
        as_of: datetime | date | str | pd.Timestamp | None = None,
        smooth: bool = True,
    ) -> MarketRegime:
        """Detect the latest regime using no rows after ``as_of``."""

        with self._lock:
            data = self._prepare_prices(frame, as_of=as_of)
            metrics = self._metrics(data)
            rule_name, rule_confidence = self._rule_based(metrics)
            hmm_result = self._hmm_signal(
                data,
                metrics,
                rule_name,
                rule_confidence,
            )
            hmm_state: int | None = None
            hmm_posterior: float | None = None
            probabilities: Mapping[str, float] = MappingProxyType({})
            if hmm_result is None:
                raw_name = rule_name
                raw_confidence = rule_confidence
                method = "rules"
                hmm_used = False
            else:
                (
                    raw_name,
                    raw_confidence,
                    hmm_state,
                    hmm_posterior,
                    probabilities,
                ) = hmm_result
                method = "hybrid"
                hmm_used = True

            if smooth:
                confirmed_name, confirmed_confidence, was_smoothed = self._smooth(
                    raw_name,
                    raw_confidence,
                    metrics,
                )
            else:
                confirmed_name = raw_name
                confirmed_confidence = raw_confidence
                was_smoothed = False

            stale = bool(frame.attrs.get("stale", False))
            synthetic = bool(frame.attrs.get("is_synthetic", False))
            regime = self._build_regime(
                confirmed_name,
                confirmed_confidence,
                metrics,
                raw_name=raw_name,
                smoothed=was_smoothed,
                hmm_used=hmm_used,
                stale_input=stale,
            )
            self._last_diagnostics = RegimeDiagnostics(
                as_of=metrics.as_of.isoformat(),
                raw_regime=raw_name,
                confirmed_regime=confirmed_name,
                method=method,
                raw_confidence=raw_confidence,
                confirmed_confidence=regime.confidence,
                hmm_state=hmm_state,
                hmm_posterior=hmm_posterior,
                state_probabilities=probabilities,
                history_size=len(self._history),
                stale_input=stale,
                synthetic_input=synthetic,
            )
            return regime

    def fit_detect(
        self,
        frame: pd.DataFrame,
        *,
        as_of: datetime | date | str | pd.Timestamp | None = None,
        refit: bool = False,
        smooth: bool = True,
    ) -> MarketRegime:
        """Fit when needed, then detect. Rules remain available if HMM is optional."""

        if self.hmm_config.enabled and (
            refit
            or not self.trained
            or self.needs_refit(frame, as_of=as_of)
        ):
            self.fit(frame, as_of=as_of)
        return self.detect(frame, as_of=as_of, smooth=smooth)

    def reset_smoothing(self) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            self._history.clear()
            self._confirmed_name = None

    def _get_adjustments(self, regime: str) -> dict[str, float]:
        """Compatibility helper returning a defensive copy."""

        if regime not in self._adjustments:
            raise ValueError(f"Unknown regime: {regime!r}")
        return dict(self._adjustments[regime])

    @staticmethod
    def _compute_drawdown(close: pd.Series) -> float:
        """Compatibility helper: return current, not historical-minimum, drawdown."""

        series = pd.to_numeric(close, errors="coerce").dropna()
        if series.empty or (series <= 0).any():
            raise RegimeDataError("Close series must contain positive values")
        return float(series.iloc[-1] / series.cummax().iloc[-1] - 1.0)

    def format_report(self, regime: MarketRegime) -> str:
        """Create a concise human-readable report without mutating state."""

        characteristics = regime.characteristics
        lines = [
            "=" * 64,
            "MARKET REGIME DETECTION",
            "=" * 64,
            f"Detected regime       : {regime.name.upper().replace('_', ' ')}",
            f"Confidence            : {regime.confidence:.1%}",
            f"Return (20d)          : "
            f"{characteristics.get('return_20d', 0.0):.2%}",
            f"Return (60d)          : "
            f"{characteristics.get('return_60d', 0.0):.2%}",
            f"Volatility (20d ann.) : "
            f"{characteristics.get('volatility_20d', 0.0):.2%}",
            f"Current drawdown      : "
            f"{characteristics.get('current_drawdown', 0.0):.2%}",
            f"Max drawdown (252d)   : "
            f"{characteristics.get('max_drawdown_252d', 0.0):.2%}",
            "",
            "Recommended allocation adjustments:",
        ]
        for asset, change in regime.recommended_adjustments.items():
            direction = "UP" if change > 0 else ("DOWN" if change < 0 else "HOLD")
            lines.append(
                f"  {direction:>4}  {asset.capitalize():<10} {change:+.1%}"
            )
        lines.append("=" * 64)
        return "\n".join(lines)


__all__ = [
    "HMMConfig",
    "MarketRegimeDetector",
    "ModelFitReport",
    "REGIME_NAMES",
    "RegimeConfig",
    "RegimeDataError",
    "RegimeDetectionError",
    "RegimeDiagnostics",
    "RegimeModelError",
    "SAFETY_REGIMES",
    "StateProfile",
]


#MODUL 4






import importlib
import logging
import math
import threading
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from types import MappingProxyType
from typing import Any, Callable, Final, Mapping, Sequence

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

# This distribution is a single-file build. Core names are defined above.


UTC: Final = timezone.utc
LOGGER = logging.getLogger(f"{APP_NAME}.ensemble")
MODEL_NAMES: Final = ("xgboost", "rf", "lstm", "rl")
_EPSILON: Final = 1e-12


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Exceptions and reports
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


class EnsembleError(SmartInvestError):
    """Base exception for Module 4."""


class EnsembleDataError(EnsembleError, ValueError):
    """Raised when features or targets are invalid."""


class EnsembleNotTrainedError(EnsembleError):
    """Raised when strict prediction is requested without a matching artifact."""


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ModelMetrics:
    model: str
    observations: int
    mse: float
    rmse: float
    mae: float
    directional_accuracy: float
    correlation: float | None
    skill_vs_zero: float


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class TrainingReport:
    success: bool
    ticker: str
    horizon_days: int
    reason: str
    rows: int
    feature_count: int
    validation_observations: int
    validation_folds: int
    trained_through: str | None
    available_models: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    metrics: Mapping[str, ModelMetrics] = field(
        default_factory=lambda: MappingProxyType({})
    )
    weights: Mapping[str, float] = field(
        default_factory=lambda: MappingProxyType({})
    )
    retained_previous_artifact: bool = False


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PredictionDiagnostics:
    ticker: str
    horizon_days: int
    as_of: str | None
    trained_through: str | None
    available_models: tuple[str, ...]
    raw_predictions: Mapping[str, float]
    effective_weights: Mapping[str, float]
    ensemble_prediction: float
    disagreement: float
    validation_rmse: float
    zero_baseline_rmse: float
    direction_threshold: float
    confidence: float
    confidence_is_probability: bool = False


@dataclass(slots=True)
class _Artifact:
    ticker: str
    horizon: int
    feature_names: tuple[str, ...]
    scaler: "_RobustScaler"
    models: dict[str, Any]
    weights: dict[str, float]
    metrics: dict[str, ModelMetrics]
    trained_through: pd.Timestamp
    training_rows: int
    validation_rmse: float
    baseline_rmse: float
    target_lower_bound: float
    target_upper_bound: float
    stale_training_input: bool
    synthetic_training_input: bool
    lstm_sequence_length: int


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configuration
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _ensemble_finite(name: str, value: float) -> float:
    number = float(value)
    if not math.isfinite(number):
        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be finite")
    return number


def _ensemble_positive(name: str, value: float) -> float:
    number = _ensemble_finite(name, value)
    if number <= 0:
        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be greater than zero")
    return number


def _ensemble_positive_integer(name: str, value: int) -> int:
    if isinstance(value, bool) or int(value) != value or value <= 0:
        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be a positive integer")
    return int(value)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class EnsembleConfig:
    enabled_models: tuple[str, ...] = MODEL_NAMES
    minimum_samples: int = 160
    minimum_train_samples: int = 90
    minimum_validation_samples: int = 24
    validation_fraction: float = 0.18
    walk_forward_folds: int = 3
    random_seed: int = 42
    ridge_alpha: float = 2.0

    target_clip_quantile: float = 0.005
    weight_temperature: float = 0.35
    weight_floor: float = 0.02
    direction_threshold: float = 0.005
    noise_threshold_multiplier: float = 0.25
    maximum_absolute_prediction: float = 0.75

    rf_estimators: int = 250
    rf_max_depth: int | None = 8
    rf_min_samples_leaf: int = 4
    xgb_estimators: int = 350
    xgb_max_depth: int = 4
    xgb_learning_rate: float = 0.03
    xgb_early_stopping_rounds: int = 25
    n_jobs: int = -1

    lstm_sequence_length: int = 20
    lstm_units: tuple[int, int] = (48, 24)
    lstm_dropout: float = 0.20
    lstm_epochs: int = 60
    lstm_batch_size: int = 32
    lstm_patience: int = 7

    allow_synthetic_input: bool = False
    stale_confidence_penalty: float = 0.10

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        models = tuple(dict.fromkeys(str(name).lower() for name in self.enabled_models))
        unknown = set(models).difference(MODEL_NAMES)
        if unknown:
            raise ValueError(
                "Unknown enabled_models: " + ", ".join(sorted(unknown))
            )
        if not models:
            raise ValueError("enabled_models cannot be empty")
        object.__setattr__(self, "enabled_models", models)
        for name in (
            "minimum_samples",
            "minimum_train_samples",
            "minimum_validation_samples",
            "walk_forward_folds",
            "rf_estimators",
            "rf_min_samples_leaf",
            "xgb_estimators",
            "xgb_max_depth",
            "xgb_early_stopping_rounds",
            "lstm_sequence_length",
            "lstm_epochs",
            "lstm_batch_size",
            "lstm_patience",
        ):
            _ensemble_positive_integer(name, getattr(self, name))
        if self.rf_max_depth is not None:
            _ensemble_positive_integer("rf_max_depth", self.rf_max_depth)
        for name in (
            "ridge_alpha",
            "weight_temperature",
            "direction_threshold",
            "noise_threshold_multiplier",
            "maximum_absolute_prediction",
            "xgb_learning_rate",
        ):
            _ensemble_positive(name, getattr(self, name))
        if not 0.05 <= self.validation_fraction <= 0.40:
            raise ValueError("validation_fraction must be between 0.05 and 0.40")
        if not 0.0 <= self.target_clip_quantile < 0.10:
            raise ValueError("target_clip_quantile must be in [0, 0.10)")
        if not 0.0 <= self.weight_floor < 0.25:
            raise ValueError("weight_floor must be in [0, 0.25)")
        if not 0.0 <= self.lstm_dropout < 1.0:
            raise ValueError("lstm_dropout must be in [0, 1)")
        if (
            len(self.lstm_units) != 2
            or any(int(unit) != unit or unit <= 0 for unit in self.lstm_units)
        ):
            raise ValueError("lstm_units must contain two positive integers")
        if not 0.0 <= self.stale_confidence_penalty < 1.0:
            raise ValueError("stale_confidence_penalty must be in [0, 1)")
        if self.minimum_train_samples >= self.minimum_samples:
            raise ValueError("minimum_train_samples must be below minimum_samples")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Internal estimators and preprocessing
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


class _RobustScaler:
    """Small dependency-free median/IQR scaler fitted on training rows only."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.center_: np.ndarray | None = None
        self.scale_: np.ndarray | None = None

    def fit(self, values: np.ndarray) -> "_RobustScaler":
        if values.ndim != 2 or not len(values):
            raise EnsembleDataError("Scaler expects a non-empty 2D array")
        center = np.median(values, axis=0)
        q25, q75 = np.percentile(values, [25, 75], axis=0)
        scale = q75 - q25
        std = np.std(values, axis=0, ddof=0)
        scale = np.where(scale > _EPSILON, scale, std)
        scale = np.where(scale > _EPSILON, scale, 1.0)
        self.center_ = center.astype(float)
        self.scale_ = scale.astype(float)
        return self

    def transform(self, values: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        if self.center_ is None or self.scale_ is None:
            raise EnsembleError("Scaler has not been fitted")
        transformed = (np.asarray(values, dtype=float) - self.center_) / self.scale_
        return np.clip(transformed, -20.0, 20.0)

    def fit_transform(self, values: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        return self.fit(values).transform(values)


class _RidgeRegressor:
    """Closed-form ridge regression with an unpenalized intercept."""

    def __init__(self, alpha: float) -> None:
        self.alpha = _ensemble_positive("alpha", alpha)
        self.coef_: np.ndarray | None = None

    def fit(self, values: np.ndarray, target: np.ndarray) -> "_RidgeRegressor":
        x = np.asarray(values, dtype=float)
        y = np.asarray(target, dtype=float)
        design = np.column_stack((np.ones(len(x)), x))
        penalty = np.eye(design.shape[1]) * self.alpha
        penalty[0, 0] = 0.0
        try:
            self.coef_ = np.linalg.solve(
                design.T @ design + penalty,
                design.T @ y,
            )
        except np.linalg.LinAlgError:
            self.coef_ = np.linalg.pinv(design.T @ design + penalty) @ (
                design.T @ y
            )
        return self

    def predict(self, values: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        if self.coef_ is None:
            raise EnsembleError("Ridge model has not been fitted")
        design = np.column_stack(
            (np.ones(len(values)), np.asarray(values, dtype=float))
        )
        return np.asarray(design @ self.coef_, dtype=float)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Ensemble
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


class EnsembleAI:
    """Train and serve horizon-specific time-series ensemble artifacts."""

    def __init__(self, config: EnsembleConfig | None = None) -> None:
        self.config = config or EnsembleConfig()
        self._artifacts: dict[tuple[str, int], _Artifact] = {}
        self._artifact_lock = threading.RLock()
        self._key_locks: dict[tuple[str, int], threading.Lock] = {}
        self._key_locks_lock = threading.Lock()
        self._last_training_reports: dict[tuple[str, int], TrainingReport] = {}
        self._last_prediction_diagnostics: dict[
            tuple[str, int], PredictionDiagnostics
        ] = {}

        # Compatibility views used by some older SmartInvest modules.
        self.models: dict[str, Any] = {}
        self.scalers: dict[str, Any] = {}
        self.weights: dict[str, float] = {}
        self.mse_scores: dict[str, Any] = {}
        self._trained_tickers: set[str] = set()

    @staticmethod
    def _ticker(ticker: str) -> str:
        symbol = str(ticker).strip().upper()
        if not symbol or any(character.isspace() for character in symbol):
            raise EnsembleDataError(f"Invalid ticker: {ticker!r}")
        return symbol

    @staticmethod
    def _horizon(horizon: int) -> int:
        return _ensemble_positive_integer("horizon", horizon)

    @staticmethod
    def _is_synthetic_input(frame: pd.DataFrame) -> bool:
        return bool(
            frame.attrs.get("is_synthetic", False)
            or frame.attrs.get("source") == "synthetic"
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _is_stale_input(frame: pd.DataFrame) -> bool:
        return bool(
            frame.attrs.get("stale", False)
            or frame.attrs.get("source") == "cache_stale"
        )

    def _key_lock(self, key: tuple[str, int]) -> threading.Lock:
        with self._key_locks_lock:
            return self._key_locks.setdefault(key, threading.Lock())

    @property
    def trained_keys(self) -> tuple[tuple[str, int], ...]:
        with self._artifact_lock:
            return tuple(sorted(self._artifacts))

    def get_training_report(
        self,
        ticker: str,
        horizon: int = 5,
    ) -> TrainingReport | None:
        key = (self._ticker(ticker), self._horizon(horizon))
        with self._artifact_lock:
            return self._last_training_reports.get(key)

    def get_prediction_diagnostics(
        self,
        ticker: str,
        horizon: int = 5,
    ) -> PredictionDiagnostics | None:
        key = (self._ticker(ticker), self._horizon(horizon))
        with self._artifact_lock:
            return self._last_prediction_diagnostics.get(key)

    @staticmethod
    def build_forward_return_target(
        close: pd.Series,
        horizon: int = 5,
        *,
        log_return: bool = False,
        name: str | None = None,
    ) -> pd.Series:
        """Build return from close[t] to close[t+horizon], indexed at t."""

        steps = EnsembleAI._horizon(horizon)
        if not isinstance(close, pd.Series) or close.empty:
            raise EnsembleDataError("close must be a non-empty pandas Series")
        prices = pd.to_numeric(close, errors="coerce")
        if (prices.dropna() <= 0).any():
            raise EnsembleDataError("close contains non-positive prices")
        if log_return:
            target = np.log(prices.shift(-steps) / prices)
        else:
            target = prices.shift(-steps) / prices - 1.0
        target.name = name or f"forward_return_{steps}d"
        target.attrs = {
            "horizon_days": steps,
            "target_type": "forward_log_return" if log_return else "forward_return",
        }
        return target

    def _prepare_training_data(
        self,
        features: pd.DataFrame,
        target: pd.Series,
        *,
        horizon: int,
    ) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.Series]:
        if not isinstance(features, pd.DataFrame) or features.empty:
            raise EnsembleDataError("features must be a non-empty DataFrame")
        if not isinstance(target, pd.Series) or target.empty:
            raise EnsembleDataError("target must be a non-empty pandas Series")
        if self._is_synthetic_input(features) and not (
            self.config.allow_synthetic_input
        ):
            raise EnsembleDataError(
                "Synthetic features are disabled for ensemble training"
            )
        if features.columns.duplicated().any():
            raise EnsembleDataError("features contain duplicate column names")
        names = tuple(str(column) for column in features.columns)
        if len(set(names)) != len(names):
            raise EnsembleDataError(
                "feature names are not unique after string conversion"
            )

        x = features.copy()
        x.columns = names
        x.index = pd.to_datetime(x.index, errors="coerce", utc=True)
        x = x.loc[~x.index.isna()]
        x = x.loc[~x.index.duplicated(keep="last")].sort_index()
        y = target.copy()
        y.index = pd.to_datetime(y.index, errors="coerce", utc=True)
        y = y.loc[~y.index.isna()]
        y = y.loc[~y.index.duplicated(keep="last")].sort_index()

        declared_horizon = target.attrs.get("horizon_days")
        if declared_horizon is not None and int(declared_horizon) != horizon:
            raise EnsembleDataError(
                f"target declares horizon {declared_horizon}, requested {horizon}"
            )

        for column in x.columns:
            x[column] = pd.to_numeric(x[column], errors="coerce")
        y = pd.to_numeric(y, errors="coerce")
        common_index = x.index.intersection(y.index)
        if common_index.empty:
            raise EnsembleDataError("features and target have no common timestamps")
        x = x.loc[common_index]
        y = y.loc[common_index]
        finite_rows = np.isfinite(x.to_numpy(dtype=float)).all(axis=1)
        finite_rows &= np.isfinite(y.to_numpy(dtype=float))
        x = x.loc[finite_rows]
        y = y.loc[finite_rows]
        if len(x) < self.config.minimum_samples:
            raise EnsembleDataError(
                f"Training requires at least {self.config.minimum_samples} "
                f"aligned finite rows; received {len(x)}"
            )
        if not x.index.is_monotonic_increasing:
            raise EnsembleDataError("training index must be chronological")
        return x, y.astype(float)

    def _walk_forward_splits(
        self,
        observations: int,
        horizon: int,
    ) -> list[tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]]:
        validation_size = max(
            self.config.minimum_validation_samples,
            int(math.ceil(observations * self.config.validation_fraction)),
        )
        validation_size = min(
            validation_size,
            observations - self.config.minimum_train_samples - horizon,
        )
        if validation_size < self.config.minimum_validation_samples:
            raise EnsembleDataError(
                "Not enough rows for an embargoed validation window"
            )

        possible_starts = [
            observations - validation_size * offset
            for offset in range(self.config.walk_forward_folds, 0, -1)
        ]
        splits: list[tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]] = []
        for validation_start in possible_starts:
            validation_end = min(observations, validation_start + validation_size)
            train_end = validation_start - horizon
            if (
                train_end < self.config.minimum_train_samples
                or validation_end - validation_start
                < self.config.minimum_validation_samples
            ):
                continue
            splits.append(
                (
                    np.arange(0, train_end, dtype=int),
                    np.arange(validation_start, validation_end, dtype=int),
                )
            )
        if not splits:
            raise EnsembleDataError("No valid walk-forward splits were produced")
        return splits

    def _target_bounds(self, target: np.ndarray) -> tuple[float, float]:
        quantile = self.config.target_clip_quantile
        if quantile <= 0:
            lower, upper = float(np.min(target)), float(np.max(target))
        else:
            lower, upper = (
                float(value)
                for value in np.quantile(target, [quantile, 1.0 - quantile])
            )
        if not math.isfinite(lower) or not math.isfinite(upper):
            raise EnsembleDataError("target bounds are not finite")
        if lower >= upper:
            spread = max(float(np.std(target, ddof=0)), 1e-6)
            center = float(np.mean(target))
            lower, upper = center - spread, center + spread
        return lower, upper

    def _load_sklearn_rf(self) -> type[Any]:
        dependencies.load("sklearn")
        module = importlib.import_module("sklearn.ensemble")
        return module.RandomForestRegressor

    def _load_xgboost(self) -> Any:
        return dependencies.load("xgboost")

    def _load_tensorflow(self) -> Any:
        return dependencies.load("tensorflow")

    def _new_tabular_model(
        self,
        name: str,
        *,
        validation: tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray] | None = None,
    ) -> Any:
        if name == "rl":
            return _RidgeRegressor(self.config.ridge_alpha)
        if name == "rf":
            RandomForestRegressor = self._load_sklearn_rf()
            return RandomForestRegressor(
                n_estimators=self.config.rf_estimators,
                max_depth=self.config.rf_max_depth,
                min_samples_leaf=self.config.rf_min_samples_leaf,
                max_features="sqrt",
                random_state=self.config.random_seed,
                n_jobs=self.config.n_jobs,
            )
        if name == "xgboost":
            xgb = self._load_xgboost()
            parameters: dict[str, Any] = {
                "n_estimators": self.config.xgb_estimators,
                "max_depth": self.config.xgb_max_depth,
                "learning_rate": self.config.xgb_learning_rate,
                "subsample": 0.80,
                "colsample_bytree": 0.85,
                "min_child_weight": 5,
                "reg_alpha": 0.05,
                "reg_lambda": 1.0,
                "objective": "reg:squarederror",
                "eval_metric": "rmse",
                "tree_method": "hist",
                "random_state": self.config.random_seed,
                "n_jobs": self.config.n_jobs,
                "verbosity": 0,
            }
            if validation is not None:
                parameters["early_stopping_rounds"] = (
                    self.config.xgb_early_stopping_rounds
                )
            return xgb.XGBRegressor(**parameters)
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported tabular model: {name}")

    @staticmethod
    def _metric(
        name: str,
        actual: np.ndarray,
        predicted: np.ndarray,
    ) -> ModelMetrics:
        y = np.asarray(actual, dtype=float)
        p = np.asarray(predicted, dtype=float)
        valid = np.isfinite(y) & np.isfinite(p)
        y, p = y[valid], p[valid]
        if not len(y):
            raise EnsembleDataError(f"{name}: no finite validation predictions")
        errors = y - p
        mse = float(np.mean(errors**2))
        baseline_mse = float(np.mean(y**2))
        direction = float(np.mean(np.sign(y) == np.sign(p)))
        correlation: float | None = None
        if len(y) > 2 and np.std(y) > _EPSILON and np.std(p) > _EPSILON:
            candidate = float(np.corrcoef(y, p)[0, 1])
            correlation = candidate if math.isfinite(candidate) else None
        return ModelMetrics(
            model=name,
            observations=len(y),
            mse=mse,
            rmse=math.sqrt(mse),
            mae=float(np.mean(np.abs(errors))),
            directional_accuracy=direction,
            correlation=correlation,
            skill_vs_zero=1.0 - mse / max(baseline_mse, _EPSILON),
        )

    def _evaluate_tabular_model(
        self,
        name: str,
        values: np.ndarray,
        target: np.ndarray,
        splits: Sequence[tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]],
    ) -> ModelMetrics:
        actual_parts: list[np.ndarray] = []
        prediction_parts: list[np.ndarray] = []
        for train_indices, validation_indices in splits:
            scaler = _RobustScaler().fit(values[train_indices])
            x_train = scaler.transform(values[train_indices])
            x_validation = scaler.transform(values[validation_indices])
            lower, upper = self._target_bounds(target[train_indices])
            y_train = np.clip(target[train_indices], lower, upper)
            model = self._new_tabular_model(
                name,
                validation=(x_validation, target[validation_indices])
                if name == "xgboost"
                else None,
            )
            if name == "xgboost":
                model.fit(
                    x_train,
                    y_train,
                    eval_set=[(x_validation, target[validation_indices])],
                    verbose=False,
                )
            else:
                model.fit(x_train, y_train)
            predicted = np.asarray(model.predict(x_validation), dtype=float).reshape(-1)
            actual_parts.append(target[validation_indices])
            prediction_parts.append(predicted)
        return self._metric(
            name,
            np.concatenate(actual_parts),
            np.concatenate(prediction_parts),
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _sequence_arrays(
        values: np.ndarray,
        target: np.ndarray,
        target_indices: np.ndarray,
        sequence_length: int,
    ) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
        sequences: list[np.ndarray] = []
        targets: list[float] = []
        for index in target_indices:
            start = int(index) - sequence_length + 1
            if start < 0:
                continue
            sequences.append(values[start : int(index) + 1])
            targets.append(float(target[int(index)]))
        if not sequences:
            return (
                np.empty((0, sequence_length, values.shape[1]), dtype=float),
                np.empty(0, dtype=float),
            )
        return np.asarray(sequences, dtype=float), np.asarray(targets, dtype=float)

    def _new_lstm(self, tensorflow: Any, feature_count: int) -> Any:
        tensorflow.keras.utils.set_random_seed(self.config.random_seed)
        first_units, second_units = self.config.lstm_units
        model = tensorflow.keras.Sequential(
            [
                tensorflow.keras.layers.Input(
                    shape=(self.config.lstm_sequence_length, feature_count)
                ),
                tensorflow.keras.layers.LSTM(
                    first_units,
                    return_sequences=True,
                ),
                tensorflow.keras.layers.Dropout(self.config.lstm_dropout),
                tensorflow.keras.layers.LSTM(second_units),
                tensorflow.keras.layers.Dropout(self.config.lstm_dropout),
                tensorflow.keras.layers.Dense(1),
            ]
        )
        model.compile(
            optimizer=tensorflow.keras.optimizers.Adam(learning_rate=1e-3),
            loss=tensorflow.keras.losses.Huber(),
        )
        return model

    def _evaluate_lstm(
        self,
        values: np.ndarray,
        target: np.ndarray,
        split: tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray],
    ) -> ModelMetrics:
        tensorflow = self._load_tensorflow()
        train_indices, validation_indices = split
        scaler = _RobustScaler().fit(values[train_indices])
        transformed = scaler.transform(values)
        x_train, y_train = self._sequence_arrays(
            transformed,
            target,
            train_indices,
            self.config.lstm_sequence_length,
        )
        x_validation, y_validation = self._sequence_arrays(
            transformed,
            target,
            validation_indices,
            self.config.lstm_sequence_length,
        )
        if len(x_train) < self.config.minimum_train_samples or len(x_validation) < (
            self.config.minimum_validation_samples
        ):
            raise EnsembleDataError("Insufficient sequences for LSTM validation")
        lower, upper = self._target_bounds(y_train)
        model = self._new_lstm(tensorflow, values.shape[1])
        early_stopping = tensorflow.keras.callbacks.EarlyStopping(
            monitor="val_loss",
            patience=self.config.lstm_patience,
            restore_best_weights=True,
            min_delta=1e-6,
        )
        model.fit(
            x_train,
            np.clip(y_train, lower, upper),
            validation_data=(x_validation, y_validation),
            epochs=self.config.lstm_epochs,
            batch_size=self.config.lstm_batch_size,
            shuffle=False,
            verbose=0,
            callbacks=[early_stopping],
        )
        predicted = np.asarray(
            model.predict(x_validation, verbose=0),
            dtype=float,
        ).reshape(-1)
        tensorflow.keras.backend.clear_session()
        return self._metric("lstm", y_validation, predicted)

    def _fit_final_tabular(
        self,
        name: str,
        scaled_values: np.ndarray,
        target: np.ndarray,
        lower: float,
        upper: float,
    ) -> Any:
        model = self._new_tabular_model(name)
        model.fit(scaled_values, np.clip(target, lower, upper))
        return model

    def _fit_final_lstm(
        self,
        scaled_values: np.ndarray,
        target: np.ndarray,
        lower: float,
        upper: float,
    ) -> Any:
        tensorflow = self._load_tensorflow()
        indices = np.arange(len(target), dtype=int)
        x_sequence, y_sequence = self._sequence_arrays(
            scaled_values,
            target,
            indices,
            self.config.lstm_sequence_length,
        )
        if len(x_sequence) < self.config.minimum_train_samples:
            raise EnsembleDataError("Insufficient sequences for final LSTM")
        model = self._new_lstm(tensorflow, scaled_values.shape[1])
        callbacks: list[Any] = []
        validation_split = 0.0
        if len(x_sequence) >= self.config.minimum_train_samples + (
            self.config.minimum_validation_samples
        ):
            validation_split = min(
                0.20,
                max(
                    0.10,
                    self.config.minimum_validation_samples / len(x_sequence),
                ),
            )
            callbacks.append(
                tensorflow.keras.callbacks.EarlyStopping(
                    monitor="val_loss",
                    patience=self.config.lstm_patience,
                    restore_best_weights=True,
                    min_delta=1e-6,
                )
            )
        model.fit(
            x_sequence,
            np.clip(y_sequence, lower, upper),
            validation_split=validation_split,
            epochs=self.config.lstm_epochs,
            batch_size=self.config.lstm_batch_size,
            shuffle=False,
            verbose=0,
            callbacks=callbacks,
        )
        return model

    def _weights_from_metrics(
        self,
        metrics: Mapping[str, ModelMetrics],
        target_scale: float,
    ) -> dict[str, float]:
        if not metrics:
            return {}
        scale = max(float(target_scale), 1e-6)
        names = list(metrics)
        logits = np.array(
            [
                -metrics[name].rmse
                / (scale * self.config.weight_temperature)
                + 0.50 * (metrics[name].directional_accuracy - 0.50)
                + 0.20 * max(-1.0, min(1.0, metrics[name].skill_vs_zero))
                for name in names
            ],
            dtype=float,
        )
        logits -= float(np.max(logits))
        raw = np.exp(np.clip(logits, -50.0, 0.0))
        raw /= max(float(raw.sum()), _EPSILON)
        floor = min(
            self.config.weight_floor,
            (1.0 - 1e-9) / len(names),
        )
        adjusted = floor + (1.0 - floor * len(names)) * raw
        adjusted /= adjusted.sum()
        return {
            name: float(weight)
            for name, weight in zip(names, adjusted, strict=True)
        }

    def _commit_artifact(
        self,
        key: tuple[str, int],
        artifact: _Artifact,
        report: TrainingReport,
    ) -> None:
        ticker, horizon = key
        with self._artifact_lock:
            self._artifacts[key] = artifact
            self._last_training_reports[key] = report
            self._trained_tickers.add(ticker)
            suffix = f"{ticker}_{horizon}d"
            self.scalers[suffix] = artifact.scaler
            self.weights = dict(artifact.weights)
            self.mse_scores[suffix] = {
                name: metric.mse for name, metric in artifact.metrics.items()
            }
            for name, model in artifact.models.items():
                self.models[f"{suffix}_{name}"] = model

    def train(
        self,
        ticker: str,
        features: pd.DataFrame,
        target: pd.Series | None = None,
        *,
        close: pd.Series | None = None,
        horizon: int = 5,
    ) -> bool:
        """Train a horizon-specific artifact.

        Supply either an explicitly indexed forward-return ``target`` or a
        ``close`` series from which this method will safely build one.
        """

        symbol = self._ticker(ticker)
        steps = self._horizon(horizon)
        key = (symbol, steps)
        with self._key_lock(key):
            with self._artifact_lock:
                previous = self._artifacts.get(key)
            try:
                if target is None:
                    if close is None:
                        raise EnsembleDataError(
                            "Provide target or close when training"
                        )
                    target = self.build_forward_return_target(close, steps)
                x_frame, y_series = self._prepare_training_data(
                    features,
                    target,
                    horizon=steps,
                )
                values = x_frame.to_numpy(dtype=float)
                target_values = y_series.to_numpy(dtype=float)
                splits = self._walk_forward_splits(len(values), steps)

                metrics: dict[str, ModelMetrics] = {}
                failures: dict[str, str] = {}
                for name in self.config.enabled_models:
                    try:
                        if name == "lstm":
                            # Neural validation uses the latest embargoed fold
                            # to keep training cost bounded.
                            metric = self._evaluate_lstm(
                                values,
                                target_values,
                                splits[-1],
                            )
                        else:
                            metric = self._evaluate_tabular_model(
                                name,
                                values,
                                target_values,
                                splits,
                            )
                        metrics[name] = metric
                    except Exception as exc:
                        failures[name] = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
                        LOGGER.warning(
                            "%s %dd: %s validation unavailable: %s",
                            symbol,
                            steps,
                            name,
                            exc,
                        )
                if not metrics:
                    raise EnsembleError(
                        "No model produced out-of-sample validation predictions"
                    )

                final_scaler = _RobustScaler().fit(values)
                scaled_values = final_scaler.transform(values)
                lower, upper = self._target_bounds(target_values)
                final_models: dict[str, Any] = {}
                for name in metrics:
                    try:
                        final_models[name] = (
                            self._fit_final_lstm(
                                scaled_values,
                                target_values,
                                lower,
                                upper,
                            )
                            if name == "lstm"
                            else self._fit_final_tabular(
                                name,
                                scaled_values,
                                target_values,
                                lower,
                                upper,
                            )
                        )
                    except Exception as exc:
                        failures[name] = (
                            f"final fit: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
                        )
                        LOGGER.warning(
                            "%s %dd: %s final fit unavailable: %s",
                            symbol,
                            steps,
                            name,
                            exc,
                        )
                metrics = {
                    name: metric
                    for name, metric in metrics.items()
                    if name in final_models
                }
                if not final_models:
                    raise EnsembleError("No model completed final fitting")

                validation_targets = np.concatenate(
                    [target_values[validation] for _, validation in splits]
                )
                target_scale = float(np.std(validation_targets, ddof=0))
                weights = self._weights_from_metrics(metrics, target_scale)
                validation_rmse = math.sqrt(
                    sum(weights[name] * metrics[name].mse for name in weights)
                )
                baseline_rmse = math.sqrt(
                    float(np.mean(validation_targets**2))
                )
                bounded_lower = max(
                    -self.config.maximum_absolute_prediction,
                    lower,
                )
                bounded_upper = min(
                    self.config.maximum_absolute_prediction,
                    upper,
                )
                if bounded_lower >= bounded_upper:
                    bounded_lower = -self.config.maximum_absolute_prediction
                    bounded_upper = self.config.maximum_absolute_prediction
                artifact = _Artifact(
                    ticker=symbol,
                    horizon=steps,
                    feature_names=tuple(x_frame.columns),
                    scaler=final_scaler,
                    models=final_models,
                    weights=weights,
                    metrics=metrics,
                    trained_through=x_frame.index[-1],
                    training_rows=len(x_frame),
                    validation_rmse=validation_rmse,
                    baseline_rmse=baseline_rmse,
                    target_lower_bound=bounded_lower,
                    target_upper_bound=bounded_upper,
                    stale_training_input=self._is_stale_input(features),
                    synthetic_training_input=self._is_synthetic_input(features),
                    lstm_sequence_length=self.config.lstm_sequence_length,
                )
                reason = "trained"
                if failures:
                    reason += "; unavailable: " + ", ".join(
                        f"{name} ({message})"
                        for name, message in failures.items()
                    )
                report = TrainingReport(
                    success=True,
                    ticker=symbol,
                    horizon_days=steps,
                    reason=reason,
                    rows=len(x_frame),
                    feature_count=x_frame.shape[1],
                    validation_observations=sum(
                        len(validation) for _, validation in splits
                    ),
                    validation_folds=len(splits),
                    trained_through=x_frame.index[-1].isoformat(),
                    available_models=tuple(final_models),
                    metrics=MappingProxyType(dict(metrics)),
                    weights=MappingProxyType(dict(weights)),
                )
                self._commit_artifact(key, artifact, report)
                return True
            except Exception as exc:
                retained = previous is not None
                report = TrainingReport(
                    success=retained,
                    ticker=symbol,
                    horizon_days=steps,
                    reason=(
                        f"retraining failed; retained previous artifact: "
                        f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
                        if retained
                        else f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
                    ),
                    rows=previous.training_rows if previous else 0,
                    feature_count=len(previous.feature_names) if previous else 0,
                    validation_observations=0,
                    validation_folds=0,
                    trained_through=(
                        previous.trained_through.isoformat()
                        if previous
                        else None
                    ),
                    available_models=(
                        tuple(previous.models) if previous else ()
                    ),
                    metrics=MappingProxyType(
                        dict(previous.metrics) if previous else {}
                    ),
                    weights=MappingProxyType(
                        dict(previous.weights) if previous else {}
                    ),
                    retained_previous_artifact=retained,
                )
                with self._artifact_lock:
                    self._last_training_reports[key] = report
                LOGGER.error(
                    "Ensemble training failed for %s %dd: %s",
                    symbol,
                    steps,
                    exc,
                )
                return retained

    def _prepare_prediction_features(
        self,
        features: pd.DataFrame,
        artifact: _Artifact,
    ) -> pd.DataFrame:
        if not isinstance(features, pd.DataFrame) or features.empty:
            raise EnsembleDataError("features must be a non-empty DataFrame")
        if self._is_synthetic_input(features) and not (
            self.config.allow_synthetic_input
        ):
            raise EnsembleDataError(
                "Synthetic features are disabled for ensemble prediction"
            )
        missing = [
            name for name in artifact.feature_names if name not in features.columns
        ]
        if missing:
            raise EnsembleDataError(
                "Prediction features missing trained columns: "
                + ", ".join(missing)
            )
        frame = features.loc[:, list(artifact.feature_names)].copy()
        frame.index = pd.to_datetime(frame.index, errors="coerce", utc=True)
        frame = frame.loc[~frame.index.isna()]
        frame = frame.loc[~frame.index.duplicated(keep="last")].sort_index()
        for column in frame.columns:
            frame[column] = pd.to_numeric(frame[column], errors="coerce")
        finite = np.isfinite(frame.to_numpy(dtype=float)).all(axis=1)
        frame = frame.loc[finite]
        if frame.empty:
            raise EnsembleDataError(
                "No finite prediction-feature rows remain"
            )
        return frame

    def _model_predictions(
        self,
        artifact: _Artifact,
        frame: pd.DataFrame,
    ) -> tuple[dict[str, float], dict[str, str]]:
        latest = artifact.scaler.transform(
            frame.iloc[-1:].to_numpy(dtype=float)
        )
        predictions: dict[str, float] = {}
        failures: dict[str, str] = {}
        for name, model in artifact.models.items():
            try:
                if name == "lstm":
                    length = artifact.lstm_sequence_length
                    if len(frame) < length:
                        raise EnsembleDataError(
                            f"LSTM requires {length} recent feature rows"
                        )
                    sequence = artifact.scaler.transform(
                        frame.iloc[-length:].to_numpy(dtype=float)
                    ).reshape(1, length, -1)
                    predicted = float(
                        np.asarray(
                            model.predict(sequence, verbose=0),
                            dtype=float,
                        ).reshape(-1)[0]
                    )
                else:
                    predicted = float(
                        np.asarray(model.predict(latest), dtype=float).reshape(-1)[0]
                    )
                if not math.isfinite(predicted):
                    raise EnsembleError("prediction is not finite")
                predictions[name] = float(
                    np.clip(
                        predicted,
                        artifact.target_lower_bound,
                        artifact.target_upper_bound,
                    )
                )
            except Exception as exc:
                failures[name] = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
                LOGGER.warning(
                    "%s %dd: %s prediction unavailable: %s",
                    artifact.ticker,
                    artifact.horizon,
                    name,
                    exc,
                )
        return predictions, failures

    def _confidence(
        self,
        artifact: _Artifact,
        predictions: Mapping[str, float],
        effective_weights: Mapping[str, float],
        disagreement: float,
        stale_features: bool,
    ) -> float:
        weighted_skill = sum(
            effective_weights[name]
            * max(-1.0, min(1.0, artifact.metrics[name].skill_vs_zero))
            for name in effective_weights
        )
        weighted_direction = sum(
            effective_weights[name]
            * artifact.metrics[name].directional_accuracy
            for name in effective_weights
        )
        performance = max(
            0.0,
            min(
                1.0,
                0.50
                + 0.35 * weighted_skill
                + 0.30 * (weighted_direction - 0.50),
            ),
        )
        agreement = (
            math.exp(
                -disagreement / max(artifact.validation_rmse, 1e-6)
            )
            if len(predictions) >= 2
            else 0.50
        )
        model_coverage = len(predictions) / max(len(artifact.models), 1)
        model_evidence = model_coverage * min(1.0, len(predictions) / 3.0)
        confidence = (
            0.65 * performance + 0.25 * agreement + 0.10 * model_evidence
        )
        if artifact.stale_training_input:
            confidence -= self.config.stale_confidence_penalty / 2.0
        if stale_features:
            confidence -= self.config.stale_confidence_penalty
        return min(0.95, max(0.01, confidence))

    def predict(
        self,
        ticker: str,
        features: pd.DataFrame,
        horizon: int = 5,
        *,
        strict: bool = False,
    ) -> EnsemblePrediction:
        """Predict the exact horizon for which an artifact was trained."""

        symbol = self._ticker(ticker)
        steps = self._horizon(horizon)
        key = (symbol, steps)
        with self._artifact_lock:
            artifact = self._artifacts.get(key)
        if artifact is None:
            if strict:
                raise EnsembleNotTrainedError(
                    f"No trained artifact for {symbol} at {steps} days"
                )
            return EnsemblePrediction(ticker=symbol, horizon_days=steps)

        try:
            frame = self._prepare_prediction_features(features, artifact)
            predictions, _ = self._model_predictions(artifact, frame)
            if not predictions:
                raise EnsembleError("All model predictions failed")
            available_weight = sum(
                artifact.weights.get(name, 0.0) for name in predictions
            )
            if available_weight <= 0:
                effective_weights = {
                    name: 1.0 / len(predictions) for name in predictions
                }
            else:
                effective_weights = {
                    name: artifact.weights.get(name, 0.0) / available_weight
                    for name in predictions
                }
            ensemble = sum(
                effective_weights[name] * prediction
                for name, prediction in predictions.items()
            )
            prediction_values = np.array(list(predictions.values()), dtype=float)
            weight_values = np.array(
                [effective_weights[name] for name in predictions],
                dtype=float,
            )
            disagreement = float(
                math.sqrt(
                    np.sum(
                        weight_values
                        * (prediction_values - ensemble) ** 2
                    )
                )
            )
            threshold = max(
                self.config.direction_threshold,
                self.config.noise_threshold_multiplier
                * artifact.validation_rmse,
            )
            direction = (
                "up"
                if ensemble > threshold
                else "down"
                if ensemble < -threshold
                else "neutral"
            )
            confidence = self._confidence(
                artifact,
                predictions,
                effective_weights,
                disagreement,
                stale_features=self._is_stale_input(features),
            )
            result = EnsemblePrediction(
                ticker=symbol,
                horizon_days=steps,
                xgboost_pred=predictions.get("xgboost"),
                rf_pred=predictions.get("rf"),
                lstm_pred=predictions.get("lstm"),
                rl_pred=predictions.get("rl"),
                ensemble_pred=float(ensemble),
                confidence=float(confidence),
                model_weights=dict(effective_weights),
                direction=direction,
            )
            diagnostics = PredictionDiagnostics(
                ticker=symbol,
                horizon_days=steps,
                as_of=frame.index[-1].isoformat(),
                trained_through=artifact.trained_through.isoformat(),
                available_models=tuple(predictions),
                raw_predictions=MappingProxyType(dict(predictions)),
                effective_weights=MappingProxyType(dict(effective_weights)),
                ensemble_prediction=float(ensemble),
                disagreement=disagreement,
                validation_rmse=artifact.validation_rmse,
                zero_baseline_rmse=artifact.baseline_rmse,
                direction_threshold=threshold,
                confidence=confidence,
            )
            with self._artifact_lock:
                self._last_prediction_diagnostics[key] = diagnostics
            return result
        except Exception:
            if strict:
                raise
            LOGGER.exception(
                "Ensemble prediction failed for %s %dd",
                symbol,
                steps,
            )
            return EnsemblePrediction(ticker=symbol, horizon_days=steps)

    def format_report(self, prediction: EnsemblePrediction) -> str:
        """Format a prediction and label confidence as a quality score."""

        model_predictions = (
            ("XGBoost", "xgboost", prediction.xgboost_pred),
            ("Random Forest", "rf", prediction.rf_pred),
            ("LSTM", "lstm", prediction.lstm_pred),
            ("Regularized linear", "rl", prediction.rl_pred),
        )
        lines = [
            "=" * 64,
            f"ENSEMBLE PREDICTION - {prediction.ticker}",
            "=" * 64,
            f"Horizon              : {prediction.horizon_days} days",
            f"Expected return       : {prediction.ensemble_pred:+.3%}",
            f"Direction             : {prediction.direction.upper()}",
            f"Model-quality score   : {prediction.confidence:.1%}",
            "",
            "Model contributions:",
        ]
        for label, key, value in model_predictions:
            if value is not None:
                weight = prediction.model_weights.get(key, 0.0)
                lines.append(
                    f"  {label:<20} {value:+.3%}  weight={weight:.1%}"
                )
        lines.extend(
            [
                "",
                "Note: the quality score is not a probability of profit.",
                "=" * 64,
            ]
        )
        return "\n".join(lines)

# This distribution is a single-file build. Core names are defined above.


LOGGER = logging.getLogger(f"{APP_NAME}.strategy")
RISK_LEVELS: Final = ("low", "medium", "high")
REGIMES: Final = (
    "bull",
    "bear",
    "sideways",
    "high_volatility",
    "crisis",
)
_EPSILON: Final = 1e-12

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Exceptions and diagnostics
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


class StrategyEngineError(SmartInvestError):
    """Base exception for Strategy Engine failures."""


class StrategyInputError(StrategyEngineError, ValueError):
    """Raised when regime, predictions or profile values are invalid."""


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PredictionSummary:
    usable_predictions: int
    excluded_predictions: int
    reference_horizon_days: int
    expected_return: float
    weighted_up_fraction: float
    weighted_down_fraction: float
    mean_quality: float
    disagreement: float
    effective_sample_size: float

    @property
    def signal_strength(self) -> float:
        return min(
            1.0,
            abs(self.expected_return)
            / max(0.01 * math.sqrt(self.reference_horizon_days / 20.0), 1e-6),
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ExposureSummary:
    equity: float
    defensive: float
    cash: float
    leveraged: float
    inverse: float
    largest_sleeve: float
    concentration_hhi: float
    effective_sleeves: float


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class StrategyEvaluation:
    strategy_name: str
    eligible: bool
    score: float
    regime_component: float
    profile_component: float
    signal_component: float
    diversification_component: float
    evidence_component: float
    exposure: ExposureSummary
    constraint_violations: tuple[str, ...] = ()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Validated configuration and profile
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _strategy_finite(name: str, value: float) -> float:
    number = float(value)
    if not math.isfinite(number):
        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be finite")
    return number


def _unit(name: str, value: float) -> float:
    number = _strategy_finite(name, value)
    if not 0.0 <= number <= 1.0:
        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be between 0 and 1")
    return number


def _strategy_positive_integer(name: str, value: int) -> int:
    if isinstance(value, bool) or int(value) != value or value <= 0:
        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be a positive integer")
    return int(value)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class InvestorProfile:
    """Minimum profile needed for allocation constraints.

    A real suitability process normally requires more information.  This
    object intentionally exposes that limitation instead of inferring a
    complete investor profile from a single risk-tolerance label.
    """

    risk_tolerance: str = "medium"
    time_horizon_years: float = 10.0
    minimum_cash_weight: float = 0.05
    maximum_equity_weight: float | None = None
    maximum_single_sleeve: float = 0.55
    allow_leveraged_products: bool = False
    allow_inverse_products: bool = False
    forbidden_sleeves: frozenset[str] = frozenset()

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        risk = str(self.risk_tolerance).strip().lower()
        if risk not in RISK_LEVELS:
            raise ValueError("risk_tolerance must be low, medium or high")
        object.__setattr__(self, "risk_tolerance", risk)
        horizon = _strategy_finite("time_horizon_years", self.time_horizon_years)
        if horizon <= 0 or horizon > 100:
            raise ValueError("time_horizon_years must be in (0, 100]")
        object.__setattr__(self, "time_horizon_years", horizon)
        _unit("minimum_cash_weight", self.minimum_cash_weight)
        _unit("maximum_single_sleeve", self.maximum_single_sleeve)
        if self.maximum_single_sleeve < 0.25:
            raise ValueError(
                "maximum_single_sleeve must be at least 0.25 for the "
                "four-sleeve dynamic allocation"
            )
        if self.minimum_cash_weight > self.maximum_single_sleeve:
            raise ValueError(
                "minimum_cash_weight cannot exceed maximum_single_sleeve"
            )
        if self.maximum_equity_weight is not None:
            _unit("maximum_equity_weight", self.maximum_equity_weight)
        forbidden = frozenset(
            str(name).strip().lower()
            for name in self.forbidden_sleeves
            if str(name).strip()
        )
        object.__setattr__(self, "forbidden_sleeves", forbidden)

    @property
    def effective_equity_cap(self) -> float:
        if self.maximum_equity_weight is not None:
            explicit = self.maximum_equity_weight
        else:
            explicit = {"low": 0.45, "medium": 0.70, "high": 0.90}[
                self.risk_tolerance
            ]
        horizon_cap = (
            0.30
            if self.time_horizon_years < 2
            else 0.45
            if self.time_horizon_years < 5
            else 1.0
        )
        return min(explicit, horizon_cap)

    @property
    def effective_cash_floor(self) -> float:
        horizon_floor = (
            0.25
            if self.time_horizon_years < 2
            else 0.12
            if self.time_horizon_years < 5
            else 0.0
        )
        return max(self.minimum_cash_weight, horizon_floor)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class StrategyEngineConfig:
    prediction_reference_horizon_days: int = 20
    minimum_prediction_quality: float = 0.10
    maximum_abs_daily_log_signal: float = 0.03
    maximum_dynamic_equity_tilt: float = 0.08
    prediction_disagreement_penalty: float = 0.30

    regime_score_weight: float = 35.0
    profile_score_weight: float = 25.0
    signal_score_weight: float = 15.0
    diversification_score_weight: float = 15.0
    evidence_score_weight: float = 10.0

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
            _strategy_positive_integer(
            "prediction_reference_horizon_days",
            self.prediction_reference_horizon_days,
        )
        _unit("minimum_prediction_quality", self.minimum_prediction_quality)
        for name in (
            "maximum_abs_daily_log_signal",
            "maximum_dynamic_equity_tilt",
        ):
            if _strategy_finite(name, getattr(self, name)) <= 0:
                raise ValueError(f"{name} must be greater than zero")
        _unit(
            "prediction_disagreement_penalty",
            self.prediction_disagreement_penalty,
        )
        weights = (
            self.regime_score_weight,
            self.profile_score_weight,
            self.signal_score_weight,
            self.diversification_score_weight,
            self.evidence_score_weight,
        )
        if any(_strategy_finite("score weight", weight) < 0 for weight in weights):
            raise ValueError("score weights cannot be negative")
        if not math.isclose(sum(weights), 100.0, abs_tol=1e-9):
            raise ValueError("strategy score weights must sum to 100")


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class _Template:
    name: str
    strategy_type: str
    regime_target: str
    allocations: Mapping[str, float]
    expected_return: str
    expected_volatility: str
    description: str


def _template(
    name: str,
    strategy_type: str,
    regime_target: str,
    allocations: Mapping[str, float],
    expected_return: str,
    expected_volatility: str,
    description: str,
) -> _Template:
    return _Template(
        name=name,
        strategy_type=strategy_type,
        regime_target=regime_target,
        allocations=MappingProxyType(dict(allocations)),
        expected_return=expected_return,
        expected_volatility=expected_volatility,
        description=description,
    )

        "value",

            "dividend_stocks": 0.25,
