    ) -> pd.DataFrame | pd.Series:
        """Rolling sample volatility without silently inventing data by default.

        Set ``fallback_volatility`` explicitly when a downstream rule requires
        complete early-window values. The fallback is on the daily scale.
        """

        if not isinstance(returns, (pd.Series, pd.DataFrame)):
            raise RiskAnalysisError("returns must be a Series or DataFrame")
        if not 2 <= int(window) <= 10_000:
            raise ValueError("window must be in [2, 10000]")
        min_periods = minimum_periods if minimum_periods is not None else window
        if not 2 <= int(min_periods) <= int(window):
            raise ValueError("minimum_periods must be in [2, window]")

        numeric = returns.apply(pd.to_numeric, errors="coerce") if isinstance(
            returns, pd.DataFrame
        ) else pd.to_numeric(returns, errors="coerce")
        vol = numeric.rolling(window=window, min_periods=min_periods).std(ddof=1)
        if fallback_volatility is not None:
        fallback = _risk_finite_float(fallback_volatility, "fallback_volatility")
            if fallback < 0:
                raise ValueError("fallback_volatility cannot be negative")
            vol = vol.fillna(fallback)
        if annualize:
            vol = vol * math.sqrt(self.config.periods_per_year)
        return vol

    def _sortino(
        self, returns: pd.Series, risk_free_rate: Optional[float] = None
    ) -> tuple[float, float]:
        """Annualized Sortino using lower partial deviation over all periods."""

        clean = self._validate_return_series(returns)
        annual_rf = (
            self.config.risk_free_rate
            if risk_free_rate is None
            else _risk_finite_float(risk_free_rate, "risk_free_rate")
        )
        daily_rf = math.expm1(
            math.log1p(annual_rf) / self.config.periods_per_year
        ) if annual_rf > -1.0 else annual_rf / self.config.periods_per_year
        excess = clean - daily_rf
        downside = np.minimum(excess.to_numpy(dtype=float), 0.0)
        downside_daily = math.sqrt(float(np.mean(np.square(downside))))
        downside_annual = downside_daily * math.sqrt(self.config.periods_per_year)
        annual_excess = float(excess.mean()) * self.config.periods_per_year
        if downside_annual <= _EPSILON:
            ratio = math.inf if annual_excess > 0 else 0.0
        else:
            ratio = annual_excess / downside_annual
        return ratio, downside_annual

    def sortino(
        self, returns: pd.Series, risk_free_rate: Optional[float] = None
    ) -> float:
        return self._sortino(returns, risk_free_rate)[0]

    @staticmethod
    def _classify_risk(
        var_99: float,
        volatility: float,
        max_drawdown: float,
        beta: Optional[float],
    ) -> str:
        score = 0
        loss_99 = max(0.0, -var_99)
        score += 2 if loss_99 > 0.04 else 1 if loss_99 > 0.02 else 0
        score += 2 if volatility > 0.40 else 1 if volatility > 0.25 else 0
        score += 2 if max_drawdown < -0.40 else 1 if max_drawdown < -0.25 else 0
        if beta is not None and math.isfinite(beta):
            absolute_beta = abs(beta)
            score += 2 if absolute_beta > 1.5 else 1 if absolute_beta > 1.2 else 0
        levels = ("low", "medium", "medium-high", "high", "critical")
        return levels[min(score, len(levels) - 1)]

    @staticmethod
    def _identify_risk_sources(
        ticker: str,
        var_99: float,
        volatility: float,
        max_drawdown: float,
        beta: Optional[float],
        serial_risk: float,
        observations: int,
    ) -> list[str]:
        sources: list[str] = []
        if -var_99 > 0.03:
            sources.append(f"High historical 99% one-day loss quantile ({-var_99:.2%})")
        if volatility > 0.30:
            sources.append(f"Elevated annualized volatility ({volatility:.1%})")
        if max_drawdown < -0.30:
            sources.append(f"Large observed drawdown ({max_drawdown:.1%})")
        if beta is not None and abs(beta) > 1.3:
            sources.append(f"High absolute market beta ({beta:.2f})")
        if serial_risk > 0.15:
            sources.append(
                f"Elevated return serial dependence ({serial_risk:.2f})"
            )
        if observations < 250:
            sources.append("Short history makes tail estimates less reliable")
        if any(token in ticker for token in ("BTC", "ETH", "CRYPTO")):
            sources.append("Digital asset may carry material liquidity and gap risk")
        if not sources:
            sources.append("No threshold breach in the configured historical metrics")
        return sources

    def portfolio_risk(
        self,
        price_data: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame],
        weights: Mapping[str, float],
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Return a backward-compatible portfolio risk dictionary."""

        detailed = self.portfolio_risk_detailed(price_data, weights)
        return {
            "portfolio_return_annual": round(detailed.annualized_return, 6),
            "portfolio_volatility": round(detailed.annualized_volatility, 6),
            "portfolio_var_95": round(detailed.var_95, 6),
            "portfolio_var_99": round(detailed.var_99, 6),
            "portfolio_cvar_95": round(detailed.cvar_95, 6),
            "max_drawdown": round(detailed.max_drawdown, 6),
            "correlation_matrix": detailed.correlation_matrix.round(4),
            "diversification_ratio": round(detailed.diversification_ratio, 4),
            "effective_number_of_positions": round(
                detailed.effective_number_of_positions, 3
            ),
            "component_volatility_contributions": dict(
                detailed.component_volatility_contributions
            ),
            "warnings": list(detailed.warnings),
        }

    def portfolio_risk_detailed(
        self,
        price_data: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame],
        weights: Mapping[str, float],
    ) -> PortfolioRiskReport:
        if not isinstance(price_data, Mapping) or not price_data:
            raise RiskAnalysisError("price_data must be a non-empty mapping")
        clean_weights = self._validate_weights(weights)
        normalized_prices: dict[str, pd.DataFrame] = {}
        for raw_ticker, frame in price_data.items():
            ticker = str(raw_ticker).strip().upper()
            if not ticker:
                raise RiskAnalysisError("price_data ticker cannot be empty")
            if ticker in normalized_prices:
                raise RiskAnalysisError(
                    f"duplicate price_data ticker after normalization: {ticker}"
                )
            normalized_prices[ticker] = frame

        missing = sorted(set(clean_weights) - set(normalized_prices))
        if missing:
            raise RiskAnalysisError(
                "Missing price data for weighted assets: " + ", ".join(missing)
            )

        series: list[pd.Series] = []
        warnings: list[str] = []
        for ticker in clean_weights:
            _, returns, quality = self._prepare_prices(
                normalized_prices[ticker], ticker
            )
            series.append(returns.rename(ticker))
            warnings.extend(f"{ticker}: {item}" for item in quality.warnings)

        matrix = pd.concat(series, axis=1, join="inner").dropna()
        if len(matrix) < self.config.minimum_observations:
            raise RiskAnalysisError(
                "Too few overlapping return observations across portfolio assets: "
                f"{len(matrix)}"
            )
        tickers = tuple(clean_weights)
        weight_vector = np.array([clean_weights[t] for t in tickers], dtype=float)
        returns_values = matrix.loc[:, tickers].to_numpy(dtype=float)
        portfolio_returns = pd.Series(
            returns_values @ weight_vector,
            index=matrix.index,
            name="portfolio",
        )

        sample_cov = matrix.loc[:, tickers].cov().to_numpy(dtype=float)
        diagonal = np.diag(np.diag(sample_cov))
        shrink = self.config.covariance_shrinkage
        covariance = (1.0 - shrink) * sample_cov + shrink * diagonal
        variance = float(weight_vector @ covariance @ weight_vector)
        portfolio_daily_vol = math.sqrt(max(variance, 0.0))
        annual_vol = portfolio_daily_vol * math.sqrt(
            self.config.periods_per_year
        )

        individual_vol = np.sqrt(np.maximum(np.diag(covariance), 0.0))
        weighted_standalone = float(np.abs(weight_vector) @ individual_vol)
        diversification_ratio = (
            weighted_standalone / portfolio_daily_vol
            if portfolio_daily_vol > _EPSILON
            else float("nan")
        )
        marginal = (
            covariance @ weight_vector / portfolio_daily_vol
            if portfolio_daily_vol > _EPSILON
            else np.zeros_like(weight_vector)
        )
        components = weight_vector * marginal
        contribution_sum = float(components.sum())
        if abs(contribution_sum) > _EPSILON:
            component_shares = components / contribution_sum
        else:
            component_shares = np.zeros_like(components)

        nav = (1.0 + portfolio_returns).cumprod()
        if bool((1.0 + portfolio_returns <= 0).any()):
            warnings.append(
                "At least one modeled daily portfolio return is <= -100%; "
                "compounded NAV and drawdown are not economically meaningful."
            )
        max_drawdown = self._max_drawdown(nav.clip(lower=_EPSILON))
        var_95 = self._historical_var(
            portfolio_returns, self.config.confidence_95
        )
        var_99 = self._historical_var(
            portfolio_returns, self.config.confidence_99
        )
        cvar_95 = self._expected_shortfall(
            portfolio_returns, self.config.confidence_95, var_95
        )
        annual_return = float(
            portfolio_returns.mean() * self.config.periods_per_year
        )

        correlation = matrix.loc[:, tickers].corr()
        if len(tickers) > 1:
            off_diagonal = correlation.to_numpy()[np.triu_indices(len(tickers), 1)]
            largest_correlation = float(np.nanmax(off_diagonal))
        else:
            largest_correlation = 1.0
            warnings.append("A one-asset portfolio has no diversification benefit.")

        absolute_weights = np.abs(weight_vector)
        normalized_absolute = absolute_weights / max(
            float(absolute_weights.sum()), _EPSILON
        )
        hhi = float(np.square(normalized_absolute).sum())
        effective_positions = 1.0 / hhi if hhi > _EPSILON else 0.0
        if largest_correlation > 0.85 and len(tickers) > 1:
            warnings.append(
                f"High pairwise correlation detected ({largest_correlation:.2f})."
            )
        warnings.append(
            "Portfolio results assume constant weights and ignore trading costs, "
            "liquidity, taxes, and rebalancing."
        )

        return PortfolioRiskReport(
            tickers=tickers,
            weights=MappingProxyType(dict(clean_weights)),
            observations=len(matrix),
            annualized_return=annual_return,
            annualized_volatility=annual_vol,
            var_95=var_95,
            var_99=var_99,
            cvar_95=cvar_95,
            max_drawdown=max_drawdown,
            diversification_ratio=diversification_ratio,
            effective_number_of_positions=effective_positions,
            largest_correlation=largest_correlation,
            component_volatility_contributions=MappingProxyType(
                {
                    ticker: float(component_shares[index])
                    for index, ticker in enumerate(tickers)
                }
            ),
            correlation_matrix=correlation,
            warnings=tuple(dict.fromkeys(warnings)),
        )

    def _validate_weights(
        self, weights: Mapping[str, float]
    ) -> dict[str, float]:
        if not isinstance(weights, Mapping) or not weights:
            raise RiskAnalysisError("weights must be a non-empty mapping")
        clean: dict[str, float] = {}
        for raw_ticker, raw_weight in weights.items():
            ticker = str(raw_ticker).strip().upper()
            if not ticker:
                raise RiskAnalysisError("weight ticker cannot be empty")
            weight = _risk_finite_float(raw_weight, f"weight[{ticker}]")
            if not self.config.allow_short_positions and weight < 0:
                raise RiskAnalysisError(
                    f"Negative weight for {ticker}; short positions are disabled"
                )
            if abs(weight) > self.config.weight_tolerance:
                clean[ticker] = weight
        if not clean:
            raise RiskAnalysisError("all supplied weights are zero")
        net = sum(clean.values())
        gross = sum(abs(value) for value in clean.values())
        if abs(net - 1.0) > self.config.weight_tolerance:
            raise RiskAnalysisError(
                f"net portfolio weights must sum to 1.0; received {net:.8f}"
            )
        if gross > self.config.maximum_gross_exposure + self.config.weight_tolerance:
            raise RiskAnalysisError(
                f"gross exposure {gross:.4f} exceeds configured maximum "
                f"{self.config.maximum_gross_exposure:.4f}"
            )
        return clean

    def format_report(
        self, report: RiskReport | AssetRiskAnalysis
    ) -> str:
        analysis = report if isinstance(report, AssetRiskAnalysis) else None
        summary = analysis.report if analysis is not None else report
        beta_text = (
            f"{summary.beta:.3f}"
            if math.isfinite(float(summary.beta))
            else "N/A"
        )
        lines = [
            "-" * 64,
            f"RISK INTELLIGENCE - {summary.ticker}",
            "-" * 64,
            f"Risk level              : {summary.risk_level.upper()}",
            f"Historical VaR 95%      : {summary.var_95:.3%}",
            f"Historical VaR 99%      : {summary.var_99:.3%}",
            f"Expected shortfall 95%  : {summary.cvar_95:.3%}",
            f"Maximum drawdown        : {summary.max_drawdown:.2%}",
            f"Annualized volatility   : {summary.volatility_annual:.2%}",
            f"Sortino ratio           : {summary.sortino_ratio:.3f}",
            f"Market beta             : {beta_text}",
            f"Serial-dependence risk  : {summary.correlation_risk:.3f}",
            "",
            "Main risk indicators:",
        ]
        lines.extend(f"  - {source}" for source in summary.main_risk_sources)
        if analysis is not None:
            diagnostics = analysis.diagnostics
            lines.extend(
                [
                    "",
                    "Diagnostics:",
                    f"  Observations           : {diagnostics.data_quality.observations}",
                    f"  EWMA volatility        : {diagnostics.ewma_volatility_forecast:.2%}",
                    f"  Negative-return share  : {diagnostics.probability_of_negative_return:.1%}",
                    f"  Worst daily return     : {diagnostics.worst_daily_return:.2%}",
                    "",
                    "Limitations:",
                ]
            )
            lines.extend(f"  - {item}" for item in diagnostics.limitations)
        lines.append("-" * 64)
        return "\n".join(lines)


__all__ = [
    "AssetRiskAnalysis",
    "AssetRiskDiagnostics",
    "DataQuality",
    "PortfolioRiskReport",
    "RiskAnalysisError",
    "RiskConfig",
    "RiskIntelligence",
]





# MODULE 8





import logging
import math
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from types import MappingProxyType
from typing import Any, Final, Mapping, Optional

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

# This distribution is a single-file build. Core names are defined above.


LOGGER = logging.getLogger(f"{APP_NAME}.xai")
_EPSILON: Final = 1e-12
_ACTIONS: Final = frozenset({"increase", "decrease", "hold", "buy", "sell"})
_RISK_LEVEL_SCORE: Final = MappingProxyType(
    {
        "low": 0.10,
        "medium": 0.32,
        "medium-high": 0.55,
        "high": 0.78,
        "critical": 0.95,
    }
)
_MODEL_FIELDS: Final = (
    ("xgboost", "xgboost_pred"),
    ("random_forest", "rf_pred"),
    ("lstm", "lstm_pred"),
    ("rl", "rl_pred"),
)


class ExplanationError(SmartInvestError):
    """Raised when an explanation would be invalid or misleading."""


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class XAIConfig:
    """Decision and presentation policy for rule-based explanations."""

    minimum_model_quality: float = 0.45
    action_model_quality: float = 0.55
    positive_return_threshold: float = 0.005
    negative_return_threshold: float = -0.005
    maximum_auto_risk: float = 0.60
    forced_reduction_risk: float = 0.75
    strong_signal_threshold: float = 0.30
    maximum_absolute_prediction: float = 1.0
    require_ticker_match: bool = True
    maximum_allocation_pct: float = 100.0

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        for name in (
            "minimum_model_quality",
            "action_model_quality",
            "maximum_auto_risk",
            "forced_reduction_risk",
            "strong_signal_threshold",
        ):
            value = float(getattr(self, name))
            if not math.isfinite(value) or not 0.0 <= value <= 1.0:
                raise ValueError(f"{name} must be finite and in [0, 1]")
        if self.action_model_quality < self.minimum_model_quality:
            raise ValueError(
                "action_model_quality cannot be below minimum_model_quality"
            )
        if self.forced_reduction_risk < self.maximum_auto_risk:
            raise ValueError(
                "forced_reduction_risk cannot be below maximum_auto_risk"
            )
        if not math.isfinite(float(self.positive_return_threshold)):
            raise ValueError("positive_return_threshold must be finite")
        if not math.isfinite(float(self.negative_return_threshold)):
            raise ValueError("negative_return_threshold must be finite")
        if self.negative_return_threshold >= self.positive_return_threshold:
            raise ValueError(
                "negative_return_threshold must be below positive_return_threshold"
            )
        if float(self.maximum_absolute_prediction) <= 0:
            raise ValueError("maximum_absolute_prediction must be positive")
        if not 0 < float(self.maximum_allocation_pct) <= 100:
            raise ValueError("maximum_allocation_pct must be in (0, 100]")


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class EvidenceItem:
    """One traceable input supporting or constraining the explanation."""

    category: str
    signal: str
    score: float
    source: str
    text: str


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class XAIDiagnostics:
    prediction_return: float
    horizon_days: int
    model_quality: float
    confidence_is_probability: bool
    model_predictions: Mapping[str, float]
    model_agreement: Optional[float]
    evidence_coverage: float
    action_was_automatic: bool
    direction_consistent: bool
    feature_timestamp: Optional[str]
    evidence: tuple[EvidenceItem, ...]
    missing_evidence: tuple[str, ...]
    warnings: tuple[str, ...]
    limitations: tuple[str, ...]
    feature_importance: Mapping[str, float] = field(
        default_factory=lambda: MappingProxyType({})
    )


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class XAIAnalysis:
    explanation: XAIExplanation
    diagnostics: XAIDiagnostics


def _xai_finite(value: Any, name: str) -> float:
    try:
        result = float(value)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise ExplanationError(f"{name} must be numeric") from exc
    if not math.isfinite(result):
        raise ExplanationError(f"{name} must be finite")
    return result


def _clip_score(value: float) -> float:
    return float(np.clip(value, -1.0, 1.0))


class ExplainableAI:
    """Create conservative, auditable explanations for model recommendations."""

    def __init__(self, config: Optional[XAIConfig] = None) -> None:
        self.config = config or XAIConfig()

    def explain(
        self,
        ticker: str,
        prediction: EnsemblePrediction,
        risk_report: RiskReport,
        features: pd.DataFrame,
        action: str = "auto",
        allocation_pct: float = 0.0,
        *,
        sentiment_score: Optional[float] = None,
        feature_importance: Optional[Mapping[str, float]] = None,
    ) -> XAIExplanation:
        """Return the Module-1-compatible explanation."""

        return self.explain_detailed(
            ticker=ticker,
            prediction=prediction,
            risk_report=risk_report,
            features=features,
            action=action,
            allocation_pct=allocation_pct,
            sentiment_score=sentiment_score,
            feature_importance=feature_importance,
        ).explanation

    def explain_detailed(
        self,
        ticker: str,
        prediction: EnsemblePrediction,
        risk_report: RiskReport,
        features: pd.DataFrame,
        action: str = "auto",
        allocation_pct: float = 0.0,
        *,
        sentiment_score: Optional[float] = None,
        feature_importance: Optional[Mapping[str, float]] = None,
    ) -> XAIAnalysis:
        symbol = self._validate_inputs(
            ticker, prediction, risk_report, features, allocation_pct
        )
        latest, feature_timestamp, feature_warnings = self._latest_features(features)
        prediction_return = _xai_finite(
            prediction.ensemble_pred, "prediction.ensemble_pred"
        )
        if abs(prediction_return) > self.config.maximum_absolute_prediction:
            raise ExplanationError(
                "prediction.ensemble_pred exceeds the configured plausible range; "
                "verify that it is expressed as a decimal return"
            )
        model_quality = _xai_finite(prediction.confidence, "prediction.confidence")
        if not 0.0 <= model_quality <= 1.0:
            raise ExplanationError("prediction.confidence must be in [0, 1]")

        momentum, momentum_evidence, momentum_missing = self._momentum_evidence(
            latest
        )
        value, value_evidence, value_missing = self._value_evidence(latest)
        sentiment, sentiment_evidence, sentiment_missing = self._sentiment_evidence(
            latest, sentiment_score
        )
        risk_score, risk_evidence = self._risk_evidence(risk_report)
        model_predictions = self._model_predictions(prediction)
        agreement, agreement_evidence = self._model_agreement(
            model_predictions, prediction_return
        )
        importance = self._validate_feature_importance(feature_importance)

        stated_direction = str(prediction.direction).strip().lower()
        derived_direction = self._return_direction(prediction_return)
        direction_consistent = stated_direction in {derived_direction, "neutral"} or (
            derived_direction == "neutral" and stated_direction in {"up", "down"}
        )
        warnings = list(feature_warnings)
        if not direction_consistent:
            warnings.append(
                f"Prediction direction '{stated_direction}' conflicts with the "
                f"return-derived direction '{derived_direction}'."
            )
        if model_quality < self.config.minimum_model_quality:
            warnings.append(
                "Model-quality score is below the explanation reliability threshold."
            )

        automatic = str(action).strip().lower() == "auto"
        selected_action = (
            self._determine_action_from_scores(
                prediction_return, model_quality, risk_score, momentum
            )
            if automatic
            else self._validate_action(action)
        )
        evidence = (
            list(momentum_evidence)
            + list(value_evidence)
            + list(sentiment_evidence)
            + list(risk_evidence)
            + list(agreement_evidence)
        )
        missing = tuple(
            dict.fromkeys(momentum_missing + value_missing + sentiment_missing)
        )
        available_categories = {
            item.category for item in evidence if item.category != "risk"
        }
        expected_categories = {"momentum", "value", "sentiment", "model_agreement"}
        evidence_coverage = (
            len(available_categories.intersection(expected_categories))
            / len(expected_categories)
        )
        explanation_quality = self._explanation_quality(
            model_quality=model_quality,
            agreement=agreement,
            evidence_coverage=evidence_coverage,
            has_risk=True,
            warning_count=len(warnings),
        )
        reasons = self._render_reasons(
            prediction=prediction,
            prediction_return=prediction_return,
            model_quality=model_quality,
            action=selected_action,
            evidence=evidence,
            missing=missing,
            importance=importance,
        )

        explanation = XAIExplanation(
            ticker=symbol,
            action=selected_action,
            allocation_pct=_xai_finite(allocation_pct, "allocation_pct"),
            reasons=reasons,
            sentiment_score=round(sentiment, 4),
            momentum_score=round(momentum, 4),
            value_score=round(value, 4),
            risk_score=round(risk_score, 4),
            confidence=round(explanation_quality, 4),
        )
        diagnostics = XAIDiagnostics(
            prediction_return=prediction_return,
            horizon_days=int(prediction.horizon_days),
            model_quality=model_quality,
            confidence_is_probability=False,
            model_predictions=MappingProxyType(dict(model_predictions)),
            model_agreement=agreement,
            evidence_coverage=evidence_coverage,
            action_was_automatic=automatic,
            direction_consistent=direction_consistent,
            feature_timestamp=feature_timestamp,
            evidence=tuple(evidence),
            missing_evidence=missing,
            warnings=tuple(dict.fromkeys(warnings)),
            limitations=(
                "This is a rule-based post-hoc explanation, not a causal explanation.",
                "Model quality and explanation quality are not probabilities of profit.",
                "Feature importance, when supplied, describes the source model only "
                "and does not establish causality.",
                "The action does not account for suitability, holdings, taxes, "
                "liquidity, transaction costs, or portfolio-level constraints.",
            ),
            feature_importance=MappingProxyType(dict(importance)),
        )
        return XAIAnalysis(explanation=explanation, diagnostics=diagnostics)

    def _validate_inputs(
        self,
        ticker: str,
        prediction: EnsemblePrediction,
        risk_report: RiskReport,
        features: pd.DataFrame,
        allocation_pct: float,
    ) -> str:
        symbol = str(ticker).strip().upper()
        if not symbol:
            raise ExplanationError("ticker cannot be empty")
        if not isinstance(prediction, EnsemblePrediction):
            raise ExplanationError("prediction must be an EnsemblePrediction")
        if not isinstance(risk_report, RiskReport):
            raise ExplanationError("risk_report must be a RiskReport")
        if not isinstance(features, pd.DataFrame):
            raise ExplanationError("features must be a pandas DataFrame")
        if int(prediction.horizon_days) <= 0:
            raise ExplanationError("prediction.horizon_days must be positive")

        prediction_ticker = str(prediction.ticker).strip().upper()
        risk_ticker = str(risk_report.ticker).strip().upper()
        if self.config.require_ticker_match:
            mismatches = [
                label
                for label, value in (
                    ("prediction", prediction_ticker),
                    ("risk report", risk_ticker),
                )
                if value != symbol
            ]
            if mismatches:
                raise ExplanationError(
                    f"ticker mismatch for {', '.join(mismatches)}; expected {symbol}"
                )
        allocation = _xai_finite(allocation_pct, "allocation_pct")
        if not 0.0 <= allocation <= self.config.maximum_allocation_pct:
            raise ExplanationError(
                f"allocation_pct must be in [0, {self.config.maximum_allocation_pct}]"
            )
        return symbol

    @staticmethod
    def _latest_features(
        features: pd.DataFrame,
    ) -> tuple[pd.Series, Optional[str], tuple[str, ...]]:
        if features.empty:
            return pd.Series(dtype=float), None, ("Feature frame is empty.",)
        numeric = features.apply(pd.to_numeric, errors="coerce")
        finite_rows = numeric.replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan).dropna(how="all")
        if finite_rows.empty:
            return (
                pd.Series(dtype=float),
                None,
                ("Feature frame has no finite numeric evidence.",),
            )
        latest = finite_rows.iloc[-1]
        timestamp = (
            str(finite_rows.index[-1])
            if finite_rows.index[-1] is not None
            else None
        )
        warnings: list[str] = []
        if latest.isna().any():
            warnings.append("Some latest-row features are missing or non-finite.")
        return latest, timestamp, tuple(warnings)

    @staticmethod
    def _value(latest: pd.Series, name: str) -> Optional[float]:
        if name not in latest.index or pd.isna(latest[name]):
            return None
        value = float(latest[name])
        return value if math.isfinite(value) else None

    def _momentum_evidence(
        self, latest: pd.Series
    ) -> tuple[float, tuple[EvidenceItem, ...], list[str]]:
        components: list[tuple[float, float, EvidenceItem]] = []

        return_20d = self._value(latest, "returns_20d")
        if return_20d is not None:
            score = math.tanh(return_20d / 0.10)
            components.append(
                (
                    score,
                    0.35,
                    EvidenceItem(
                        "momentum",
                        "positive" if score > 0 else "negative" if score < 0 else "neutral",
                        score,
                        "returns_20d",
                        f"20-day trailing return is {return_20d:+.2%}.",
                    ),
                )
            )

        versus_sma = self._value(latest, "price_vs_sma_50")
        if versus_sma is not None:
            score = math.tanh(versus_sma / 0.10)
            components.append(
                (
                    score,
                    0.30,
                    EvidenceItem(
                        "momentum",
                        "positive" if score > 0 else "negative" if score < 0 else "neutral",
                        score,
                        "price_vs_sma_50",
                        f"Price is {versus_sma:+.2%} relative to its 50-day average.",
                    ),
                )
            )

        rsi = self._value(latest, "rsi_14")
        if rsi is not None:
            if not 0.0 <= rsi <= 100.0:
                raise ExplanationError("rsi_14 must be in [0, 100]")
            score = _clip_score((rsi - 50.0) / 30.0)
            components.append(
                (
                    score,
                    0.20,
                    EvidenceItem(
                        "momentum",
                        "positive" if score > 0 else "negative" if score < 0 else "neutral",
                        score,
                        "rsi_14",
                        f"RSI(14) is {rsi:.1f}; it is a momentum indicator, not valuation.",
                    ),
                )
            )

        macd_hist = self._value(latest, "macd_hist")
        if macd_hist is not None:
            score = 1.0 if macd_hist > 0 else -1.0 if macd_hist < 0 else 0.0
            components.append(
                (
                    score,
                    0.15,
                    EvidenceItem(
                        "momentum",
                        "positive" if score > 0 else "negative" if score < 0 else "neutral",
                        score,
                        "macd_hist",
                        f"MACD histogram is {'positive' if score > 0 else 'negative' if score < 0 else 'flat'}.",
                    ),
                )
            )

        if not components:
            return 0.0, (), ["momentum features"]
        total_weight = sum(item[1] for item in components)
        aggregate = sum(score * weight for score, weight, _ in components) / total_weight
        return _clip_score(aggregate), tuple(item[2] for item in components), []

    def _value_evidence(
        self, latest: pd.Series
    ) -> tuple[float, tuple[EvidenceItem, ...], list[str]]:
        """Use only explicit fundamental valuation evidence, never RSI."""

        components: list[tuple[float, EvidenceItem]] = []
        definitions = (
            ("earnings_yield", 0.08, 0.06, False, "earnings yield"),
            ("free_cash_flow_yield", 0.07, 0.06, False, "free-cash-flow yield"),
            ("book_to_market", 0.50, 0.50, False, "book-to-market"),
            ("pe_zscore", 0.0, 1.5, True, "P/E z-score"),
        )
        for name, center, scale, invert, label in definitions:
            raw = self._value(latest, name)
            if raw is None:
                continue
            score = math.tanh((raw - center) / scale)
            if invert:
                score = -score
            components.append(
                (
                    score,
                    EvidenceItem(
                        "value",
                        "attractive" if score > 0.2 else "expensive" if score < -0.2 else "neutral",
                        score,
                        name,
                        f"Latest {label} is {raw:.4g}.",
                    ),
                )
            )
        if not components:
            return 0.0, (), ["fundamental valuation features"]
        return (
            _clip_score(float(np.mean([item[0] for item in components]))),
            tuple(item[1] for item in components),
            [],
        )

    def _sentiment_evidence(
        self, latest: pd.Series, explicit_score: Optional[float]
    ) -> tuple[float, tuple[EvidenceItem, ...], list[str]]:
        source = "explicit sentiment_score"
        score_value = explicit_score
        if score_value is None:
            score_value = self._value(latest, "sentiment_score")
            source = "features.sentiment_score"
        if score_value is None:
            return 0.0, (), ["independent sentiment evidence"]
        score = _xai_finite(score_value, "sentiment_score")
        if not -1.0 <= score <= 1.0:
            raise ExplanationError("sentiment_score must be in [-1, 1]")
        item = EvidenceItem(
            category="sentiment",
            signal="positive" if score > 0.2 else "negative" if score < -0.2 else "neutral",
            score=score,
            source=source,
            text=f"Independent sentiment score is {score:+.2f}.",
        )
        return score, (item,), []

    def _risk_evidence(
        self, risk: RiskReport
    ) -> tuple[float, tuple[EvidenceItem, ...]]:
        level = str(risk.risk_level).strip().lower()
        base = _RISK_LEVEL_SCORE.get(level)
        if base is None:
            raise ExplanationError(f"unsupported risk level: {risk.risk_level!r}")
        volatility = max(0.0, _xai_finite(risk.volatility_annual, "volatility_annual"))
        drawdown = max(0.0, -_xai_finite(risk.max_drawdown, "max_drawdown"))
        tail_loss = max(0.0, -_xai_finite(risk.cvar_95, "cvar_95"))
        metric_score = float(
            np.mean(
                [
                    min(1.0, volatility / 0.60),
                    min(1.0, drawdown / 0.60),
                    min(1.0, tail_loss / 0.08),
                ]
            )
        )
        score = float(np.clip(0.65 * base + 0.35 * metric_score, 0.0, 1.0))
        evidence = (
            EvidenceItem(
                "risk",
                "elevated" if score >= 0.6 else "moderate" if score >= 0.3 else "contained",
                score,
                "RiskReport",
                f"Risk level is {level}; annual volatility is {volatility:.1%}, "
                f"maximum drawdown {risk.max_drawdown:.1%}, and historical "
                f"CVaR(95%) {risk.cvar_95:.2%}.",
            ),
        )
        return score, evidence

    @staticmethod
    def _model_predictions(
        prediction: EnsemblePrediction,
    ) -> dict[str, float]:
        values: dict[str, float] = {}
        for label, attribute in _MODEL_FIELDS:
            raw = getattr(prediction, attribute)
            if raw is None:
                continue
                values[label] = _xai_finite(raw, f"prediction.{attribute}")
        return values

    @staticmethod
    def _model_agreement(
        model_predictions: Mapping[str, float],
        ensemble_prediction: float,
    ) -> tuple[Optional[float], tuple[EvidenceItem, ...]]:
        if len(model_predictions) < 2:
            return None, ()
        reference_sign = (
            1 if ensemble_prediction > 0 else -1 if ensemble_prediction < 0 else 0
        )
        signs = np.sign(np.fromiter(model_predictions.values(), dtype=float))
        agreement = (
            float(np.mean(signs == reference_sign))
            if reference_sign
            else float(np.mean(signs == 0))
        )
        item = EvidenceItem(
            "model_agreement",
            "high" if agreement >= 0.75 else "mixed",
            2.0 * agreement - 1.0,
            "component model predictions",
            f"{agreement:.0%} of {len(model_predictions)} available component "
            "models agree with the ensemble direction.",
        )
        return agreement, (item,)

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_feature_importance(
        importance: Optional[Mapping[str, float]],
    ) -> dict[str, float]:
        if importance is None:
            return {}
        if not isinstance(importance, Mapping):
            raise ExplanationError("feature_importance must be a mapping")
        clean: dict[str, float] = {}
        for raw_name, raw_value in importance.items():
            name = str(raw_name).strip()
            if not name:
                raise ExplanationError("feature_importance name cannot be empty")
                value = _xai_finite(raw_value, f"feature_importance[{name}]")
            if value < 0:
                raise ExplanationError("feature importance cannot be negative")
            clean[name] = value
        total = sum(clean.values())
        if clean and total <= _EPSILON:
            raise ExplanationError("feature importance values sum to zero")
        return (
            {name: value / total for name, value in clean.items()}
            if clean
            else {}
        )

    def _return_direction(self, predicted_return: float) -> str:
        if predicted_return >= self.config.positive_return_threshold:
            return "up"
        if predicted_return <= self.config.negative_return_threshold:
            return "down"
        return "neutral"

    def _determine_action_from_scores(
        self,
        predicted_return: float,
        model_quality: float,
        risk_score: float,
        momentum_score: float,
    ) -> str:
        if risk_score >= self.config.forced_reduction_risk:
            return "decrease"
        if model_quality < self.config.action_model_quality:
            return "hold"
        if (
            predicted_return <= self.config.negative_return_threshold
            or (
                momentum_score <= -self.config.strong_signal_threshold
                and risk_score > self.config.maximum_auto_risk
            )
        ):
            return "decrease"
        if (
            predicted_return >= self.config.positive_return_threshold
            and risk_score <= self.config.maximum_auto_risk
            and momentum_score > -self.config.strong_signal_threshold
        ):
            return "increase"
        return "hold"

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_action(action: str) -> str:
        selected = str(action).strip().lower()
        if selected not in _ACTIONS:
            raise ExplanationError(
                f"unsupported action {action!r}; expected one of {sorted(_ACTIONS)}"
            )
        return selected

    @staticmethod
    def _explanation_quality(
        *,
        model_quality: float,
        agreement: Optional[float],
        evidence_coverage: float,
        has_risk: bool,
        warning_count: int,
    ) -> float:
        agreement_score = 0.5 if agreement is None else agreement
        score = (
            0.50 * model_quality
            + 0.20 * agreement_score
            + 0.20 * evidence_coverage
            + 0.10 * float(has_risk)
            - min(0.20, 0.04 * warning_count)
        )
        return float(np.clip(score, 0.0, 1.0))

    def _render_reasons(
        self,
        *,
        prediction: EnsemblePrediction,
        prediction_return: float,
        model_quality: float,
        action: str,
        evidence: list[EvidenceItem],
        missing: tuple[str, ...],
        importance: Mapping[str, float],
    ) -> list[str]:
        reasons = [
            f"Ensemble expected return is {prediction_return:+.2%} over "
            f"{prediction.horizon_days} days; model-quality score is "
            f"{model_quality:.0%} and is not a probability."
        ]
        relevant = sorted(evidence, key=lambda item: abs(item.score), reverse=True)
        reasons.extend(item.text for item in relevant[:5])
        if importance:
            leaders = sorted(importance.items(), key=lambda item: item[1], reverse=True)[:3]
            text = ", ".join(f"{name} ({weight:.0%})" for name, weight in leaders)
            reasons.append(
                f"Supplied model feature-importance leaders: {text}; importance "
                "does not imply causality or signal direction."
            )
        if missing:
            reasons.append("Unavailable evidence: " + ", ".join(missing) + ".")
        if action == "hold" and model_quality < self.config.action_model_quality:
            reasons.append(
                "Automatic action remains HOLD because model quality is below "
                "the configured action threshold."
            )
        elif action == "decrease":
            reasons.append(
                "The automatic policy favors risk reduction due to downside "
                "signal and/or elevated measured risk."
            )
        elif action == "increase":
            reasons.append(
                "The automatic policy permits an increase because expected return, "
                "model quality, momentum, and risk pass configured thresholds."
            )
        return reasons

    # Compatibility helpers retained with corrected semantics.
    def _compute_momentum(self, features: pd.DataFrame) -> float:
        latest, _, _ = self._latest_features(features)
        return self._momentum_evidence(latest)[0]

    def _compute_value(self, features: pd.DataFrame) -> float:
        latest, _, _ = self._latest_features(features)
        return self._value_evidence(latest)[0]

    @staticmethod
    def _infer_sentiment(
        ticker: str, prediction: EnsemblePrediction
    ) -> float:
        """Return neutral when no independent sentiment evidence is supplied."""

        del ticker, prediction
        return 0.0

    def _risk_to_score(self, risk: RiskReport) -> float:
        return self._risk_evidence(risk)[0]

    def _determine_action(
        self, prediction: EnsemblePrediction, risk: RiskReport
    ) -> str:
        return self._determine_action_from_scores(
            _xai_finite(prediction.ensemble_pred, "prediction.ensemble_pred"),
            _xai_finite(prediction.confidence, "prediction.confidence"),
            self._risk_to_score(risk),
            0.0,
        )

    def format_report(self, explanation: XAIExplanation | XAIAnalysis) -> str:
        analysis = explanation if isinstance(explanation, XAIAnalysis) else None
        summary = analysis.explanation if analysis is not None else explanation
        lines = [
            "-" * 68,
            f"EXPLAINABLE DECISION SUPPORT - {summary.ticker}",
            "-" * 68,
            f"Action                    : {summary.action.upper()}",
            f"Allocation target         : {summary.allocation_pct:.1f}%",
            f"Explanation-quality score : {summary.confidence:.1%}",
            "",
            "Score breakdown:",
            f"  Momentum                 : {summary.momentum_score:+.3f}",
            f"  Fundamental value        : {summary.value_score:+.3f}",
            f"  Independent sentiment    : {summary.sentiment_score:+.3f}",
            f"  Risk (lower is better)   : {summary.risk_score:.3f}",
            "",
            "Evidence and policy rationale:",
        ]
        lines.extend(f"  - {reason}" for reason in summary.reasons)
        if analysis is not None:
            diagnostics = analysis.diagnostics
            if diagnostics.warnings:
                lines.extend(["", "Warnings:"])
                lines.extend(f"  - {warning}" for warning in diagnostics.warnings)
            lines.extend(["", "Limitations:"])
            lines.extend(
                f"  - {limitation}" for limitation in diagnostics.limitations
            )
        lines.append("-" * 68)
        return "\n".join(lines)


__all__ = [
    "EvidenceItem",
    "ExplainableAI",
    "ExplanationError",
    "XAIAnalysis",
    "XAIConfig",
    "XAIDiagnostics",
]



# MODULE 9



import logging
import math
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from types import MappingProxyType
from typing import Any, Final, Mapping, Optional

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

# This distribution is a single-file build. Dependencies are defined above.


LOGGER = logging.getLogger(f"{APP_NAME}.backtesting")
_EPSILON: Final = 1e-12
_TRADING_DAYS: Final = 252
_FREQUENCIES: Final = frozenset(
    {"daily", "weekly", "monthly", "quarterly", "yearly", "none", "buy_and_hold"}
)

# Conceptual sleeves map only to representative liquid proxies. Missing sleeves
# remain in cash; they are never silently redirected to an unrelated asset.
DEFAULT_ASSET_MAPPING: Final = MappingProxyType(
    {
        "equities": ("SPY",),
        "broad_market": ("SPY",),
        "tech_etf": ("QQQ",),
        "growth_equities": ("QQQ", "VUG"),
        "high_momentum_ai": ("QQQ",),
        "leading_sector": ("XLK", "QQQ"),
        "undervalued_equities": ("VTV",),
        "dividend_stocks": ("VIG", "SCHD"),
        "dividend_aristocrats": ("NOBL", "VIG"),
        "reits": ("VNQ",),
        "energy": ("XLE",),
        "leveraged_etf": ("UPRO",),
        "inverse_etf": ("SH",),
        "bonds": ("AGG", "BND"),
        "long_bonds": ("TLT",),
        "tips": ("TIP",),
        "gold": ("GLD",),
        "commodities": ("DBC",),
        "cash": (),
    }
)


class BacktestError(SmartInvestError):
    """Base error for Module 9."""


class BacktestDataError(BacktestError):
    """Raised when market data cannot support a reliable backtest."""


class BacktestConfigurationError(BacktestError):
    """Raised when simulation settings are inconsistent or unsafe."""


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class BacktestConfig:
    minimum_observations: int = 60
    periods_per_year: int = _TRADING_DAYS
    risk_free_rate: float = 0.045
    maximum_forward_fill: int = 3
    weight_tolerance: float = 1e-6
    maximum_single_weight: float = 1.0
    minimum_stop_pct: float = 0.02
    maximum_stop_pct: float = 0.25
    maximum_transaction_cost_pct: float = 0.10

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if int(self.minimum_observations) < 20:
            raise ValueError("minimum_observations must be at least 20")
        if not 2 <= int(self.periods_per_year) <= 366:
            raise ValueError("periods_per_year must be in [2, 366]")
        if not -0.99 < float(self.risk_free_rate) <= 1.0:
            raise ValueError("risk_free_rate must be in (-0.99, 1]")
        if not 0 <= int(self.maximum_forward_fill) <= 10:
            raise ValueError("maximum_forward_fill must be in [0, 10]")
        if not 0 <= float(self.weight_tolerance) <= 0.01:
            raise ValueError("weight_tolerance must be in [0, 0.01]")
        if not 0 < float(self.maximum_single_weight) <= 1:
            raise ValueError("maximum_single_weight must be in (0, 1]")
        if not 0 < float(self.minimum_stop_pct) < float(self.maximum_stop_pct) < 1:
            raise ValueError(
                "stop bounds must satisfy 0 < minimum < maximum < 1"
            )
        if not 0 <= float(self.maximum_transaction_cost_pct) < 1:
            raise ValueError("maximum_transaction_cost_pct must be in [0, 1)")


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ExecutionEvent:
    date: str
    ticker: str
    event_type: str
    notional: float
    cost: float
    price: float
    units_delta: float
    reason: str


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class BacktestDiagnostics:
    target_weights: Mapping[str, float]
    cash_target_weight: float
    sleeve_mapping: Mapping[str, tuple[str, ...]]
    unmapped_sleeves: Mapping[str, float]
    initial_capital: float
    final_equity: float
    transaction_costs: float
    turnover_notional: float
    rebalance_count: int
    executed_orders: int
    observations: int
    elapsed_years: float
    exposure_history: pd.DataFrame = field(repr=False, compare=False)
    execution_events: tuple[ExecutionEvent, ...] = ()
    warnings: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    assumptions: tuple[str, ...] = ()


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class BacktestAnalysis:
    result: BacktestResult
    diagnostics: BacktestDiagnostics


@dataclass(slots=True)
class _SimulationState:
    units: np.ndarray
    cash: float
    peak_prices: np.ndarray
    active: np.ndarray
    transaction_costs: float = 0.0
    turnover_notional: float = 0.0
    rebalance_count: int = 0
    events: list[ExecutionEvent] = field(default_factory=list)
    stop_events: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)


def _backtest_finite(value: Any, name: str) -> float:
    try:
        result = float(value)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise BacktestConfigurationError(f"{name} must be numeric") from exc
    if not math.isfinite(result):
        raise BacktestConfigurationError(f"{name} must be finite")
    return result


class BacktestingLab:
    """Backtest a static allocation without silent substitutions."""

    DEFAULT_COMMISSION: float = 0.001
    DEFAULT_SLIPPAGE: float = 0.0005
    DEFAULT_USE_TRAILING_STOP: bool = True
    DEFAULT_SL_VOL_MULTIPLIER: float = 2.0
    DEFAULT_VOLATILITY_WINDOW: int = 20

    def __init__(
        self,
        risk_intelligence: Optional[RiskIntelligence] = None,
        config: Optional[BacktestConfig] = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.config = config or BacktestConfig()
        self.risk_intel = risk_intelligence or RiskIntelligence()

    def run(
        self,
        strategy: Strategy,
        price_data: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame],
        start_date: Optional[str] = None,
        end_date: Optional[str] = None,
        initial_capital: float = 100_000.0,
        rebalance_freq: str = "monthly",
        commission_pct: float = DEFAULT_COMMISSION,
        slippage_pct: float = DEFAULT_SLIPPAGE,
        use_trailing_stop: bool = DEFAULT_USE_TRAILING_STOP,
        sl_vol_multiplier: float = DEFAULT_SL_VOL_MULTIPLIER,
        volatility_window: int = DEFAULT_VOLATILITY_WINDOW,
        *,
        asset_mapping: Optional[Mapping[str, tuple[str, ...] | list[str] | str]] = None,
    ) -> BacktestResult:
        """Return the Module-1-compatible result."""

        return self.run_detailed(
            strategy=strategy,
            price_data=price_data,
            start_date=start_date,
            end_date=end_date,
            initial_capital=initial_capital,
            rebalance_freq=rebalance_freq,
            commission_pct=commission_pct,
            slippage_pct=slippage_pct,
            use_trailing_stop=use_trailing_stop,
            sl_vol_multiplier=sl_vol_multiplier,
            volatility_window=volatility_window,
            asset_mapping=asset_mapping,
        ).result

    def run_detailed(
        self,
        strategy: Strategy,
        price_data: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame],
        start_date: Optional[str] = None,
        end_date: Optional[str] = None,
        initial_capital: float = 100_000.0,
        rebalance_freq: str = "monthly",
        commission_pct: float = DEFAULT_COMMISSION,
        slippage_pct: float = DEFAULT_SLIPPAGE,
        use_trailing_stop: bool = DEFAULT_USE_TRAILING_STOP,
        sl_vol_multiplier: float = DEFAULT_SL_VOL_MULTIPLIER,
        volatility_window: int = DEFAULT_VOLATILITY_WINDOW,
        *,
        asset_mapping: Optional[Mapping[str, tuple[str, ...] | list[str] | str]] = None,
    ) -> BacktestAnalysis:
        if not isinstance(strategy, Strategy):
            raise BacktestConfigurationError("strategy must be a Strategy")
        capital = _backtest_finite(initial_capital, "initial_capital")
        if capital <= 0:
            raise BacktestConfigurationError("initial_capital must be positive")
        frequency = str(rebalance_freq).strip().lower()
        if frequency not in _FREQUENCIES:
            raise BacktestConfigurationError(
                f"unsupported rebalance_freq; expected one of {sorted(_FREQUENCIES)}"
            )
        commission = self._cost_rate(commission_pct, "commission_pct")
        slippage = self._cost_rate(slippage_pct, "slippage_pct")
        cost_rate = commission + slippage
        if cost_rate > self.config.maximum_transaction_cost_pct:
            raise BacktestConfigurationError(
                "combined commission and slippage exceed configured maximum"
            )
        multiplier = _backtest_finite(sl_vol_multiplier, "sl_vol_multiplier")
        if multiplier <= 0:
            raise BacktestConfigurationError("sl_vol_multiplier must be positive")
        window = int(volatility_window)
        if not 5 <= window <= 252:
            raise BacktestConfigurationError(
                "volatility_window must be in [5, 252]"
            )

        normalized_data = self._normalize_price_data(price_data)
        mapping = self._normalize_mapping(asset_mapping)
        target_weights, cash_weight, used_mapping, unmapped = (
            self._map_allocations_to_tickers_detailed(
                strategy.allocations, tuple(normalized_data), mapping
            )
        )
        if not target_weights:
            raise BacktestConfigurationError(
                "strategy maps entirely to cash or unavailable sleeves; "
                "provide applicable price data or an explicit asset_mapping"
            )
        closes, data_warnings = self._prepare_closes(
            normalized_data,
            tuple(target_weights),
            start_date,
            end_date,
        )
        volatility = self._lagged_volatility(closes, window)
        rebalance_flags = self._rebalance_flags(closes.index, frequency)
        equity, exposures, state = self._simulate_event_driven(
            closes=closes,
            target_weights=target_weights,
            initial_capital=capital,
            rebalance_flags=rebalance_flags,
            cost_rate=cost_rate,
            use_trailing_stop=bool(use_trailing_stop),
            sl_vol_multiplier=multiplier,
            lagged_volatility=volatility,
        )
        returns = equity.pct_change(fill_method=None)
        # The first execution cost is a real loss versus starting capital.
        returns.iloc[0] = float(equity.iloc[0] / capital - 1.0)
        returns = returns.dropna()
        if returns.empty:
            raise BacktestDataError("simulation produced no return observations")
        metrics = self._metrics(equity, returns, capital)
        month_end_equity = equity.resample("ME").last()
        monthly_returns = month_end_equity.pct_change(fill_method=None)
        if not monthly_returns.empty:
            monthly_returns.iloc[0] = float(
                month_end_equity.iloc[0] / capital - 1.0
            )
        monthly_returns = monthly_returns.dropna()
        win_rate = (
            float((monthly_returns > 0).mean())
            if not monthly_returns.empty
            else 0.0
        )
        period = (
            f"{closes.index[0].strftime('%Y-%m-%d')} to "
            f"{closes.index[-1].strftime('%Y-%m-%d')}"
        )
        result = BacktestResult(
            strategy_name=strategy.name,
            period=period,
            assets=list(target_weights),
            total_return=round(metrics["total_return"], 6),
            cagr=round(metrics["cagr"], 6),
            max_drawdown=round(metrics["max_drawdown"], 6),
            sharpe_ratio=round(metrics["sharpe"], 4),
            sortino_ratio=round(metrics["sortino"], 4),
            calmar_ratio=round(metrics["calmar"], 4),
            win_rate=round(win_rate, 4),
            trades=len(state.events),
            equity_curve=equity,
            monthly_returns=monthly_returns,
            stop_loss_enabled=bool(use_trailing_stop),
            stop_loss_triggers=len(state.stop_events),
            stop_loss_events=state.stop_events,
        )
        warnings = list(data_warnings)
        if unmapped:
            warnings.append(
                "Unmapped strategy sleeves remained in cash: "
                + ", ".join(f"{name} ({weight:.1%})" for name, weight in unmapped.items())
            )
        if use_trailing_stop:
            warnings.append(
                "Stops use volatility known through the previous observation and "
                "execute at the current close; intraday stop execution is not modeled."
            )
        if any(ticker in {"UPRO", "SH"} for ticker in target_weights):
            warnings.append(
                "Leveraged/inverse ETP path dependence and financing drag are only "
                "captured insofar as they appear in the supplied adjusted prices."
            )
        assumptions = (
            "Target weights are static throughout the test.",
            "Trades execute at supplied closing prices with proportional costs.",
            "Fractional units and a zero-yield cash account are allowed.",
            "Input prices should be split- and distribution-adjusted total-return prices.",
            "No taxes, borrow fees, bid/ask dynamics, market impact, capacity, "
            "delistings, or order rejection are modeled.",
            "Results are hypothetical and past performance does not predict future results.",
        )
        diagnostics = BacktestDiagnostics(
            target_weights=MappingProxyType(dict(target_weights)),
            cash_target_weight=cash_weight,
            sleeve_mapping=MappingProxyType(dict(used_mapping)),
            unmapped_sleeves=MappingProxyType(dict(unmapped)),
            initial_capital=capital,
            final_equity=float(equity.iloc[-1]),
            transaction_costs=state.transaction_costs,
            turnover_notional=state.turnover_notional,
            rebalance_count=state.rebalance_count,
            executed_orders=len(state.events),
            observations=len(equity),
            elapsed_years=metrics["elapsed_years"],
            exposure_history=exposures,
            execution_events=tuple(state.events),
            warnings=tuple(dict.fromkeys(warnings)),
            assumptions=assumptions,
        )
        return BacktestAnalysis(result=result, diagnostics=diagnostics)

    def _cost_rate(self, value: Any, name: str) -> float:
        rate = _backtest_finite(value, name)
        if not 0 <= rate < 1:
            raise BacktestConfigurationError(f"{name} must be in [0, 1)")
        return rate

    @staticmethod
    def _normalize_price_data(
        price_data: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame],
    ) -> dict[str, pd.DataFrame]:
        if not isinstance(price_data, Mapping) or not price_data:
            raise BacktestDataError("price_data must be a non-empty mapping")
        normalized: dict[str, pd.DataFrame] = {}
        for raw_ticker, frame in price_data.items():
            ticker = str(raw_ticker).strip().upper()
            if not ticker:
                raise BacktestDataError("price_data ticker cannot be empty")
            if ticker in normalized:
                raise BacktestDataError(
                    f"duplicate price ticker after normalization: {ticker}"
                )
            if not isinstance(frame, pd.DataFrame):
                raise BacktestDataError(f"{ticker}: price data must be a DataFrame")
            normalized[ticker] = frame
        return normalized

    @staticmethod
    def _normalize_mapping(
        override: Optional[Mapping[str, tuple[str, ...] | list[str] | str]],
    ) -> dict[str, tuple[str, ...]]:
        result = {key: tuple(value) for key, value in DEFAULT_ASSET_MAPPING.items()}
        if override is None:
            return result
        if not isinstance(override, Mapping):
            raise BacktestConfigurationError("asset_mapping must be a mapping")
        for raw_sleeve, raw_candidates in override.items():
            sleeve = str(raw_sleeve).strip().lower()
            if not sleeve:
                raise BacktestConfigurationError("asset_mapping sleeve cannot be empty")
            if isinstance(raw_candidates, str):
                candidates = (raw_candidates.strip().upper(),)
            else:
                candidates = tuple(
                    str(item).strip().upper() for item in raw_candidates
                )
            if any(not item for item in candidates):
                raise BacktestConfigurationError(
                    f"asset_mapping[{sleeve}] contains an empty ticker"
                )
            result[sleeve] = tuple(dict.fromkeys(candidates))
        return result

    def _map_allocations_to_tickers_detailed(
        self,
        allocations: Mapping[str, float],
        available: tuple[str, ...],
        mapping: Mapping[str, tuple[str, ...]],
    ) -> tuple[
        dict[str, float],
        float,
        dict[str, tuple[str, ...]],
        dict[str, float],
    ]:
        if not isinstance(allocations, Mapping) or not allocations:
            raise BacktestConfigurationError("strategy allocations cannot be empty")
        available_set = set(available)
        total = 0.0
        ticker_weights: dict[str, float] = {}
        cash_weight = 0.0
        used_mapping: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {}
        unmapped: dict[str, float] = {}

        for raw_sleeve, raw_weight in allocations.items():
            sleeve_raw = str(raw_sleeve).strip()
            sleeve = sleeve_raw.lower()
            if not sleeve:
                raise BacktestConfigurationError("allocation sleeve cannot be empty")
            weight = _backtest_finite(raw_weight, f"allocation[{sleeve}]")
            if weight < -self.config.weight_tolerance:
                raise BacktestConfigurationError(
                    "negative allocations are not supported by this long-only engine"
                )
            if weight <= self.config.weight_tolerance:
                continue
            total += weight
            direct_ticker = sleeve_raw.upper()
            if sleeve == "cash":
                cash_weight += weight
                used_mapping[sleeve] = ()
                continue
            if direct_ticker in available_set:
                matches = (direct_ticker,)
            else:
                matches = tuple(
                    ticker
                    for ticker in mapping.get(sleeve, ())
                    if ticker in available_set
                )
            if not matches:
                cash_weight += weight
                unmapped[sleeve] = unmapped.get(sleeve, 0.0) + weight
                used_mapping[sleeve] = ()
                continue
            used_mapping[sleeve] = matches
            split = weight / len(matches)
            for ticker in matches:
                ticker_weights[ticker] = ticker_weights.get(ticker, 0.0) + split

        if total > 1.0 + self.config.weight_tolerance:
            raise BacktestConfigurationError(
                f"strategy allocations exceed 100% ({total:.4f})"
            )
        if total < 1.0 - self.config.weight_tolerance:
            cash_weight += 1.0 - total
        for ticker, weight in ticker_weights.items():
            if weight > self.config.maximum_single_weight + self.config.weight_tolerance:
                raise BacktestConfigurationError(
                    f"{ticker} target weight {weight:.2%} exceeds configured maximum"
                )
        mapped_total = sum(ticker_weights.values()) + cash_weight
        if abs(mapped_total - 1.0) > self.config.weight_tolerance:
            raise BacktestConfigurationError(
                f"mapped allocations must sum to 100%; received {mapped_total:.8f}"
            )
        return ticker_weights, cash_weight, used_mapping, unmapped

    def _map_allocations_to_tickers(
        self,
        allocations: Mapping[str, float],
        available: list[str],
    ) -> dict[str, float]:
        """Compatibility wrapper with safe cash handling."""

        normalized_available = tuple(str(item).strip().upper() for item in available)
        weights, _, _, _ = self._map_allocations_to_tickers_detailed(
            allocations,
            normalized_available,
            self._normalize_mapping(None),
        )
        return weights

    def _prepare_closes(
        self,
        price_data: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame],
        tickers: tuple[str, ...],
        start_date: Optional[str],
        end_date: Optional[str],
    ) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, tuple[str, ...]]:
        series: list[pd.Series] = []
        warnings: list[str] = []
        for ticker in tickers:
            frame = price_data[ticker]
            if "Close" not in frame.columns:
                raise BacktestDataError(f"{ticker}: missing required 'Close' column")
            values = pd.to_numeric(frame["Close"], errors="coerce")
            values = values[~values.index.duplicated(keep="last")].sort_index()
            if not isinstance(values.index, pd.DatetimeIndex):
                try:
                    values.index = pd.to_datetime(values.index, errors="raise")
                except Exception as exc:
                    raise BacktestDataError(
                        f"{ticker}: index must be datetime-like"
                    ) from exc
            if values.index.tz is not None:
                values.index = values.index.tz_convert("UTC").tz_localize(None)
            invalid = values.isna() | ~np.isfinite(values) | (values <= 0)
            if bool(invalid.any()):
                warnings.append(
                    f"{ticker}: {int(invalid.sum())} invalid price rows were discarded."
                )
            values = values.loc[~invalid].astype(float).rename(ticker)
            if values.empty:
                raise BacktestDataError(f"{ticker}: no valid positive prices")
            series.append(values)

        closes = pd.concat(series, axis=1, join="outer").sort_index()
        if start_date is not None:
            start = self._parse_date(start_date, "start_date")
            closes = closes.loc[closes.index >= start]
        if end_date is not None:
            end = self._parse_date(end_date, "end_date")
            closes = closes.loc[closes.index <= end]
        if start_date is not None and end_date is not None and start > end:
            raise BacktestConfigurationError("start_date must not exceed end_date")
        before_fill = int(closes.isna().sum().sum())
        if self.config.maximum_forward_fill:
            closes = closes.ffill(limit=self.config.maximum_forward_fill)
        closes = closes.dropna(how="any")
        if before_fill:
            warnings.append(
                f"Aligned series contained {before_fill} missing cells; forward fill "
                f"was limited to {self.config.maximum_forward_fill} observations."
            )
        if len(closes) < self.config.minimum_observations:
            raise BacktestDataError(
                f"at least {self.config.minimum_observations} aligned observations "
                f"are required; received {len(closes)}"
            )
        if not closes.index.is_monotonic_increasing or closes.index.has_duplicates:
            raise BacktestDataError("prepared dates must be unique and increasing")
        returns = closes.pct_change(fill_method=None).iloc[1:]
        if bool((returns <= -1.0).any().any()):
            raise BacktestDataError("price series implies a return of -100% or below")
        return closes, tuple(warnings)

    @staticmethod
    def _parse_date(value: str, name: str) -> pd.Timestamp:
        try:
            timestamp = pd.Timestamp(value)
        except Exception as exc:
            raise BacktestConfigurationError(f"{name} is not a valid date") from exc
        if timestamp.tz is not None:
            timestamp = timestamp.tz_convert("UTC").tz_localize(None)
        return timestamp

    def _lagged_volatility(
        self, closes: pd.DataFrame, window: int
    ) -> pd.DataFrame:
        returns = closes.pct_change(fill_method=None)
        volatility = self.risk_intel.rolling_volatility(
            returns,
            window=window,
            annualize=False,
            minimum_periods=window,
            fallback_volatility=None,
        )
        if not isinstance(volatility, pd.DataFrame):
            raise BacktestError("risk rolling_volatility returned an invalid type")
        # A stop evaluated at close t may only use volatility through t-1.
        return volatility.shift(1)

    @staticmethod
    def _rebalance_flags(
        index: pd.DatetimeIndex, frequency: str
    ) -> np.ndarray:
        flags = np.zeros(len(index), dtype=bool)
        flags[0] = True
        if frequency in {"none", "buy_and_hold"}:
            return flags
        if frequency == "daily":
            flags[:] = True
            return flags
        periods = {
            "weekly": index.to_period("W-FRI"),
            "monthly": index.to_period("M"),
            "quarterly": index.to_period("Q"),
            "yearly": index.to_period("Y"),
        }[frequency]
        flags[1:] = np.asarray(periods[1:] != periods[:-1])
        return flags

    def _simulate_event_driven(
        self,
        *,
        closes: pd.DataFrame,
        target_weights: Mapping[str, float],
        initial_capital: float,
        rebalance_flags: np.ndarray,
        cost_rate: float,
        use_trailing_stop: bool,
        sl_vol_multiplier: float,
        lagged_volatility: pd.DataFrame,
    ) -> tuple[pd.Series, pd.DataFrame, _SimulationState]:
        tickers = tuple(target_weights)
        weights = np.array([target_weights[ticker] for ticker in tickers], dtype=float)
        state = _SimulationState(
            units=np.zeros(len(tickers), dtype=float),
            cash=initial_capital,
            peak_prices=closes.loc[closes.index[0], tickers].to_numpy(dtype=float),
            active=np.ones(len(tickers), dtype=bool),
        )
        equity_values: list[float] = []
        exposure_rows: list[np.ndarray] = []

        for position, date in enumerate(closes.index):
            prices = closes.loc[date, tickers].to_numpy(dtype=float)
            position_values = state.units * prices
            pre_trade_equity = float(position_values.sum() + state.cash)
            if pre_trade_equity <= _EPSILON:
                raise BacktestError(f"portfolio equity became non-positive on {date}")

            if rebalance_flags[position]:
                self._execute_rebalance(
                    state, date, tickers, prices, weights, cost_rate
                )
            elif use_trailing_stop:
                state.peak_prices = np.where(
                    state.active,
                    np.maximum(state.peak_prices, prices),
                    state.peak_prices,
                )
                volatility = lagged_volatility.loc[date, tickers].to_numpy(dtype=float)
                self._execute_stops(
                    state=state,
                    date=date,
                    tickers=tickers,
                    prices=prices,
                    volatility=volatility,
                    multiplier=sl_vol_multiplier,
                    cost_rate=cost_rate,
                )

            values = state.units * prices
            equity = float(values.sum() + state.cash)
            if equity <= _EPSILON or not math.isfinite(equity):
                raise BacktestError(f"invalid portfolio equity on {date}")
            equity_values.append(equity)
            exposure_rows.append(
                np.concatenate((values / equity, np.array([state.cash / equity])))
            )

        exposure_columns = list(tickers) + ["CASH"]
        exposures = pd.DataFrame(
            exposure_rows, index=closes.index, columns=exposure_columns
        )
        equity_series = pd.Series(
            equity_values, index=closes.index, name="equity", dtype=float
        )
        return equity_series, exposures, state

    def _execute_rebalance(
        self,
        state: _SimulationState,
        date: pd.Timestamp,
        tickers: tuple[str, ...],
        prices: np.ndarray,
        target_weights: np.ndarray,
        cost_rate: float,
    ) -> None:
        current_values = state.units * prices
        pre_cost_equity = float(current_values.sum() + state.cash)
        post_cost_equity = pre_cost_equity
        # Fixed-point solution: E_after = E_before - c*sum|w*E_after-current|.
        for _ in range(100):
            target_values = target_weights * post_cost_equity
            traded = float(np.abs(target_values - current_values).sum())
            updated = pre_cost_equity - cost_rate * traded
            if abs(updated - post_cost_equity) <= max(1e-8, pre_cost_equity * 1e-12):
                post_cost_equity = updated
                break
            post_cost_equity = updated
        if post_cost_equity <= _EPSILON:
            raise BacktestError("transaction costs exhausted portfolio equity")
        target_values = target_weights * post_cost_equity
        deltas = target_values - current_values
        traded_notional = float(np.abs(deltas).sum())
        total_cost = traded_notional * cost_rate
        state.units = target_values / prices
        state.cash = pre_cost_equity - float(target_values.sum()) - total_cost
        if state.cash < -max(1e-7, pre_cost_equity * 1e-10):
            raise BacktestError("rebalance produced negative cash")
        state.cash = max(0.0, state.cash)
        state.transaction_costs += total_cost
        state.turnover_notional += traded_notional
        state.rebalance_count += 1
        state.active = target_values > _EPSILON
        state.peak_prices = prices.copy()

        for index, delta in enumerate(deltas):
            if abs(delta) <= max(1e-8, pre_cost_equity * 1e-12):
                continue
            notional = abs(float(delta))
            state.events.append(
                ExecutionEvent(
                    date=self._date_string(date),
                    ticker=tickers[index],
                    event_type="buy" if delta > 0 else "sell",
                    notional=notional,
                    cost=notional * cost_rate,
                    price=float(prices[index]),
                    units_delta=float(delta / prices[index]),
                    reason="scheduled_rebalance",
                )
            )

    def _execute_stops(
        self,
        *,
        state: _SimulationState,
        date: pd.Timestamp,
        tickers: tuple[str, ...],
        prices: np.ndarray,
        volatility: np.ndarray,
        multiplier: float,
        cost_rate: float,
    ) -> None:
        for index, ticker in enumerate(tickers):
            if not state.active[index] or state.units[index] <= _EPSILON:
                continue
            sigma = float(volatility[index])
            if not math.isfinite(sigma) or sigma <= 0:
                continue
            threshold = float(
                np.clip(
                    multiplier * sigma,
                    self.config.minimum_stop_pct,
                    self.config.maximum_stop_pct,
                )
            )
            peak = float(state.peak_prices[index])
            drawdown = float(prices[index] / peak - 1.0)
            if drawdown > -threshold:
                continue
            units = float(state.units[index])
            notional = units * float(prices[index])
            cost = notional * cost_rate
            state.cash += notional - cost
            state.units[index] = 0.0
            state.active[index] = False
            state.transaction_costs += cost
            state.turnover_notional += notional
            event = ExecutionEvent(
                date=self._date_string(date),
                ticker=ticker,
                event_type="sell",
                notional=notional,
                cost=cost,
                price=float(prices[index]),
                units_delta=-units,
                reason="trailing_stop",
            )
            state.events.append(event)
            state.stop_events.append(
                {
                    "date": event.date,
