                    "ticker": ticker,
                    "drawdown": round(drawdown, 6),
                    "threshold": round(-threshold, 6),
                    "notional": round(notional, 2),
                    "cost": round(cost, 2),
                    "volatility_source": "lagged_daily_rolling_std",
                }
            )

    @staticmethod
    def _date_string(value: Any) -> str:
        return (
            value.date().isoformat()
            if hasattr(value, "date")
            else str(value)
        )

    def _metrics(
        self,
        equity: pd.Series,
        returns: pd.Series,
        initial_capital: float,
    ) -> dict[str, float]:
        total_return = float(equity.iloc[-1] / initial_capital - 1.0)
        if isinstance(equity.index, pd.DatetimeIndex) and len(equity) > 1:
            elapsed_years = max(
                (equity.index[-1] - equity.index[0]).total_seconds()
                / (365.2425 * 24 * 60 * 60),
                1.0 / self.config.periods_per_year,
            )
        else:
            elapsed_years = max(
                (len(equity) - 1) / self.config.periods_per_year,
                1.0 / self.config.periods_per_year,
            )
        growth = float(equity.iloc[-1] / initial_capital)
        cagr = growth ** (1.0 / elapsed_years) - 1.0 if growth > 0 else -1.0
        # Include starting capital as the initial high-water mark so entry costs
        # and an immediate first-period loss are not erased from drawdown.
        drawdown_equity = pd.Series(
            np.concatenate(([initial_capital], equity.to_numpy(dtype=float)))
        )
        max_drawdown = self._max_drawdown(drawdown_equity)
        sharpe = self._sharpe(returns)
        sortino = self._sortino(returns)
        # BacktestResult requires a finite number; zero denotes an undefined
        # ratio when no drawdown was observed.
        calmar = cagr / abs(max_drawdown) if max_drawdown < -_EPSILON else 0.0
        return {
            "total_return": total_return,
            "cagr": cagr,
            "max_drawdown": max_drawdown,
            "sharpe": sharpe,
            "sortino": sortino,
            "calmar": calmar,
            "elapsed_years": elapsed_years,
        }

    @staticmethod
    def _max_drawdown(equity: pd.Series) -> float:
        clean = pd.to_numeric(equity, errors="coerce").dropna()
        if clean.empty or bool((clean <= 0).any()):
            raise BacktestDataError("equity must contain positive finite values")
        drawdown = clean / clean.cummax() - 1.0
        return float(drawdown.min())

    def _daily_risk_free(self) -> float:
        return math.expm1(
            math.log1p(self.config.risk_free_rate) / self.config.periods_per_year
        )

    def _sharpe(self, returns: pd.Series) -> float:
        clean = pd.to_numeric(returns, errors="coerce").dropna()
        if len(clean) < 2:
            return 0.0
        excess = clean - self._daily_risk_free()
        deviation = float(excess.std(ddof=1))
        if deviation <= _EPSILON:
            return 0.0
        return float(
            excess.mean() / deviation * math.sqrt(self.config.periods_per_year)
        )

    def _sortino(self, returns: pd.Series) -> float:
        clean = pd.to_numeric(returns, errors="coerce").dropna()
        if clean.empty:
            return 0.0
        excess = clean - self._daily_risk_free()
        downside = np.minimum(excess.to_numpy(dtype=float), 0.0)
        downside_deviation = math.sqrt(float(np.mean(np.square(downside))))
        if downside_deviation <= _EPSILON:
            return 0.0
        return float(
            excess.mean()
            / downside_deviation
            * math.sqrt(self.config.periods_per_year)
        )

    def _empty_result(self, name: str) -> BacktestResult:
        """Compatibility helper; public runs raise descriptive errors instead."""

        return BacktestResult(
            strategy_name=str(name),
            period="N/A",
            assets=[],
            total_return=0.0,
            cagr=0.0,
            max_drawdown=0.0,
            sharpe_ratio=0.0,
            sortino_ratio=0.0,
            calmar_ratio=0.0,
            win_rate=0.0,
            trades=0,
        )

    def format_report(
        self, result: BacktestResult | BacktestAnalysis
    ) -> str:
        analysis = result if isinstance(result, BacktestAnalysis) else None
        summary = analysis.result if analysis is not None else result
        lines = [
            "=" * 72,
            f"BACKTESTING LAB - {summary.strategy_name}",
            "=" * 72,
            f"Period                 : {summary.period}",
            f"Assets                 : {', '.join(summary.assets) or 'None'}",
            "",
            "Performance:",
            f"  Total return         : {summary.total_return:.2%}",
            f"  CAGR                 : {summary.cagr:.2%}",
            f"  Maximum drawdown     : {summary.max_drawdown:.2%}",
            f"  Sharpe ratio         : {summary.sharpe_ratio:.3f}",
            f"  Sortino ratio        : {summary.sortino_ratio:.3f}",
            f"  Calmar ratio         : {summary.calmar_ratio:.3f}",
            f"  Positive months      : {summary.win_rate:.1%}",
            f"  Executed orders      : {summary.trades}",
            f"  Trailing stops       : {'ON' if summary.stop_loss_enabled else 'OFF'} "
            f"({summary.stop_loss_triggers} triggers)",
        ]
        if analysis is not None:
            diagnostics = analysis.diagnostics
            lines.extend(
                [
                    "",
                    "Execution diagnostics:",
                    f"  Initial capital      : {diagnostics.initial_capital:,.2f}",
                    f"  Final equity         : {diagnostics.final_equity:,.2f}",
                    f"  Transaction costs    : {diagnostics.transaction_costs:,.2f}",
                    f"  Traded notional      : {diagnostics.turnover_notional:,.2f}",
                    f"  Rebalance events     : {diagnostics.rebalance_count}",
                    f"  Target cash          : {diagnostics.cash_target_weight:.1%}",
                ]
            )
            if diagnostics.warnings:
                lines.extend(["", "Warnings:"])
                lines.extend(f"  - {item}" for item in diagnostics.warnings)
            lines.extend(["", "Assumptions and limitations:"])
            lines.extend(f"  - {item}" for item in diagnostics.assumptions)
        if summary.stop_loss_events:
            lines.extend(["", "Most recent stop events:"])
            for event in summary.stop_loss_events[-5:]:
                lines.append(
                    f"  - {event['date']} {event['ticker']}: "
                    f"{event['drawdown']:.2%} versus {event['threshold']:.2%}"
                )
        lines.append("=" * 72)
        return "\n".join(lines)

    def compare_strategies(self, results: list[BacktestResult]) -> str:
        if not results:
            return "No results to compare."
        valid = [item for item in results if isinstance(item, BacktestResult)]
        if len(valid) != len(results):
            raise BacktestConfigurationError(
                "all comparison items must be BacktestResult objects"
            )
        ordered = sorted(
            valid,
            key=lambda item: (
                math.isfinite(float(item.sharpe_ratio)),
                float(item.sharpe_ratio),
                float(item.cagr),
            ),
            reverse=True,
        )
        lines = [
            "=" * 82,
            "STRATEGY COMPARISON (sorted by historical Sharpe ratio)",
            "=" * 82,
            f"{'Strategy':<30} {'CAGR':>9} {'MaxDD':>9} {'Sharpe':>9} "
            f"{'Win%':>8} {'Orders':>8}",
            "-" * 82,
        ]
        for item in ordered:
            lines.append(
                f"{item.strategy_name[:30]:<30} {item.cagr:>8.2%} "
                f"{item.max_drawdown:>8.2%} {item.sharpe_ratio:>9.3f} "
                f"{item.win_rate:>7.1%} {item.trades:>8}"
            )
        lines.append("=" * 82)
        return "\n".join(lines)


__all__ = [
    "BacktestAnalysis",
    "BacktestConfig",
    "BacktestConfigurationError",
    "BacktestDataError",
    "BacktestDiagnostics",
    "BacktestError",
    "BacktestingLab",
    "DEFAULT_ASSET_MAPPING",
    "ExecutionEvent",
]

# MODULE 10







import logging
import math
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from types import MappingProxyType
from typing import Any, Callable, Final, Mapping, Optional

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

try:
    from scipy.optimize import OptimizeResult, minimize

    SCIPY_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:  # Optional acceleration; a deterministic fallback is included.
    OptimizeResult = Any  # type: ignore[misc,assignment]
    minimize = None  # type: ignore[assignment]
    SCIPY_AVAILABLE = False

# This distribution is a single-file build. Core names are defined above.


LOGGER = logging.getLogger(f"{APP_NAME}.optimizer")
_EPSILON: Final = 1e-12
_METHODS: Final = frozenset(
    {
        "max_sharpe",
        "min_volatility",
        "target_return",
        "risk_parity",
        "black_litterman",
    }
)


class OptimizationError(SmartInvestError):
    """Base error for Module 10."""


class OptimizationDataError(OptimizationError):
    """Raised when input data cannot support stable optimization."""


class OptimizationInfeasibleError(OptimizationError):
    """Raised when portfolio constraints have no feasible solution."""


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class OptimizerConfig:
    periods_per_year: int = 252
    minimum_observations: int = 60
    covariance_shrinkage: float = 0.10
    eigenvalue_floor: float = 1e-10
    default_lower_bound: float = 0.0
    default_upper_bound: float = 1.0
    solver_tolerance: float = 1e-10
    solver_max_iterations: int = 2_000
    random_restarts: int = 8
    random_seed: int = 48
    risk_free_rate: float = 0.045
    weight_tolerance: float = 1e-7

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not 2 <= int(self.periods_per_year) <= 366:
            raise ValueError("periods_per_year must be in [2, 366]")
        if int(self.minimum_observations) < 20:
            raise ValueError("minimum_observations must be at least 20")
        if not 0.0 <= float(self.covariance_shrinkage) <= 1.0:
            raise ValueError("covariance_shrinkage must be in [0, 1]")
        if not 0.0 < float(self.eigenvalue_floor) < 1.0:
            raise ValueError("eigenvalue_floor must be in (0, 1)")
        if not 0.0 <= float(self.default_lower_bound) <= 1.0:
            raise ValueError("default_lower_bound must be in [0, 1]")
        if not 0.0 < float(self.default_upper_bound) <= 1.0:
            raise ValueError("default_upper_bound must be in (0, 1]")
        if self.default_lower_bound > self.default_upper_bound:
            raise ValueError("default_lower_bound cannot exceed default_upper_bound")
        if float(self.solver_tolerance) <= 0:
            raise ValueError("solver_tolerance must be positive")
        if int(self.solver_max_iterations) < 100:
            raise ValueError("solver_max_iterations must be at least 100")
        if not 1 <= int(self.random_restarts) <= 100:
            raise ValueError("random_restarts must be in [1, 100]")
        if not -0.99 < float(self.risk_free_rate) <= 1.0:
            raise ValueError("risk_free_rate must be in (-0.99, 1]")
        if not 0.0 < float(self.weight_tolerance) <= 0.01:
            raise ValueError("weight_tolerance must be in (0, 0.01]")


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PortfolioSolution:
    method: str
    weights: Mapping[str, float]
    expected_return: float
    expected_volatility: float
    sharpe_ratio: float
    risk_contributions: Mapping[str, float]
    one_way_turnover: Optional[float]
    observations: int
    objective_value: float
    solver_message: str
    covariance_condition_number: float
    warnings: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    assumptions: tuple[str, ...] = ()


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class EfficientFrontier:
    solutions: tuple[PortfolioSolution, ...]
    minimum_return: float
    maximum_return: float
    failed_targets: tuple[float, ...] = ()


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class _PreparedInputs:
    returns: pd.DataFrame
    tickers: tuple[str, ...]
    mean: np.ndarray
    covariance: np.ndarray
    condition_number: float
    warnings: tuple[str, ...]


@dataclass(slots=True)
class _FallbackResult:
    x: np.ndarray
    fun: float
    success: bool
    message: str


def _optimizer_finite(value: Any, name: str) -> float:
    try:
        result = float(value)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise OptimizationDataError(f"{name} must be numeric") from exc
    if not math.isfinite(result):
        raise OptimizationDataError(f"{name} must be finite")
    return result


class PortfolioOptimizer:
    """Deterministic, long-only constrained portfolio optimizer."""

    def __init__(self, config: Optional[OptimizerConfig] = None) -> None:
        self.config = config or OptimizerConfig()

    @staticmethod
    def apply_turnover_constraint(
        new_weights: Mapping[str, float],
        prev_weights: Optional[Mapping[str, float]],
        max_turnover: float = 0.15,
        crisis_regime: bool = False,
    ) -> dict[str, float]:
        """Interpolate exactly toward a target without renormalization drift.

        Both portfolios must be valid long-only portfolios summing to one.
        Public optimization methods impose the limit inside SLSQP; this helper
        remains useful for external target portfolios.
        """

        new = PortfolioOptimizer._validated_weight_mapping(
            new_weights, "new_weights"
        )
        if prev_weights is None or crisis_regime:
            return new
        previous = PortfolioOptimizer._validated_weight_mapping(
            prev_weights, "prev_weights"
        )
            limit = _optimizer_finite(max_turnover, "max_turnover")
        if not 0.0 <= limit <= 1.0:
            raise OptimizationInfeasibleError("max_turnover must be in [0, 1]")

        universe = tuple(sorted(set(new) | set(previous)))
        new_vector = np.array([new.get(ticker, 0.0) for ticker in universe])
        old_vector = np.array([previous.get(ticker, 0.0) for ticker in universe])
        delta = new_vector - old_vector
        turnover = 0.5 * float(np.abs(delta).sum())
        if turnover <= limit + 1e-12:
            return {ticker: float(weight) for ticker, weight in zip(universe, new_vector)}
        if limit == 0:
            return {
                ticker: float(weight)
                for ticker, weight in zip(universe, old_vector)
            }
        scale = limit / turnover
        constrained = old_vector + scale * delta
        constrained[np.abs(constrained) < 1e-15] = 0.0
        # Convex interpolation of two valid portfolios remains valid and sums
        # to one, so clipping or renormalizing would only introduce error.
        realized = 0.5 * float(np.abs(constrained - old_vector).sum())
        if realized > limit + 1e-10 or abs(float(constrained.sum()) - 1.0) > 1e-10:
            raise OptimizationError("turnover interpolation failed invariant checks")
        return {
            ticker: float(weight)
            for ticker, weight in zip(universe, constrained)
            if weight > 1e-14
        }

    def mean_variance(
        self,
        returns_df: pd.DataFrame,
        target_return: Optional[float] = None,
        risk_free_rate: Optional[float] = None,
        prev_weights: Optional[Mapping[str, float]] = None,
        max_turnover: float = 0.15,
        crisis_regime: bool = False,
    ) -> dict[str, float]:
        """Maximize Sharpe, or minimize volatility above ``target_return``."""

        return dict(
            self.mean_variance_detailed(
                returns_df,
                target_return=target_return,
                risk_free_rate=risk_free_rate,
                prev_weights=prev_weights,
                max_turnover=max_turnover,
                crisis_regime=crisis_regime,
            ).weights
        )

    def mean_variance_detailed(
        self,
        returns_df: pd.DataFrame,
        target_return: Optional[float] = None,
        risk_free_rate: Optional[float] = None,
        prev_weights: Optional[Mapping[str, float]] = None,
        max_turnover: float = 0.15,
        crisis_regime: bool = False,
        *,
        bounds: Optional[Mapping[str, tuple[float, float]]] = None,
    ) -> PortfolioSolution:
        prepared = self._prepare_returns(returns_df)
        if target_return is None:
            return self._max_sharpe_solution(
                prepared,
                risk_free_rate,
                prev_weights,
                max_turnover,
                crisis_regime,
                bounds,
            )
        target = _optimizer_finite(target_return, "target_return")
        return self._minimum_variance_solution(
            prepared,
            target,
            risk_free_rate,
            prev_weights,
            max_turnover,
            crisis_regime,
            bounds,
            method="target_return",
        )

    def max_sharpe(
        self,
        returns_df: pd.DataFrame,
        risk_free_rate: Optional[float] = None,
        prev_weights: Optional[Mapping[str, float]] = None,
        max_turnover: float = 0.15,
        crisis_regime: bool = False,
    ) -> dict[str, float]:
        return self.mean_variance(
            returns_df,
            target_return=None,
            risk_free_rate=risk_free_rate,
            prev_weights=prev_weights,
            max_turnover=max_turnover,
            crisis_regime=crisis_regime,
        )

    def min_volatility(
        self,
        returns_df: pd.DataFrame,
        prev_weights: Optional[Mapping[str, float]] = None,
        max_turnover: float = 0.15,
        crisis_regime: bool = False,
    ) -> dict[str, float]:
        return dict(
            self.min_volatility_detailed(
                returns_df,
                prev_weights=prev_weights,
                max_turnover=max_turnover,
                crisis_regime=crisis_regime,
            ).weights
        )

    def min_volatility_detailed(
        self,
        returns_df: pd.DataFrame,
        prev_weights: Optional[Mapping[str, float]] = None,
        max_turnover: float = 0.15,
        crisis_regime: bool = False,
        *,
        bounds: Optional[Mapping[str, tuple[float, float]]] = None,
    ) -> PortfolioSolution:
        prepared = self._prepare_returns(returns_df)
        return self._minimum_variance_solution(
            prepared,
            None,
            None,
            prev_weights,
            max_turnover,
            crisis_regime,
            bounds,
            method="min_volatility",
        )

    def risk_parity(
        self,
        returns_df: pd.DataFrame,
        prev_weights: Optional[Mapping[str, float]] = None,
        max_turnover: float = 0.15,
        crisis_regime: bool = False,
    ) -> dict[str, float]:
        return dict(
            self.risk_parity_detailed(
                returns_df,
                prev_weights=prev_weights,
                max_turnover=max_turnover,
                crisis_regime=crisis_regime,
            ).weights
        )

    def risk_parity_detailed(
        self,
        returns_df: pd.DataFrame,
        prev_weights: Optional[Mapping[str, float]] = None,
        max_turnover: float = 0.15,
        crisis_regime: bool = False,
        *,
        bounds: Optional[Mapping[str, tuple[float, float]]] = None,
    ) -> PortfolioSolution:
        prepared = self._prepare_returns(returns_df)
        scipy_bounds = self._bounds(prepared.tickers, bounds)
        previous, turnover_limit = self._turnover_inputs(
            prepared.tickers,
            prev_weights,
            max_turnover,
            crisis_regime,
            scipy_bounds,
        )

        def objective(weights: np.ndarray) -> float:
            variance = float(weights @ prepared.covariance @ weights)
            if variance <= _EPSILON:
                return 1e12
            contributions = weights * (prepared.covariance @ weights) / variance
            target = np.full(len(weights), 1.0 / len(weights))
            return float(np.square(contributions - target).sum())

        result = self._solve(
            objective,
            prepared,
            scipy_bounds,
            previous,
            turnover_limit,
            extra_constraints=(),
        )
        return self._solution(
            "risk_parity",
            result,
            prepared,
            self.config.risk_free_rate,
            previous,
            prepared.warnings,
        )

    def black_litterman(
        self,
        returns_df: pd.DataFrame,
        views: Optional[Mapping[str, float]] = None,
        risk_aversion: float = 2.5,
        prev_weights: Optional[Mapping[str, float]] = None,
        max_turnover: float = 0.15,
        crisis_regime: bool = False,
        *,
        market_weights: Optional[Mapping[str, float]] = None,
        view_confidences: Optional[Mapping[str, float]] = None,
        tau: float = 0.05,
    ) -> dict[str, float]:
        return dict(
            self.black_litterman_detailed(
                returns_df,
                views=views,
                risk_aversion=risk_aversion,
                prev_weights=prev_weights,
                max_turnover=max_turnover,
                crisis_regime=crisis_regime,
                market_weights=market_weights,
                view_confidences=view_confidences,
                tau=tau,
            ).weights
        )

    def black_litterman_detailed(
        self,
        returns_df: pd.DataFrame,
        views: Optional[Mapping[str, float]] = None,
        risk_aversion: float = 2.5,
        prev_weights: Optional[Mapping[str, float]] = None,
        max_turnover: float = 0.15,
        crisis_regime: bool = False,
        *,
        market_weights: Optional[Mapping[str, float]] = None,
        view_confidences: Optional[Mapping[str, float]] = None,
        tau: float = 0.05,
        bounds: Optional[Mapping[str, tuple[float, float]]] = None,
    ) -> PortfolioSolution:
        prepared = self._prepare_returns(returns_df)
        delta = _optimizer_finite(risk_aversion, "risk_aversion")
        if delta <= 0:
            raise OptimizationDataError("risk_aversion must be positive")
        tau_value = _optimizer_finite(tau, "tau")
        if not 0 < tau_value <= 1:
            raise OptimizationDataError("tau must be in (0, 1]")
        market = self._market_weights(prepared.tickers, market_weights)
        prior = delta * prepared.covariance @ market
        posterior_mean, posterior_covariance, bl_warnings = self._posterior(
            prepared,
            prior,
            views,
            view_confidences,
            tau_value,
            equal_market_weights_assumed=market_weights is None,
        )
        posterior = _PreparedInputs(
            returns=prepared.returns,
            tickers=prepared.tickers,
            mean=posterior_mean,
            covariance=self._nearest_psd(posterior_covariance),
            condition_number=float(np.linalg.cond(posterior_covariance)),
            warnings=prepared.warnings + bl_warnings,
        )
        scipy_bounds = self._bounds(prepared.tickers, bounds)
        previous, turnover_limit = self._turnover_inputs(
            prepared.tickers,
            prev_weights,
            max_turnover,
            crisis_regime,
            scipy_bounds,
        )

        def negative_utility(weights: np.ndarray) -> float:
            return float(
                0.5 * delta * weights @ posterior.covariance @ weights
                - weights @ posterior.mean
            )

        result = self._solve(
            negative_utility,
            posterior,
            scipy_bounds,
            previous,
            turnover_limit,
            extra_constraints=(),
        )
        return self._solution(
            "black_litterman",
            result,
            posterior,
            self.config.risk_free_rate,
            previous,
            posterior.warnings,
        )

    def efficient_frontier(
        self,
        returns_df: pd.DataFrame,
        points: int = 20,
        *,
        bounds: Optional[Mapping[str, tuple[float, float]]] = None,
    ) -> EfficientFrontier:
        if not 3 <= int(points) <= 200:
            raise ValueError("points must be in [3, 200]")
        prepared = self._prepare_returns(returns_df)
        scipy_bounds = self._bounds(prepared.tickers, bounds)
        minimum_return, maximum_return = self._feasible_return_range(
            prepared.mean, scipy_bounds
        )
        targets = np.linspace(minimum_return, maximum_return, int(points))
        solutions: list[PortfolioSolution] = []
        failures: list[float] = []
        for target in targets:
            try:
                solutions.append(
                    self._minimum_variance_solution(
                        prepared,
                        float(target),
                        None,
                        None,
                        1.0,
                        True,
                        bounds,
                        method="target_return",
                    )
                )
            except OptimizationError:
                failures.append(float(target))
        if not solutions:
            raise OptimizationError("no efficient-frontier target was solved")
        return EfficientFrontier(
            solutions=tuple(solutions),
            minimum_return=minimum_return,
            maximum_return=maximum_return,
            failed_targets=tuple(failures),
        )

    def _max_sharpe_solution(
        self,
        prepared: _PreparedInputs,
        risk_free_rate: Optional[float],
        prev_weights: Optional[Mapping[str, float]],
        max_turnover: float,
        crisis_regime: bool,
        bounds: Optional[Mapping[str, tuple[float, float]]],
    ) -> PortfolioSolution:
        risk_free = (
            self.config.risk_free_rate
            if risk_free_rate is None
            else _optimizer_finite(risk_free_rate, "risk_free_rate")
        )
        scipy_bounds = self._bounds(prepared.tickers, bounds)
        previous, turnover_limit = self._turnover_inputs(
            prepared.tickers,
            prev_weights,
            max_turnover,
            crisis_regime,
            scipy_bounds,
        )

        def negative_sharpe(weights: np.ndarray) -> float:
            expected = float(weights @ prepared.mean)
            variance = float(weights @ prepared.covariance @ weights)
            volatility = math.sqrt(max(variance, 0.0))
            if volatility <= _EPSILON:
                return 1e12
            return -(expected - risk_free) / volatility

        result = self._solve(
            negative_sharpe,
            prepared,
            scipy_bounds,
            previous,
            turnover_limit,
            extra_constraints=(),
        )
        return self._solution(
            "max_sharpe",
            result,
            prepared,
            risk_free,
            previous,
            prepared.warnings,
        )

    def _minimum_variance_solution(
        self,
        prepared: _PreparedInputs,
        target_return: Optional[float],
        risk_free_rate: Optional[float],
        prev_weights: Optional[Mapping[str, float]],
        max_turnover: float,
        crisis_regime: bool,
        bounds: Optional[Mapping[str, tuple[float, float]]],
        *,
        method: str,
    ) -> PortfolioSolution:
        scipy_bounds = self._bounds(prepared.tickers, bounds)
        previous, turnover_limit = self._turnover_inputs(
            prepared.tickers,
            prev_weights,
            max_turnover,
            crisis_regime,
            scipy_bounds,
        )
        constraints: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        if target_return is not None:
            minimum, maximum = self._feasible_return_range(
                prepared.mean, scipy_bounds, previous, turnover_limit
            )
            if target_return < minimum - 1e-8 or target_return > maximum + 1e-8:
                raise OptimizationInfeasibleError(
                    f"target_return {target_return:.4%} is outside the feasible "
                    f"range [{minimum:.4%}, {maximum:.4%}]"
                )
            constraints.append(
                {
                    "type": "ineq",
                    "fun": lambda weights, target=target_return: float(
                        weights @ prepared.mean - target
                    ),
                }
            )

        def variance(weights: np.ndarray) -> float:
            return float(weights @ prepared.covariance @ weights)

        result = self._solve(
            variance,
            prepared,
            scipy_bounds,
            previous,
            turnover_limit,
            extra_constraints=tuple(constraints),
        )
        risk_free = (
            self.config.risk_free_rate
            if risk_free_rate is None
            else _optimizer_finite(risk_free_rate, "risk_free_rate")
        )
        return self._solution(
            method,
            result,
            prepared,
            risk_free,
            previous,
            prepared.warnings,
        )

    def _prepare_returns(self, returns_df: pd.DataFrame) -> _PreparedInputs:
        if not isinstance(returns_df, pd.DataFrame):
            raise OptimizationDataError("returns_df must be a pandas DataFrame")
        if returns_df.empty or returns_df.shape[1] == 0:
            raise OptimizationDataError("returns_df cannot be empty")
        tickers = tuple(str(column).strip().upper() for column in returns_df.columns)
        if any(not ticker for ticker in tickers):
            raise OptimizationDataError("return column names cannot be empty")
        if len(set(tickers)) != len(tickers):
            raise OptimizationDataError(
                "return columns collide after ticker normalization"
            )
        numeric = returns_df.apply(pd.to_numeric, errors="coerce")
        numeric.columns = tickers
        original_rows = len(numeric)
        numeric = numeric.replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan).dropna(how="any")
        if len(numeric) < self.config.minimum_observations:
            raise OptimizationDataError(
                f"at least {self.config.minimum_observations} complete observations "
                f"are required; received {len(numeric)}"
            )
        variances = numeric.var(ddof=1)
        zero_variance = list(variances.index[variances <= _EPSILON])
        if zero_variance:
            raise OptimizationDataError(
                "zero-variance assets cannot be optimized: "
                + ", ".join(zero_variance)
            )
        mean = numeric.mean().to_numpy(dtype=float) * self.config.periods_per_year
        sample_covariance = (
            numeric.cov().to_numpy(dtype=float) * self.config.periods_per_year
        )
        diagonal = np.diag(np.diag(sample_covariance))
        shrink = self.config.covariance_shrinkage
        covariance = (
            (1.0 - shrink) * sample_covariance + shrink * diagonal
        )
        covariance = self._nearest_psd(covariance)
        condition = float(np.linalg.cond(covariance))
        warnings: list[str] = []
        removed = original_rows - len(numeric)
        if removed:
            warnings.append(
                f"{removed} rows with missing or non-finite returns were removed."
            )
        if len(numeric) < 250:
            warnings.append(
                "Less than roughly one trading year is available; estimates are unstable."
            )
        if condition > 1e6:
            warnings.append(
                f"Covariance remains ill-conditioned ({condition:.2e}) after shrinkage."
            )
        return _PreparedInputs(
            returns=numeric,
            tickers=tickers,
            mean=mean,
            covariance=covariance,
            condition_number=condition,
            warnings=tuple(warnings),
        )

    def _nearest_psd(self, covariance: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        symmetric = 0.5 * (covariance + covariance.T)
        eigenvalues, eigenvectors = np.linalg.eigh(symmetric)
        scale = max(float(np.max(np.abs(eigenvalues))), 1.0)
        floor = self.config.eigenvalue_floor * scale
        clipped = np.maximum(eigenvalues, floor)
        result = (eigenvectors * clipped) @ eigenvectors.T
        return 0.5 * (result + result.T)

    def _bounds(
        self,
        tickers: tuple[str, ...],
        custom: Optional[Mapping[str, tuple[float, float]]],
    ) -> tuple[tuple[float, float], ...]:
        normalized: dict[str, tuple[float, float]] = {}
        if custom is not None:
            if not isinstance(custom, Mapping):
                raise OptimizationDataError("bounds must be a mapping")
            for raw_ticker, raw_pair in custom.items():
                ticker = str(raw_ticker).strip().upper()
                if ticker not in tickers:
                    raise OptimizationDataError(
                        f"bound supplied for unknown ticker: {ticker}"
                    )
                if not isinstance(raw_pair, (tuple, list)) or len(raw_pair) != 2:
                    raise OptimizationDataError(
                        f"bound for {ticker} must be a (lower, upper) pair"
                    )
            lower = _optimizer_finite(raw_pair[0], f"bounds[{ticker}].lower")
            upper = _optimizer_finite(raw_pair[1], f"bounds[{ticker}].upper")
                if not 0 <= lower <= upper <= 1:
                    raise OptimizationDataError(
                        f"bounds for {ticker} must satisfy 0 <= lower <= upper <= 1"
                    )
                normalized[ticker] = (lower, upper)
        result = tuple(
            normalized.get(
                ticker,
                (
                    self.config.default_lower_bound,
                    self.config.default_upper_bound,
                ),
            )
            for ticker in tickers
        )
        lower_sum = sum(item[0] for item in result)
        upper_sum = sum(item[1] for item in result)
        if lower_sum > 1.0 + self.config.weight_tolerance or upper_sum < 1.0 - self.config.weight_tolerance:
            raise OptimizationInfeasibleError(
                f"weight bounds cannot sum to one (lower sum={lower_sum:.4f}, "
                f"upper sum={upper_sum:.4f})"
            )
        return result

    def _turnover_inputs(
        self,
        tickers: tuple[str, ...],
        previous: Optional[Mapping[str, float]],
        max_turnover: float,
        crisis_regime: bool,
        bounds: tuple[tuple[float, float], ...],
    ) -> tuple[Optional[np.ndarray], Optional[float]]:
        if previous is None:
            return None, None
        normalized = self._validated_weight_mapping(previous, "prev_weights")
        unknown = sorted(set(normalized) - set(tickers))
        if unknown:
            raise OptimizationDataError(
                "prev_weights contains assets absent from returns_df: "
                + ", ".join(unknown)
            )
        vector = np.array([normalized.get(ticker, 0.0) for ticker in tickers])
        if any(
            vector[index] < lower - self.config.weight_tolerance
            or vector[index] > upper + self.config.weight_tolerance
            for index, (lower, upper) in enumerate(bounds)
        ):
            raise OptimizationInfeasibleError(
                "prev_weights violates the configured asset bounds"
            )
        if crisis_regime:
            return vector, None
            limit = _optimizer_finite(max_turnover, "max_turnover")
        if not 0 <= limit <= 1:
            raise OptimizationDataError("max_turnover must be in [0, 1]")
        return vector, limit

    @staticmethod
    def _validated_weight_mapping(
        weights: Mapping[str, float], name: str
    ) -> dict[str, float]:
        if not isinstance(weights, Mapping) or not weights:
            raise OptimizationDataError(f"{name} must be a non-empty mapping")
        normalized: dict[str, float] = {}
        for raw_ticker, raw_weight in weights.items():
            ticker = str(raw_ticker).strip().upper()
            if not ticker:
                raise OptimizationDataError(f"{name} ticker cannot be empty")
            if ticker in normalized:
                raise OptimizationDataError(
                    f"{name} contains duplicate ticker {ticker}"
                )
            weight = _optimizer_finite(raw_weight, f"{name}[{ticker}]")
            if weight < 0:
                raise OptimizationDataError(f"{name} cannot contain negative weights")
            normalized[ticker] = weight
        total = sum(normalized.values())
        if abs(total - 1.0) > 1e-7:
            raise OptimizationDataError(
                f"{name} must sum to 1.0; received {total:.10f}"
            )
        return normalized

    def _initial_points(
        self,
        prepared: _PreparedInputs,
        bounds: tuple[tuple[float, float], ...],
        previous: Optional[np.ndarray],
    ) -> list[np.ndarray]:
        points: list[np.ndarray] = []
        if previous is not None:
            points.append(previous.copy())
        equal = self._project_to_bounded_simplex(
            np.full(len(bounds), 1.0 / len(bounds)), bounds
        )
        points.append(equal)
        inverse_volatility = 1.0 / np.sqrt(np.diag(prepared.covariance))
        inverse_volatility /= inverse_volatility.sum()
        points.append(self._project_to_bounded_simplex(inverse_volatility, bounds))
        points.append(self._linear_extreme(prepared.mean, bounds, maximize=True))
        points.append(self._linear_extreme(prepared.mean, bounds, maximize=False))
        rng = np.random.default_rng(self.config.random_seed)
        for _ in range(self.config.random_restarts):
            point = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(len(bounds)))
            points.append(self._project_to_bounded_simplex(point, bounds))
        unique: list[np.ndarray] = []
        for point in points:
            if not any(np.allclose(point, prior, atol=1e-10) for prior in unique):
                unique.append(point)
        return unique

    @staticmethod
    def _linear_extreme(
        coefficients: np.ndarray,
        bounds: tuple[tuple[float, float], ...],
        *,
        maximize: bool,
    ) -> np.ndarray:
        """Exact greedy solution for a bounded-simplex linear objective."""

        result = np.array([item[0] for item in bounds], dtype=float)
        remaining = 1.0 - float(result.sum())
        order = np.argsort(coefficients)
        if maximize:
            order = order[::-1]
        for index in order:
            capacity = bounds[int(index)][1] - result[int(index)]
            addition = min(remaining, capacity)
            result[int(index)] += addition
            remaining -= addition
            if remaining <= 1e-12:
                break
        if remaining > 1e-8:
            raise OptimizationInfeasibleError(
                "bounds cannot form a fully invested portfolio"
            )
        return result

    @staticmethod
    def _project_to_bounded_simplex(
        values: np.ndarray,
        bounds: tuple[tuple[float, float], ...],
    ) -> np.ndarray:
        lower = np.array([item[0] for item in bounds], dtype=float)
        upper = np.array([item[1] for item in bounds], dtype=float)
        # Bisection projection: clip(v - lambda, lower, upper), sum = 1.
        left = float(np.min(values - upper)) - 1.0
        right = float(np.max(values - lower)) + 1.0
        for _ in range(200):
            midpoint = 0.5 * (left + right)
            candidate = np.clip(values - midpoint, lower, upper)
            if candidate.sum() > 1.0:
                left = midpoint
            else:
                right = midpoint
        projected = np.clip(values - 0.5 * (left + right), lower, upper)
        if abs(float(projected.sum()) - 1.0) > 1e-8:
            raise OptimizationInfeasibleError("failed to project weights to bounds")
        return projected

    def _solve(
        self,
        objective: Callable[[np.ndarray], float],
        prepared: _PreparedInputs,
        bounds: tuple[tuple[float, float], ...],
        previous: Optional[np.ndarray],
        turnover_limit: Optional[float],
        extra_constraints: tuple[dict[str, Any], ...],
    ) -> OptimizeResult:
        if not SCIPY_AVAILABLE:
            return self._solve_fallback(
                objective,
                prepared,
                bounds,
                previous,
                turnover_limit,
                extra_constraints,
            )
        constraints: list[dict[str, Any]] = [
            {"type": "eq", "fun": lambda weights: float(weights.sum() - 1.0)}
        ]
        constraints.extend(extra_constraints)
        if previous is not None and turnover_limit is not None:
            constraints.append(
                {
                    "type": "ineq",
                    "fun": lambda weights, old=previous, limit=turnover_limit: float(
                        limit - 0.5 * np.abs(weights - old).sum()
                    ),
                }
            )
        successes: list[OptimizeResult] = []
        messages: list[str] = []
        for initial in self._initial_points(prepared, bounds, previous):
            result = minimize(
                objective,
                initial,
                method="SLSQP",
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=constraints,
                options={
                    "ftol": self.config.solver_tolerance,
                    "maxiter": self.config.solver_max_iterations,
                    "disp": False,
                },
            )
            messages.append(str(result.message))
            if result.success and self._is_feasible(
                result.x,
                bounds,
                previous,
                turnover_limit,
                extra_constraints,
            ):
                successes.append(result)
        if not successes:
            unique_messages = "; ".join(dict.fromkeys(messages))
            raise OptimizationInfeasibleError(
                "optimizer found no feasible solution"
                + (f": {unique_messages}" if unique_messages else "")
            )
        return min(successes, key=lambda item: float(item.fun))

    def _solve_fallback(
        self,
        objective: Callable[[np.ndarray], float],
        prepared: _PreparedInputs,
        bounds: tuple[tuple[float, float], ...],
        previous: Optional[np.ndarray],
        turnover_limit: Optional[float],
        extra_constraints: tuple[dict[str, Any], ...],
    ) -> _FallbackResult:
        """Deterministic pairwise coordinate solver used when SciPy is absent.

        Every candidate remains on the bounded simplex. Turnover and any
        target-return inequality are checked before evaluation, so the fallback
        never reports an infeasible portfolio as successful.
        """

        starts = self._initial_points(prepared, bounds, previous)
        feasible_starts: list[np.ndarray] = []
        for point in starts:
            candidate = point.copy()
            if previous is not None and turnover_limit is not None:
                turnover = 0.5 * float(np.abs(candidate - previous).sum())
                if turnover > turnover_limit and turnover > _EPSILON:
                    candidate = previous + (turnover_limit / turnover) * (
                        candidate - previous
                    )
            if self._is_feasible(
                candidate,
                bounds,
                previous,
                turnover_limit,
                extra_constraints,
            ):
                feasible_starts.append(candidate)
        if not feasible_starts:
            raise OptimizationInfeasibleError(
                "fallback optimizer found no feasible starting point"
            )

        best_weights: Optional[np.ndarray] = None
        best_value = math.inf
        tolerance = max(self.config.solver_tolerance, 1e-12)
        max_passes = min(self.config.solver_max_iterations, 5_000)
        for start in feasible_starts:
            weights = start.copy()
            value = float(objective(weights))
            step = 0.25
            passes = 0
            while step > 1e-8 and passes < max_passes:
                passes += 1
                improved = False
                candidate_best = weights
                candidate_value = value
                for source in range(len(weights)):
                    available = weights[source] - bounds[source][0]
                    if available <= 1e-14:
                        continue
                    for destination in range(len(weights)):
                        if source == destination:
                            continue
                        capacity = bounds[destination][1] - weights[destination]
                        move = min(step, available, capacity)
                        if move <= 1e-14:
                            continue
                        candidate = weights.copy()
                        candidate[source] -= move
                        candidate[destination] += move
                        if not self._is_feasible(
                            candidate,
                            bounds,
                            previous,
                            turnover_limit,
                            extra_constraints,
                        ):
                            continue
                        candidate_objective = float(objective(candidate))
                        if candidate_objective < candidate_value - tolerance:
                            candidate_best = candidate
                            candidate_value = candidate_objective
                            improved = True
                if improved:
                    weights = candidate_best
                    value = candidate_value
                else:
                    step *= 0.5
            if value < best_value:
                best_weights = weights
                best_value = value
        if best_weights is None or not math.isfinite(best_value):
            raise OptimizationInfeasibleError(
                "fallback optimizer did not produce a finite solution"
            )
        return _FallbackResult(
            x=best_weights,
            fun=best_value,
            success=True,
            message="Deterministic bounded coordinate solver converged (SciPy unavailable)",
        )

    def _is_feasible(
        self,
        weights: np.ndarray,
        bounds: tuple[tuple[float, float], ...],
        previous: Optional[np.ndarray],
        turnover_limit: Optional[float],
        extra_constraints: tuple[dict[str, Any], ...],
    ) -> bool:
        tolerance = max(self.config.weight_tolerance, 1e-6)
        if abs(float(weights.sum()) - 1.0) > tolerance:
            return False
        if any(
            weights[index] < lower - tolerance
            or weights[index] > upper + tolerance
            for index, (lower, upper) in enumerate(bounds)
        ):
            return False
        if previous is not None and turnover_limit is not None:
            if 0.5 * float(np.abs(weights - previous).sum()) > turnover_limit + tolerance:
                return False
        return all(float(constraint["fun"](weights)) >= -tolerance for constraint in extra_constraints)

    def _solution(
        self,
        method: str,
        result: OptimizeResult,
        prepared: _PreparedInputs,
        risk_free_rate: float,
        previous: Optional[np.ndarray],
        warnings: tuple[str, ...],
    ) -> PortfolioSolution:
        weights = np.asarray(result.x, dtype=float)
        weights[np.abs(weights) < 1e-12] = 0.0
        weights /= weights.sum()
        expected = float(weights @ prepared.mean)
        variance = float(weights @ prepared.covariance @ weights)
        volatility = math.sqrt(max(variance, 0.0))
        sharpe = (
            (expected - risk_free_rate) / volatility
            if volatility > _EPSILON
            else 0.0
        )
        marginal_variance = prepared.covariance @ weights
        if variance > _EPSILON:
            contributions = weights * marginal_variance / variance
        else:
            contributions = np.zeros_like(weights)
        turnover = (
            0.5 * float(np.abs(weights - previous).sum())
            if previous is not None
            else None
        )
        assumptions = (
            "Expected returns are annualized historical arithmetic means unless "
            "Black-Litterman is used.",
            "Covariance is annualized, shrunk toward its diagonal, and projected PSD.",
            "Weights are long-only, fully invested, and exclude transaction costs, "
            "taxes, liquidity, market impact, and lot-size constraints.",
            "Optimized weights are highly sensitive to estimation error and should "
            "be stress-tested out of sample.",
        )
        solution_warnings = list(warnings)
        if not SCIPY_AVAILABLE:
            solution_warnings.append(
                "SciPy is unavailable; the deterministic bounded coordinate "
                "solver was used."
            )
        return PortfolioSolution(
            method=method,
            weights=MappingProxyType(
                {
                    ticker: float(weight)
                    for ticker, weight in zip(prepared.tickers, weights)
                }
            ),
            expected_return=expected,
            expected_volatility=volatility,
            sharpe_ratio=sharpe,
            risk_contributions=MappingProxyType(
                {
                    ticker: float(value)
                    for ticker, value in zip(prepared.tickers, contributions)
                }
            ),
            one_way_turnover=turnover,
            observations=len(prepared.returns),
            objective_value=float(result.fun),
            solver_message=str(result.message),
            covariance_condition_number=prepared.condition_number,
            warnings=tuple(dict.fromkeys(solution_warnings)),
            assumptions=assumptions,
        )

    def _feasible_return_range(
        self,
        mean: np.ndarray,
        bounds: tuple[tuple[float, float], ...],
        previous: Optional[np.ndarray] = None,
        turnover_limit: Optional[float] = None,
    ) -> tuple[float, float]:
        dummy = _PreparedInputs(
            returns=pd.DataFrame(np.zeros((20, len(mean)))),
            tickers=tuple(str(index) for index in range(len(mean))),
            mean=mean,
            covariance=np.eye(len(mean)),
            condition_number=1.0,
            warnings=(),
        )
        minimum_result = self._solve(
            lambda weights: float(weights @ mean),
            dummy,
            bounds,
            previous,
            turnover_limit,
            extra_constraints=(),
        )
        maximum_result = self._solve(
            lambda weights: -float(weights @ mean),
            dummy,
            bounds,
            previous,
            turnover_limit,
            extra_constraints=(),
        )
        return (
            float(minimum_result.x @ mean),
            float(maximum_result.x @ mean),
        )

    def _market_weights(
        self,
        tickers: tuple[str, ...],
        market_weights: Optional[Mapping[str, float]],
    ) -> np.ndarray:
        if market_weights is None:
            return np.full(len(tickers), 1.0 / len(tickers))
        normalized = self._validated_weight_mapping(
            market_weights, "market_weights"
        )
        if set(normalized) != set(tickers):
            missing = sorted(set(tickers) - set(normalized))
            extra = sorted(set(normalized) - set(tickers))
            details = []
            if missing:
                details.append("missing " + ", ".join(missing))
            if extra:
                details.append("unknown " + ", ".join(extra))
            raise OptimizationDataError(
                "market_weights universe mismatch: " + "; ".join(details)
            )
        return np.array([normalized[ticker] for ticker in tickers], dtype=float)

    def _posterior(
        self,
        prepared: _PreparedInputs,
        prior: np.ndarray,
        views: Optional[Mapping[str, float]],
        confidences: Optional[Mapping[str, float]],
        tau: float,
        *,
        equal_market_weights_assumed: bool,
    ) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray, tuple[str, ...]]:
        if not views:
            warnings = [
                "No Black-Litterman views supplied; posterior equals the "
                "equilibrium prior."
            ]
            if equal_market_weights_assumed:
                warnings.append(
                    "Equal market weights were assumed because market_weights "
                    "was not supplied."
                )
            return (
                prior,
                prepared.covariance * (1.0 + tau),
                tuple(warnings),
            )
        normalized_views: dict[str, float] = {}
        for raw_ticker, raw_return in views.items():
            ticker = str(raw_ticker).strip().upper()
            if ticker not in prepared.tickers:
                raise OptimizationDataError(
                    f"view supplied for unknown ticker: {ticker}"
                )
            if ticker in normalized_views:
                raise OptimizationDataError(f"duplicate view ticker: {ticker}")
            normalized_views[ticker] = _optimizer_finite(
                raw_return, f"views[{ticker}]"
            )
        normalized_confidence: dict[str, float] = {}
        if confidences is not None:
            for raw_ticker, raw_value in confidences.items():
                ticker = str(raw_ticker).strip().upper()
                if ticker not in normalized_views:
                    raise OptimizationDataError(
                        f"confidence supplied without a matching view: {ticker}"
                    )
            value = _optimizer_finite(raw_value, f"view_confidences[{ticker}]")
                if not 0 < value < 1:
                    raise OptimizationDataError(
                        "view confidences must be strictly between 0 and 1"
                    )
                normalized_confidence[ticker] = value
        count = len(normalized_views)
        projection = np.zeros((count, len(prepared.tickers)))
        q = np.zeros(count)
        confidence_values = np.zeros(count)
        for row, (ticker, expected_return) in enumerate(normalized_views.items()):
            projection[row, prepared.tickers.index(ticker)] = 1.0
            q[row] = expected_return
            confidence_values[row] = normalized_confidence.get(ticker, 0.50)
        tau_covariance = tau * prepared.covariance
        view_variance = np.diag(projection @ tau_covariance @ projection.T)
        omega_diagonal = view_variance * (1.0 - confidence_values) / confidence_values
        omega = np.diag(np.maximum(omega_diagonal, self.config.eigenvalue_floor))
        middle = projection @ tau_covariance @ projection.T + omega
        try:
            gain = np.linalg.solve(
                middle,
                projection @ tau_covariance,
            ).T
        except np.linalg.LinAlgError as exc:
            raise OptimizationError(
                "Black-Litterman posterior system is singular"
            ) from exc
        posterior_mean = prior + gain @ (q - projection @ prior)
        posterior_covariance = (
            prepared.covariance
            + tau_covariance
            - gain @ projection @ tau_covariance
        )
        warnings = [
            "Black-Litterman view returns are annual decimal returns.",
            "View confidence controls relative uncertainty and is not a probability "
            "that the view will be correct.",
        ]
        if equal_market_weights_assumed:
            warnings.append(
                "Equal market weights were assumed because market_weights was not supplied."
            )
        return posterior_mean, posterior_covariance, tuple(warnings)

    def build_returns_matrix(
        self, price_data: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame]
    ) -> pd.DataFrame:
        if not isinstance(price_data, Mapping) or not price_data:
            raise OptimizationDataError("price_data must be a non-empty mapping")
        closes: list[pd.Series] = []
        seen: set[str] = set()
        for raw_ticker, frame in price_data.items():
            ticker = str(raw_ticker).strip().upper()
            if not ticker or ticker in seen:
                raise OptimizationDataError(
                    f"invalid or duplicate price ticker: {ticker!r}"
                )
            seen.add(ticker)
            if not isinstance(frame, pd.DataFrame) or "Close" not in frame.columns:
                raise OptimizationDataError(
                    f"{ticker}: price frame requires a Close column"
                )
            series = pd.to_numeric(frame["Close"], errors="coerce")
            series = series[~series.index.duplicated(keep="last")].sort_index()
            series = series.loc[
                series.notna() & np.isfinite(series) & (series > 0)
            ].rename(ticker)
            if len(series) < self.config.minimum_observations + 1:
                raise OptimizationDataError(
                    f"{ticker}: insufficient valid prices"
                )
            closes.append(series)
        matrix = pd.concat(closes, axis=1, join="inner").dropna(how="any")
        returns = matrix.pct_change(fill_method=None).dropna(how="any")
        # Reuse the same validation path and return its cleaned frame.
        return self._prepare_returns(returns).returns

    @staticmethod
    def format_solution(solution: PortfolioSolution) -> str:
        lines = [
            "=" * 70,
            f"PORTFOLIO OPTIMIZATION - {solution.method.upper()}",
            "=" * 70,
            f"Observations             : {solution.observations}",
            f"Expected annual return   : {solution.expected_return:.2%}",
            f"Expected annual volatility: {solution.expected_volatility:.2%}",
            f"Historical Sharpe        : {solution.sharpe_ratio:.3f}",
            f"One-way turnover         : "
            + (
                f"{solution.one_way_turnover:.2%}"
                if solution.one_way_turnover is not None
                else "N/A"
            ),
            "",
            "Weights and variance-risk contributions:",
        ]
        for ticker, weight in sorted(
            solution.weights.items(), key=lambda item: item[1], reverse=True
        ):
            contribution = solution.risk_contributions.get(ticker, 0.0)
            lines.append(
                f"  {ticker:<12} weight {weight:>8.2%} | risk {contribution:>8.2%}"
            )
        if solution.warnings:
            lines.extend(["", "Warnings:"])
            lines.extend(f"  - {item}" for item in solution.warnings)
        lines.extend(["", "Assumptions and limitations:"])
        lines.extend(f"  - {item}" for item in solution.assumptions)
        lines.append("=" * 70)
        return "\n".join(lines)

    "OptimizationError",
    "OptimizationInfeasibleError",
    "OptimizerConfig",
    "PortfolioOptimizer",
    "PortfolioSolution",
]

try:
    from vaderSentiment.vaderSentiment import SentimentIntensityAnalyzer

    _VADER_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
    SentimentIntensityAnalyzer = None  # type: ignore[assignment]
    _VADER_AVAILABLE = False

except ImportError:

LOGGER = logging.getLogger(f"{APP_NAME}.agents")
_EPSILON: Final = 1e-12
_REGIME_BIAS: Final = MappingProxyType(
    {
        "bull": ("overweight", 1.0),
        "bear": ("underweight", -0.7),
        "sideways": ("neutral", 0.0),
        "high_volatility": ("underweight", -0.5),
        "crisis": ("strongly_underweight", -1.0),
    }
)
_VALID_METHODS: Final = frozenset(
    {"auto", "max_sharpe", "min_volatility", "risk_parity", "black_litterman"}
)
_TOKEN_PATTERN: Final = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z][A-Za-z'-]*")


class MultiAgentError(SmartInvestError):
    """Base error for Module 11."""


class AgentInputError(MultiAgentError):
    """Raised when an agent receives inconsistent evidence."""


class AgentExecutionError(MultiAgentError):
    """Raised when a required agent fails."""


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class MultiAgentConfig:
    top_n: int = 5
    common_alpha_horizon_days: int = 20
    minimum_prediction_quality: float = 0.35
    actionable_prediction_quality: float = 0.50
    minimum_alpha_return: float = 0.005
    maximum_annualized_view: float = 1.50
    maximum_sentiment_view_adjustment: float = 0.03
    risk_reduce_volatility: float = 0.25
    risk_critical_volatility: float = 0.40
    default_max_turnover: float = 0.15
    max_workers: int = 4
    history_limit: int = 100
    strict_agent_failures: bool = True

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not 1 <= int(self.top_n) <= 100:
            raise ValueError("top_n must be in [1, 100]")
        if not 1 <= int(self.common_alpha_horizon_days) <= 365:
            raise ValueError("common_alpha_horizon_days must be in [1, 365]")
        for name in (
            "minimum_prediction_quality",
            "actionable_prediction_quality",
        ):
            value = float(getattr(self, name))
            if not 0 <= value <= 1:
                raise ValueError(f"{name} must be in [0, 1]")
        if self.actionable_prediction_quality < self.minimum_prediction_quality:
            raise ValueError(
                "actionable_prediction_quality cannot be below minimum_prediction_quality"
            )
        if not 0 <= float(self.minimum_alpha_return) <= 1:
            raise ValueError("minimum_alpha_return must be in [0, 1]")
        if float(self.maximum_annualized_view) <= 0:
            raise ValueError("maximum_annualized_view must be positive")
        if not 0 <= float(self.maximum_sentiment_view_adjustment) <= 0.25:
            raise ValueError(
                "maximum_sentiment_view_adjustment must be in [0, 0.25]"
            )
        if not 0 < self.risk_reduce_volatility < self.risk_critical_volatility:
            raise ValueError(
                "risk volatility thresholds must be positive and increasing"
            )
        if not 0 <= float(self.default_max_turnover) <= 1:
            raise ValueError("default_max_turnover must be in [0, 1]")
        if not 1 <= int(self.max_workers) <= 16:
            raise ValueError("max_workers must be in [1, 16]")
        if not 1 <= int(self.history_limit) <= 10_000:
            raise ValueError("history_limit must be in [1, 10000]")


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class AgentStatus:
    name: str
    status: str
    duration_ms: float
    error: Optional[str] = None


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ConsensusResult:
    stance: str
    score: float
    confidence: float
    agreement: float
    risk_veto: bool
    supporting_agents: tuple[str, ...]
    opposing_agents: tuple[str, ...]
    conflicts: tuple[str, ...]
    rationale: tuple[str, ...]
