    run_id: str
    timestamp_utc: str
    input_fingerprint: str
    requested_tickers: tuple[str, ...]
    usable_tickers: tuple[str, ...]
    agent_statuses: Mapping[str, AgentStatus]
    consensus: ConsensusResult
    warnings: tuple[str, ...]
    total_duration_ms: float


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class MultiAgentAnalysis:
    signals: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]]
    diagnostics: MultiAgentDiagnostics


def _agent_finite(value: Any, name: str) -> float:
    try:
        result = float(value)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise AgentInputError(f"{name} must be numeric") from exc
    if not math.isfinite(result):
        raise AgentInputError(f"{name} must be finite")
    return result


def _safe_mean(values: Sequence[float]) -> float:
    return float(np.mean(values)) if values else 0.0


class BaseAgent:
    """Thread-safe base class retaining a defensive copy of its last signal."""

    def __init__(self, name: str) -> None:
        self.name = str(name).strip()
        if not self.name:
            raise ValueError("agent name cannot be empty")
        self._last_signal: Optional[dict[str, Any]] = None
        self._lock = threading.RLock()

    @property
    def last_signal(self) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        with self._lock:
            return _deep_copy(self._last_signal)

    def _record(self, signal: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        materialized = _deep_copy(dict(signal))
        with self._lock:
            self._last_signal = materialized
        return _deep_copy(materialized)

    def analyze(self, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
        raise NotImplementedError


class MacroAgent(BaseAgent):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__("MacroAgent")

    def analyze(
        self,
        market_overview: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]],
        regime: MarketRegime,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if not isinstance(regime, MarketRegime):
            raise AgentInputError("regime must be a MarketRegime")
        regime_name = str(regime.name).strip().lower()
        if regime_name not in _REGIME_BIAS:
            raise AgentInputError(f"unsupported market regime: {regime.name!r}")
        confidence = _agent_finite(regime.confidence, "regime.confidence")
        if not 0 <= confidence <= 1:
            raise AgentInputError("regime.confidence must be in [0, 1]")
        returns: list[float] = []
        volatilities: list[float] = []
        for ticker, evidence in market_overview.items():
            if not isinstance(evidence, Mapping):
                continue
            try:
                returns.append(_agent_finite(evidence.get("return_1m"), f"{ticker}.return_1m"))
            except AgentInputError:
                pass
            try:
                volatilities.append(
                    _agent_finite(evidence.get("volatility_20d"), f"{ticker}.volatility_20d")
                )
            except AgentInputError:
                pass
        bias, numeric_bias = _REGIME_BIAS[regime_name]
        signal = {
            "agent": self.name,
            "status": "ok",
            "regime": regime_name,
            "regime_confidence": confidence,
            "equity_bias": bias,
            "bias_score": numeric_bias * confidence,
            "average_return_1m": _safe_mean(returns),
            "average_volatility_20d": _safe_mean(volatilities),
            "coverage": len(returns),
            "commentary": (
                f"Regime is {regime_name.replace('_', ' ')} with "
                f"{confidence:.0%} confidence. Average available 1-month return "
                f"is {_safe_mean(returns):+.2%}; equity bias is {bias}."
            ),
            "confidence_is_probability": False,
        }
        return self._record(signal)


class AlphaAgent(BaseAgent):
    """Rank horizon-normalized predictions using model quality as evidence weight."""

    def __init__(self, config: Optional[MultiAgentConfig] = None) -> None:
        super().__init__("AlphaAgent")
        self.config = config or MultiAgentConfig()

    def analyze(
        self,
        predictions: Mapping[str, EnsemblePrediction],
        top_n: Optional[int] = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if not isinstance(predictions, Mapping):
            raise AgentInputError("predictions must be a mapping")
        limit = self.config.top_n if top_n is None else int(top_n)
        if limit <= 0:
            raise AgentInputError("top_n must be positive")
        rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        rejected: dict[str, str] = {}
        for raw_ticker, prediction in predictions.items():
            ticker = str(raw_ticker).strip().upper()
            if not isinstance(prediction, EnsemblePrediction):
                rejected[ticker or str(raw_ticker)] = "invalid prediction object"
                continue
            if ticker != str(prediction.ticker).strip().upper():
                rejected[ticker] = "mapping key and prediction ticker differ"
                continue
            horizon = int(prediction.horizon_days)
            predicted_return = _agent_finite(
                prediction.ensemble_pred, f"{ticker}.ensemble_pred"
            )
            quality = _agent_finite(prediction.confidence, f"{ticker}.confidence")
            if horizon <= 0 or predicted_return <= -1 or not 0 <= quality <= 1:
                rejected[ticker] = "invalid horizon, return, or model quality"
                continue
            daily_log_return = math.log1p(predicted_return) / horizon
            common_return = math.expm1(
                daily_log_return * self.config.common_alpha_horizon_days
            )
            annualized = math.expm1(daily_log_return * 252)
            annualized = float(
                np.clip(
                    annualized,
                    -0.95,
                    self.config.maximum_annualized_view,
                )
            )
            quality_weight = (
                0.0
                if quality < self.config.minimum_prediction_quality
                else quality
            )
            score = common_return * quality_weight
            rows.append(
                {
                    "ticker": ticker,
                    "raw_return": predicted_return,
                    "horizon_days": horizon,
                    "normalized_return": common_return,
                    "expected_return_annual": annualized,
                    "model_quality": quality,
                    "score": score,
                    "direction": (
                        "up"
                        if common_return >= self.config.minimum_alpha_return
                        else "down"
                        if common_return <= -self.config.minimum_alpha_return
                        else "neutral"
                    ),
                    "actionable": (
                        quality >= self.config.actionable_prediction_quality
                        and abs(common_return) >= self.config.minimum_alpha_return
                    ),
                }
            )
        ranked = sorted(rows, key=lambda item: item["score"], reverse=True)
        bullish = [
            item for item in ranked if item["direction"] == "up" and item["actionable"]
        ]
        bearish = [
            item
            for item in reversed(ranked)
            if item["direction"] == "down" and item["actionable"]
        ]
        signal = {
            "agent": self.name,
            "status": "ok",
            "common_horizon_days": self.config.common_alpha_horizon_days,
            "top_picks": [item["ticker"] for item in bullish[:limit]],
            "avoid_list": [item["ticker"] for item in bearish[:limit]],
            "avg_ensemble_pred": _safe_mean(
                [float(item["normalized_return"]) for item in rows]
            ),
            "quality_weighted_score": _safe_mean(
                [float(item["score"]) for item in rows]
            ),
            "bullish_count": sum(item["direction"] == "up" for item in rows),
            "bearish_count": sum(item["direction"] == "down" for item in rows),
            "neutral_count": sum(item["direction"] == "neutral" for item in rows),
            "predictions": {item["ticker"]: item for item in rows},
            "rejected": rejected,
            "confidence_is_probability": False,
        }
        return self._record(signal)


class SentimentAgent(BaseAgent):
    """Analyze independent text sentiment and price momentum as separate evidence."""

    _FIN_LEXICON: Final = MappingProxyType(
        {
            "bullish": 3.5,
            "rally": 2.8,
            "surge": 2.5,
            "breakout": 2.2,
            "outperform": 2.0,
            "upgrade": 2.0,
            "beat": 1.8,
            "recovery": 1.6,
            "buy": 1.5,
            "bearish": -3.5,
            "crash": -3.8,
            "crashing": -3.8,
            "collapse": -3.5,
            "plunge": -3.0,
            "selloff": -2.8,
            "downgrade": -2.5,
            "miss": -2.0,
            "underperform": -2.2,
            "loss": -1.8,
            "sell": -1.5,
            "recession": -2.5,
            "default": -3.0,
            "uncertainty": -0.8,
            "caution": -0.6,
        }
    )

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__("SentimentAgent")
        self._vader: Optional[Any] = None
        if _VADER_AVAILABLE:
            try:
                self._vader = SentimentIntensityAnalyzer()
                self._vader.lexicon.update(self._FIN_LEXICON)
            except Exception as exc:
                LOGGER.warning("VADER initialization failed: %s", exc)
                self._vader = None

    @property
    def engine_name(self) -> str:
        if self._vader is not None:
            return "financial-vader"
        if _TEXTBLOB_AVAILABLE:
            return "financial-lexicon+textblob-fallback"
        return "transparent-financial-lexicon"

    def _score_text(self, text: str) -> float:
        cleaned = str(text).strip()
        if not cleaned:
            return 0.0
        if self._vader is not None:
            return float(self._vader.polarity_scores(cleaned)["compound"])
        tokens = [token.lower() for token in _TOKEN_PATTERN.findall(cleaned)]
        scores = [self._FIN_LEXICON[token] for token in tokens if token in self._FIN_LEXICON]
        if scores:
            # Prefer transparent domain evidence over a generic text model when
            # recognized financial terms are actually present.
            return float(math.tanh(sum(scores) / math.sqrt(len(scores) + 1.0) / 3.0))
        if _TEXTBLOB_AVAILABLE:
            try:
                return float(np.clip(TextBlob(cleaned).sentiment.polarity, -1, 1))
            except Exception:
                pass
        return 0.0

    def analyze(
        self,
        tickers: Sequence[str],
        price_data: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame],
        news_texts: Optional[Mapping[str, Sequence[str]]] = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        normalized = tuple(dict.fromkeys(str(t).strip().upper() for t in tickers))
        if any(not ticker for ticker in normalized):
            raise AgentInputError("tickers cannot contain empty values")
        normalized_prices = {
            str(ticker).strip().upper(): frame for ticker, frame in price_data.items()
        }
        normalized_news = {}
        for ticker, texts in (news_texts or {}).items():
            key = str(ticker).strip().upper()
            normalized_news[key] = (
                (texts,) if isinstance(texts, str) else tuple(str(text) for text in texts)
            )
        text_scores: dict[str, Optional[float]] = {}
        momentum_scores: dict[str, Optional[float]] = {}
        headline_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
        for ticker in normalized:
            headlines = tuple(
                text.strip()
                for text in normalized_news.get(ticker, ())
                if text.strip()
            )
            headline_counts[ticker] = len(headlines)
            text_scores[ticker] = (
                _safe_mean([self._score_text(text) for text in headlines])
                if headlines
                else None
            )
            momentum_scores[ticker] = self._price_momentum(
                normalized_prices.get(ticker)
            )
        available_text = [
            float(score) for score in text_scores.values() if score is not None
        ]
        average_text = _safe_mean(available_text)
        signal = {
            "agent": self.name,
            "status": "ok",
            "engine": self.engine_name,
            "sentiment_available": bool(available_text),
            "overall_sentiment": (
                "positive"
                if available_text and average_text > 0.10
                else "negative"
                if available_text and average_text < -0.10
                else "neutral"
                if available_text
                else "unavailable"
            ),
            "avg_score": average_text if available_text else 0.0,
            "ticker_sentiments": text_scores,
            "headline_counts": headline_counts,
            "price_momentum": momentum_scores,
            "price_momentum_is_sentiment": False,
            "coverage": len(available_text) / max(len(normalized), 1),
        }
        return self._record(signal)

    @staticmethod
    def _price_momentum(frame: Optional[pd.DataFrame]) -> Optional[float]:
        if not isinstance(frame, pd.DataFrame) or "Close" not in frame.columns:
            return None
        close = pd.to_numeric(frame["Close"], errors="coerce")
        close = close.loc[close.notna() & np.isfinite(close) & (close > 0)]
        if len(close) < 21:
            return None
        return_5 = float(close.iloc[-1] / close.iloc[-6] - 1.0)
        return_20 = float(close.iloc[-1] / close.iloc[-21] - 1.0)
        return float(np.clip((0.6 * return_5 + 0.4 * return_20) * 10.0, -1, 1))


class RiskAgent(BaseAgent):
    def __init__(
        self,
        risk_intelligence: RiskIntelligence,
        config: Optional[MultiAgentConfig] = None,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__("RiskAgent")
        self.ri = risk_intelligence
        self.config = config or MultiAgentConfig()

    def analyze(
        self,
        price_data: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame],
        weights: Mapping[str, float],
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        portfolio = self.ri.portfolio_risk(price_data, weights)
        if not portfolio:
            raise AgentExecutionError("portfolio risk returned no evidence")
            volatility = _agent_finite(
            portfolio["portfolio_volatility"], "portfolio_volatility"
        )
        if volatility >= self.config.risk_critical_volatility:
            action, severity = "reduce", "critical"
        elif volatility >= self.config.risk_reduce_volatility:
            action, severity = "reduce", "elevated"
        else:
            action, severity = "maintain", "normal"
        signal = {
            "agent": self.name,
            "status": "ok",
            "portfolio_volatility": volatility,
                "portfolio_var_95": _agent_finite(
                portfolio["portfolio_var_95"], "portfolio_var_95"
            ),
                "portfolio_var_99": _agent_finite(
                portfolio.get("portfolio_var_99", portfolio["portfolio_var_95"]),
                "portfolio_var_99",
            ),
                "portfolio_cvar_95": _agent_finite(
                portfolio.get("portfolio_cvar_95", portfolio["portfolio_var_95"]),
                "portfolio_cvar_95",
            ),
                "max_drawdown": _agent_finite(portfolio["max_drawdown"], "max_drawdown"),
                "diversification_ratio": _agent_finite(
                portfolio["diversification_ratio"], "diversification_ratio"
            ),
                "effective_number_of_positions": _agent_finite(
                portfolio.get("effective_number_of_positions", len(weights)),
                "effective_number_of_positions",
            ),
            "risk_action": action,
            "severity": severity,
            "warnings": list(portfolio.get("warnings", [])),
        }
        return self._record(signal)


class PortfolioAgent(BaseAgent):
    """Optimize with real per-ticker views and universe-safe turnover state."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        optimizer: PortfolioOptimizer,
        config: Optional[MultiAgentConfig] = None,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__("PortfolioAgent")
        self.optimizer = optimizer
        self.config = config or MultiAgentConfig()
        self._prev_weights: Optional[dict[str, float]] = None
        self._portfolio_lock = threading.RLock()

    def reset_state(self) -> None:
        with self._portfolio_lock:
            self._prev_weights = None

    @property
    def previous_weights(self) -> Optional[dict[str, float]]:
        with self._portfolio_lock:
            return _deep_copy(self._prev_weights)

    def analyze(
        self,
        price_data: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame],
        macro_signal: Mapping[str, Any],
        alpha_signal: Mapping[str, Any],
        risk_signal: Mapping[str, Any],
        method: str = "max_sharpe",
        regime: Optional[MarketRegime] = None,
        max_turnover: Optional[float] = None,
        *,
        sentiment_signal: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None,
        current_weights: Optional[Mapping[str, float]] = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        requested_method = str(method).strip().lower()
        if requested_method not in _VALID_METHODS:
            raise AgentInputError(
                f"unsupported optimization method: {requested_method!r}"
            )
        returns = self.optimizer.build_returns_matrix(price_data)
        if returns.shape[1] < 2:
            raise AgentInputError("portfolio optimization needs at least two assets")
        tickers = tuple(str(column).upper() for column in returns.columns)
        limit = (
            self.config.default_max_turnover
            if max_turnover is None
            else _agent_finite(max_turnover, "max_turnover")
        )
        if not 0 <= limit <= 1:
            raise AgentInputError("max_turnover must be in [0, 1]")
        crisis = regime is not None and str(regime.name).lower() == "crisis"
        previous, state_warning = self._resolve_previous(
            tickers, current_weights
        )
        actual_method = self._select_method(
            requested_method, regime, alpha_signal, risk_signal
        )
        solution: PortfolioSolution
        if actual_method == "risk_parity":
            solution = self.optimizer.risk_parity_detailed(
                returns,
                prev_weights=previous,
                max_turnover=limit,
                crisis_regime=crisis,
            )
        elif actual_method == "min_volatility":
            solution = self.optimizer.min_volatility_detailed(
                returns,
                prev_weights=previous,
                max_turnover=limit,
                crisis_regime=crisis,
            )
        elif actual_method == "black_litterman":
            views, confidences = self._views(
                tickers, alpha_signal, sentiment_signal
            )
            if not views:
                actual_method = "max_sharpe"
                solution = self.optimizer.mean_variance_detailed(
                    returns,
                    prev_weights=previous,
                    max_turnover=limit,
                    crisis_regime=crisis,
                )
                state_warning = _join_warning(
                    state_warning,
                    "Black-Litterman had no actionable per-ticker views; "
                    "max_sharpe was used.",
                )
            else:
                market_weights = previous if previous is not None else None
                solution = self.optimizer.black_litterman_detailed(
                    returns,
                    views=views,
                    view_confidences=confidences,
                    market_weights=market_weights,
                    prev_weights=previous,
                    max_turnover=limit,
                    crisis_regime=crisis,
                )
        else:
            solution = self.optimizer.mean_variance_detailed(
                returns,
                prev_weights=previous,
                max_turnover=limit,
                crisis_regime=crisis,
            )
        weights = dict(solution.weights)
        with self._portfolio_lock:
            self._prev_weights = dict(weights)
        signal = {
            "agent": self.name,
            "status": "ok",
            "weights": weights,
            "requested_method": requested_method,
            "method": actual_method,
            "n_assets": len(weights),
            "turnover_constrained": previous is not None and not crisis,
            "crisis_override": crisis,
            "one_way_turnover": solution.one_way_turnover,
            "expected_return": solution.expected_return,
            "expected_volatility": solution.expected_volatility,
            "historical_sharpe": solution.sharpe_ratio,
            "risk_contributions": dict(solution.risk_contributions),
            "optimizer_warnings": list(solution.warnings),
            "state_warning": state_warning,
            "macro_signal_used_for_method_selection": requested_method == "auto",
            "risk_signal_used_for_method_selection": requested_method == "auto",
        }
        return self._record(signal)

    def _resolve_previous(
        self,
        tickers: tuple[str, ...],
        explicit: Optional[Mapping[str, float]],
    ) -> tuple[Optional[dict[str, float]], Optional[str]]:
        candidate = (
                {str(t).strip().upper(): _agent_finite(w, f"current_weights[{t}]") for t, w in explicit.items()}
            if explicit is not None
            else self.previous_weights
        )
        if candidate is None:
            return None, None
        if set(candidate) != set(tickers):
            return (
                None,
                "Previous-weight universe differs from current data; turnover "
                "state was safely reset.",
            )
        total = sum(candidate.values())
        if any(weight < 0 for weight in candidate.values()) or abs(total - 1.0) > 1e-7:
            raise AgentInputError("current/previous weights must be long-only and sum to 1")
        return candidate, None

    @staticmethod
    def _select_method(
        requested: str,
        regime: Optional[MarketRegime],
        alpha: Mapping[str, Any],
        risk: Mapping[str, Any],
    ) -> str:
        if requested != "auto":
            return requested
        regime_name = str(regime.name).lower() if regime is not None else ""
        if risk.get("risk_action") == "reduce" or regime_name in {
            "crisis",
            "high_volatility",
        }:
            return "min_volatility"
        actionable = [
            item
            for item in alpha.get("predictions", {}).values()
            if isinstance(item, Mapping) and item.get("actionable")
        ]
        return "black_litterman" if actionable else "max_sharpe"

    def _views(
        self,
        tickers: tuple[str, ...],
        alpha: Mapping[str, Any],
        sentiment: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]],
    ) -> tuple[dict[str, float], dict[str, float]]:
        rows = alpha.get("predictions", {})
        sentiment_available = bool(
            sentiment and sentiment.get("sentiment_available")
        )
        sentiment_scores = (
            sentiment.get("ticker_sentiments", {}) if sentiment_available else {}
        )
        views: dict[str, float] = {}
        confidences: dict[str, float] = {}
        for ticker in tickers:
            row = rows.get(ticker) if isinstance(rows, Mapping) else None
            if not isinstance(row, Mapping) or not row.get("actionable"):
                continue
            expected = _agent_finite(
                row.get("expected_return_annual"), f"{ticker}.expected_return_annual"
            )
            quality = _agent_finite(row.get("model_quality"), f"{ticker}.model_quality")
            text_score = (
                sentiment_scores.get(ticker)
                if isinstance(sentiment_scores, Mapping)
                else None
            )
            if text_score is not None:
                expected += (
                    _agent_finite(text_score, f"{ticker}.sentiment")
                    * self.config.maximum_sentiment_view_adjustment
                )
            views[ticker] = float(
                np.clip(
                    expected,
                    -0.95,
                    self.config.maximum_annualized_view,
                )
            )
            # BL confidence is not probability; avoid numerical extremes.
            confidences[ticker] = float(np.clip(quality, 0.05, 0.95))
        return views, confidences


class MultiAgentSystem:
    """Coordinate independent evidence agents and one dependent portfolio agent."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        data_engine: Any,
        regime_detector: Any,
        ensemble_ai: Any,
        risk_intelligence: RiskIntelligence,
        optimizer: PortfolioOptimizer,
        config: Optional[MultiAgentConfig] = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.config = config or MultiAgentConfig()
        self.data_engine = data_engine
        self.regime_detector = regime_detector
        self.ensemble_ai = ensemble_ai
        self.macro_agent = MacroAgent()
        self.alpha_agent = AlphaAgent(self.config)
        self.sentiment_agent = SentimentAgent()
        self.risk_agent = RiskAgent(risk_intelligence, self.config)
        self.portfolio_agent = PortfolioAgent(optimizer, self.config)
        self._history: list[MultiAgentDiagnostics] = []
        self._history_lock = threading.RLock()

    def run(
        self,
        tickers: Sequence[str],
        price_data: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame],
        regime: MarketRegime,
        predictions: Mapping[str, EnsemblePrediction],
        opt_method: str = "max_sharpe",
        *,
        news_texts: Optional[Mapping[str, Sequence[str]]] = None,
        current_weights: Optional[Mapping[str, float]] = None,
        max_turnover: Optional[float] = None,
        strict: Optional[bool] = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            key: _deep_copy(value)
            for key, value in self.run_detailed(
                tickers=tickers,
                price_data=price_data,
                regime=regime,
                predictions=predictions,
                opt_method=opt_method,
                news_texts=news_texts,
                current_weights=current_weights,
                max_turnover=max_turnover,
                strict=strict,
            ).signals.items()
        }

    def run_detailed(
        self,
        tickers: Sequence[str],
        price_data: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame],
        regime: MarketRegime,
        predictions: Mapping[str, EnsemblePrediction],
        opt_method: str = "max_sharpe",
        *,
        news_texts: Optional[Mapping[str, Sequence[str]]] = None,
        current_weights: Optional[Mapping[str, float]] = None,
        max_turnover: Optional[float] = None,
        strict: Optional[bool] = None,
    ) -> MultiAgentAnalysis:
        started = time.perf_counter()
        strict_mode = (
            self.config.strict_agent_failures if strict is None else bool(strict)
        )
        normalized_tickers, normalized_prices, warnings = self._validate_run_inputs(
            tickers, price_data, predictions, regime
        )
        normalized_predictions = {
            str(ticker).strip().upper(): prediction
            for ticker, prediction in predictions.items()
            if str(ticker).strip().upper() in set(normalized_tickers)
        }
        overview = self._market_overview(normalized_tickers, normalized_prices)
        risk_weights = self._risk_weights(
            normalized_tickers, normalized_prices, current_weights
        )
        fingerprint = self._fingerprint(
            normalized_tickers,
            normalized_prices,
            regime,
            normalized_predictions,
            opt_method=opt_method,
            news_texts=news_texts,
            current_weights=current_weights,
            max_turnover=max_turnover,
        )
        run_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
        signals: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
        statuses: dict[str, AgentStatus] = {}
        tasks = {
            "macro": lambda: self.macro_agent.analyze(overview, regime),
            "alpha": lambda: self.alpha_agent.analyze(normalized_predictions),
            "sentiment": lambda: self.sentiment_agent.analyze(
                normalized_tickers, normalized_prices, news_texts
            ),
            "risk": lambda: self.risk_agent.analyze(
                normalized_prices, risk_weights
            ),
        }
        with ThreadPoolExecutor(
            max_workers=min(self.config.max_workers, len(tasks)),
            thread_name_prefix="smartinvest-agent",
        ) as executor:
            future_map = {}
            for key, function in tasks.items():
                task_started = time.perf_counter()
                future = executor.submit(_timed_call, function, task_started)
                future_map[future] = key
            for future in as_completed(future_map):
                key = future_map[future]
                try:
                    signal, duration = future.result()
                    signals[key] = signal
                    statuses[key] = AgentStatus(
                        name=signal.get("agent", key),
                        status="ok",
                        duration_ms=duration,
                    )
                except Exception as exc:
                    statuses[key] = AgentStatus(
                        name=key,
                        status="error",
                        duration_ms=0.0,
                        error=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
                    )
                    signals[key] = {
                        "agent": key,
                        "status": "error",
                        "error": str(exc),
                    }
        failed = [key for key, status in statuses.items() if status.status != "ok"]
        if failed and strict_mode:
            raise AgentExecutionError(
                "required agents failed: "
                + ", ".join(
                    f"{key} ({statuses[key].error})" for key in failed
                )
            )

        dependency_keys = {"macro", "alpha", "risk"}
        missing_dependencies = [
            key for key in dependency_keys if signals.get(key, {}).get("status") != "ok"
        ]
        portfolio_started = time.perf_counter()
        if missing_dependencies:
            error = (
                "portfolio skipped because dependencies failed: "
                + ", ".join(sorted(missing_dependencies))
            )
            signals["portfolio"] = {
                "agent": "PortfolioAgent",
                "status": "skipped",
                "error": error,
                "weights": {},
            }
            statuses["portfolio"] = AgentStatus(
                name="PortfolioAgent",
                status="skipped",
                duration_ms=0.0,
                error=error,
            )
        else:
            try:
                portfolio = self.portfolio_agent.analyze(
                    normalized_prices,
                    signals["macro"],
                    signals["alpha"],
                    signals["risk"],
                    opt_method,
                    regime=regime,
                    max_turnover=max_turnover,
                    sentiment_signal=signals.get("sentiment"),
                    current_weights=current_weights,
                )
                duration = (time.perf_counter() - portfolio_started) * 1000
                signals["portfolio"] = portfolio
                statuses["portfolio"] = AgentStatus(
                    name="PortfolioAgent", status="ok", duration_ms=duration
                )
            except Exception as exc:
                signals["portfolio"] = {
                    "agent": "PortfolioAgent",
                    "status": "error",
                    "error": str(exc),
                    "weights": {},
                }
                statuses["portfolio"] = AgentStatus(
                    name="PortfolioAgent",
                    status="error",
                    duration_ms=(time.perf_counter() - portfolio_started) * 1000,
                    error=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
                )
                if strict_mode:
                    raise AgentExecutionError(
                        f"PortfolioAgent failed: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
                    ) from exc
        consensus = self._consensus(signals)
        signals["consensus"] = _consensus_dict(consensus)
        total_duration = (time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000
        diagnostics = MultiAgentDiagnostics(
            run_id=run_id,
            timestamp_utc=datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
            input_fingerprint=fingerprint,
            requested_tickers=tuple(str(t).strip().upper() for t in tickers),
            usable_tickers=normalized_tickers,
            agent_statuses=MappingProxyType(dict(statuses)),
            consensus=consensus,
            warnings=tuple(dict.fromkeys(warnings)),
            total_duration_ms=total_duration,
        )
        self._record_history(diagnostics)
        return MultiAgentAnalysis(
            signals=_deep_freeze(signals),
            diagnostics=diagnostics,
        )

    def history(self) -> tuple[MultiAgentDiagnostics, ...]:
        with self._history_lock:
            return tuple(self._history)

    def _record_history(self, diagnostics: MultiAgentDiagnostics) -> None:
        with self._history_lock:
            self._history.append(diagnostics)
            overflow = len(self._history) - self.config.history_limit
            if overflow > 0:
                del self._history[:overflow]

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_run_inputs(
        tickers: Sequence[str],
        price_data: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame],
        predictions: Mapping[str, EnsemblePrediction],
        regime: MarketRegime,
    ) -> tuple[tuple[str, ...], dict[str, pd.DataFrame], list[str]]:
        if not isinstance(regime, MarketRegime):
            raise AgentInputError("regime must be a MarketRegime")
        normalized = tuple(str(ticker).strip().upper() for ticker in tickers)
        if not normalized or any(not ticker for ticker in normalized):
            raise AgentInputError("tickers must be a non-empty sequence")
        if len(set(normalized)) != len(normalized):
            raise AgentInputError("tickers contain duplicates")
        prices = {
            str(ticker).strip().upper(): frame for ticker, frame in price_data.items()
        }
        prediction_keys = [str(ticker).strip().upper() for ticker in predictions]
        if len(set(prediction_keys)) != len(prediction_keys):
            raise AgentInputError(
                "prediction keys collide after ticker normalization"
            )
        missing_prices = sorted(set(normalized) - set(prices))
        if missing_prices:
            raise AgentInputError(
                "missing price data for: " + ", ".join(missing_prices)
            )
        warnings: list[str] = []
        missing_predictions = sorted(set(normalized) - {
            str(ticker).strip().upper() for ticker in predictions
        })
        if missing_predictions:
            warnings.append(
                "No model prediction for: " + ", ".join(missing_predictions)
            )
        return normalized, {ticker: prices[ticker] for ticker in normalized}, warnings

    @staticmethod
    def _market_overview(
        tickers: tuple[str, ...],
        price_data: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame],
    ) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
        overview: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
        for ticker in tickers:
            frame = price_data[ticker]
            if not isinstance(frame, pd.DataFrame) or "Close" not in frame.columns:
                raise AgentInputError(f"{ticker}: price data requires Close")
            close = pd.to_numeric(frame["Close"], errors="coerce")
            close = close.loc[close.notna() & np.isfinite(close) & (close > 0)]
            if len(close) < 22:
                raise AgentInputError(
                    f"{ticker}: at least 22 valid closes are required"
                )
            returns = close.pct_change(fill_method=None).dropna()
            overview[ticker] = {
                "return_1m": float(close.iloc[-1] / close.iloc[-22] - 1),
                "volatility_20d": float(
                    returns.iloc[-20:].std(ddof=1) * math.sqrt(252)
                ),
                "last_close": float(close.iloc[-1]),
                "observations": len(close),
                "as_of": str(close.index[-1]),
            }
        return overview

    @staticmethod
    def _risk_weights(
        tickers: tuple[str, ...],
        price_data: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame],
        current: Optional[Mapping[str, float]],
    ) -> dict[str, float]:
        if current is None:
            return {ticker: 1.0 / len(tickers) for ticker in tickers}
        normalized = {
            str(ticker).strip().upper(): _agent_finite(
                weight, f"current_weights[{ticker}]"
            )
            for ticker, weight in current.items()
        }
        if set(normalized) != set(tickers):
            raise AgentInputError(
                "current_weights must exactly match the requested ticker universe"
            )
        if any(weight < 0 for weight in normalized.values()):
            raise AgentInputError("current_weights cannot be negative")
        if abs(sum(normalized.values()) - 1.0) > 1e-7:
            raise AgentInputError("current_weights must sum to 1")
        return normalized

    @staticmethod
    def _fingerprint(
        tickers: tuple[str, ...],
        price_data: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame],
        regime: MarketRegime,
        predictions: Mapping[str, EnsemblePrediction],
        *,
        opt_method: str,
        news_texts: Optional[Mapping[str, Sequence[str]]],
        current_weights: Optional[Mapping[str, float]],
        max_turnover: Optional[float],
    ) -> str:
        payload = {
            "tickers": tickers,
            "regime": {
                "name": regime.name,
                "confidence": regime.confidence,
            },
            "prices": {
                ticker: {
                    "rows": len(price_data[ticker]),
                    "last_index": str(price_data[ticker].index[-1])
                    if len(price_data[ticker])
                    else None,
                    "last_close": float(
                        pd.to_numeric(price_data[ticker]["Close"], errors="coerce").iloc[-1]
                    )
                    if len(price_data[ticker]) and "Close" in price_data[ticker]
                    else None,
                }
                for ticker in tickers
            },
            "predictions": {
                str(key): {
                    "return": value.ensemble_pred,
                    "quality": value.confidence,
                    "horizon": value.horizon_days,
                }
                for key, value in predictions.items()
                if isinstance(value, EnsemblePrediction)
            },
            "optimization": {
                "method": str(opt_method).strip().lower(),
                "max_turnover": max_turnover,
                "current_weights": {
                    str(key).strip().upper(): value
                    for key, value in (current_weights or {}).items()
                },
            },
            "news_hashes": {
                str(ticker).strip().upper(): [
                    hashlib.sha256(str(text).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
                    for text in (
                        (texts,) if isinstance(texts, str) else texts
                    )
                ]
                for ticker, texts in (news_texts or {}).items()
            },
        }
        encoded = json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, default=str).encode("utf-8")
        return hashlib.sha256(encoded).hexdigest()

    @staticmethod
    def _consensus(signals: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]]) -> ConsensusResult:
        macro = signals.get("macro", {})
        alpha = signals.get("alpha", {})
        sentiment = signals.get("sentiment", {})
        risk = signals.get("risk", {})
        votes: list[tuple[str, float, float]] = []
        if macro.get("status") == "ok":
            votes.append(
                (
                    "MacroAgent",
                    float(macro.get("bias_score", 0.0)),
                    float(macro.get("regime_confidence", 0.0)),
                )
            )
        if alpha.get("status") == "ok":
            alpha_score = float(
                np.clip(float(alpha.get("quality_weighted_score", 0.0)) * 20, -1, 1)
            )
            coverage = len(alpha.get("predictions", {}))
            votes.append(
                (
                    "AlphaAgent",
                    alpha_score,
                    min(1.0, coverage / 5.0),
                )
            )
        if sentiment.get("status") == "ok" and sentiment.get("sentiment_available"):
            votes.append(
                (
                    "SentimentAgent",
                    float(sentiment.get("avg_score", 0.0)),
                    float(sentiment.get("coverage", 0.0)),
                )
            )
        weighted_sum = sum(score * weight for _, score, weight in votes)
        weight_sum = sum(weight for _, _, weight in votes)
        score = weighted_sum / weight_sum if weight_sum > _EPSILON else 0.0
        positive = [name for name, value, _ in votes if value > 0.15]
        negative = [name for name, value, _ in votes if value < -0.15]
        neutral = [name for name, value, _ in votes if abs(value) <= 0.15]
        risk_veto = (
            risk.get("status") == "ok" and risk.get("risk_action") == "reduce"
        )
        if risk_veto:
            stance = "reduce_risk"
        elif score > 0.20:
            stance = "constructive"
        elif score < -0.20:
            stance = "defensive"
        else:
            stance = "neutral"
        agreement = (
            max(len(positive), len(negative), len(neutral)) / len(votes)
            if votes
            else 0.0
        )
        confidence = float(
            np.clip(
                agreement
                * _safe_mean([weight for _, _, weight in votes]),
                0,
                1,
            )
        )
        conflicts: list[str] = []
        if positive and negative:
            conflicts.append(
                "Positive and negative directional evidence disagree across agents."
            )
        if risk_veto and positive:
            conflicts.append(
                "RiskAgent overrides constructive directional evidence."
            )
        rationale = [
            f"Directional consensus score is {score:+.2f} from {len(votes)} "
            "independent evidence agents."
        ]
        if risk_veto:
            rationale.append(
                "Historical portfolio volatility crossed the configured reduction threshold."
            )
        if not sentiment.get("sentiment_available"):
            rationale.append(
                "Independent text sentiment was unavailable and did not affect consensus."
            )
        return ConsensusResult(
            stance=stance,
            score=float(score),
            confidence=confidence,
            agreement=float(agreement),
            risk_veto=risk_veto,
            supporting_agents=tuple(positive),
            opposing_agents=tuple(negative),
            conflicts=tuple(conflicts),
            rationale=tuple(rationale),
        )

    def format_report(
        self, signals: Mapping[str, Any] | MultiAgentAnalysis
    ) -> str:
        analysis = signals if isinstance(signals, MultiAgentAnalysis) else None
        data = analysis.signals if analysis is not None else signals
        macro = data.get("macro", {})
        alpha = data.get("alpha", {})
        sentiment = data.get("sentiment", {})
        risk = data.get("risk", {})
        portfolio = data.get("portfolio", {})
        consensus = data.get("consensus", {})
        lines = [
            "=" * 76,
            "MULTI-AGENT DECISION-SUPPORT REPORT",
            "=" * 76,
            "",
            "Consensus:",
            f"  Stance                 : {str(consensus.get('stance', 'N/A')).upper()}",
            f"  Evidence score         : {float(consensus.get('score', 0)):+.3f}",
            f"  Agreement              : {float(consensus.get('agreement', 0)):.1%}",
            f"  Evidence quality       : {float(consensus.get('confidence', 0)):.1%}",
            f"  Risk veto              : {'YES' if consensus.get('risk_veto') else 'NO'}",
            "",
            "MacroAgent:",
            f"  Regime                 : {macro.get('regime', 'N/A')} "
            f"({float(macro.get('regime_confidence', 0)):.0%})",
            f"  Equity bias            : {macro.get('equity_bias', 'N/A')}",
            f"  Average 1M return      : {float(macro.get('average_return_1m', 0)):+.2%}",
            "",
            "AlphaAgent:",
            f"  Common horizon         : {alpha.get('common_horizon_days', 'N/A')} days",
            f"  Top picks              : {', '.join(alpha.get('top_picks', [])) or 'None'}",
            f"  Avoid list             : {', '.join(alpha.get('avoid_list', [])) or 'None'}",
            f"  Bullish/Bearish        : {alpha.get('bullish_count', 0)} / "
            f"{alpha.get('bearish_count', 0)}",
            "",
            "SentimentAgent:",
            f"  Independent text       : "
            f"{'AVAILABLE' if sentiment.get('sentiment_available') else 'UNAVAILABLE'}",
            f"  Engine                 : {sentiment.get('engine', 'N/A')}",
            f"  Overall                : {sentiment.get('overall_sentiment', 'N/A')}",
            f"  Price proxy mislabeled : NO",
            "",
            "RiskAgent:",
            f"  Portfolio volatility   : {float(risk.get('portfolio_volatility', 0)):.2%}",
            f"  Historical VaR 95%     : {float(risk.get('portfolio_var_95', 0)):.2%}",
            f"  Historical CVaR 95%    : {float(risk.get('portfolio_cvar_95', 0)):.2%}",
            f"  Risk action            : {risk.get('risk_action', 'N/A')}",
            "",
            "PortfolioAgent:",
            f"  Requested/used method  : {portfolio.get('requested_method', 'N/A')} / "
            f"{portfolio.get('method', 'N/A')}",
            f"  Expected return        : {float(portfolio.get('expected_return', 0)):.2%}",
            f"  Expected volatility    : {float(portfolio.get('expected_volatility', 0)):.2%}",
            f"  One-way turnover       : "
            + (
                f"{float(portfolio['one_way_turnover']):.2%}"
                if portfolio.get("one_way_turnover") is not None
                else "N/A"
            ),
            "  Weights:",
        ]
        for ticker, weight in sorted(
            portfolio.get("weights", {}).items(),
            key=lambda item: item[1],
            reverse=True,
        ):
            lines.append(f"    {ticker:<12} {float(weight):>8.2%}")
        if analysis is not None:
            lines.extend(
                [
                    "",
                    "Audit:",
                    f"  Run ID                 : {analysis.diagnostics.run_id}",
                    f"  Input fingerprint      : {analysis.diagnostics.input_fingerprint[:16]}...",
                    f"  Total duration         : {analysis.diagnostics.total_duration_ms:.1f} ms",
                ]
            )
            if analysis.diagnostics.warnings:
                lines.extend(["", "Warnings:"])
                lines.extend(
                    f"  - {warning}" for warning in analysis.diagnostics.warnings
                )
        lines.extend(
            [
                "",
                "Limitations:",
                "  - Agent scores are deterministic evidence summaries, not probabilities.",
                "  - Historical estimates and optimized weights may fail out of sample.",
                "  - This report does not determine suitability or execute trades.",
                "=" * 76,
            ]
        )
        return "\n".join(lines)


def _deep_copy(value: Any) -> Any:
    if value is None or isinstance(value, (str, int, float, bool)):
        return value
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return {key: _deep_copy(item) for key, item in value.items()}
    if isinstance(value, tuple):
        return tuple(_deep_copy(item) for item in value)
    if isinstance(value, list):
        return [_deep_copy(item) for item in value]
    if isinstance(value, np.generic):
        return value.item()
    return value


def _deep_freeze(value: Any) -> Any:
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return MappingProxyType(
            {key: _deep_freeze(item) for key, item in value.items()}
        )
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
        return tuple(_deep_freeze(item) for item in value)
    if isinstance(value, np.generic):
        return value.item()
    return value


def _timed_call(
    function: Any, started: float
) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], float]:
    result = function()
    return result, (time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000


def _join_warning(current: Optional[str], addition: str) -> str:
    return f"{current} {addition}".strip() if current else addition


def _consensus_dict(consensus: ConsensusResult) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "stance": consensus.stance,
        "score": consensus.score,
        "confidence": consensus.confidence,
        "agreement": consensus.agreement,
        "risk_veto": consensus.risk_veto,
        "supporting_agents": list(consensus.supporting_agents),
        "opposing_agents": list(consensus.opposing_agents),
        "conflicts": list(consensus.conflicts),
        "rationale": list(consensus.rationale),
        "confidence_is_probability": False,
    }

    import plotly.graph_objects as go

    PLOTLY_AVAILABLE = True

    go = None  # type: ignore[assignment]
    make_subplots = None  # type: ignore[assignment]

LOGGER = logging.getLogger(f"{APP_NAME}.reporting")
_EPSILON: Final = 1e-12
_RISK_RANK: Final = MappingProxyType(
    {"low": 0, "medium": 1, "medium-high": 2, "high": 3, "critical": 4}
)
_PALETTE: Final = (
    "#2F6BFF",  # blue
    "#C58B00",  # gold
    "#E67E22",  # orange
    "#708238",  # olive
    "#C45A84",  # pink
)
