class ReportingError(SmartInvestError):
    """Base error for Module 12."""


class ReportingInputError(ReportingError):
    """Raised when report evidence is malformed or inconsistent."""


class ReportingDependencyError(ReportingError):
    """Raised when an optional renderer is not installed."""


class ReportingIOError(ReportingError):
    """Raised when a report artifact cannot be saved atomically."""


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ReportingConfig:
    report_title: str = "SmartInvest 4.8 Decision-Support Report"
    dashboard_title: str = "SmartInvest 4.8 Risk and Performance Dashboard"
    top_strategies: int = 3
    top_risk_reports: int = 5
    top_explanations: int = 5
    top_backtests: int = 5
    top_scenarios: int = 5
    maximum_dashboard_assets: int = 8
    maximum_chart_points_per_series: int = 750
    minimum_trend_points: int = 8
    create_parent_directories: bool = False
    overwrite_existing: bool = True
    include_plotlyjs: str | bool = "inline"

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not str(self.report_title).strip() or not str(self.dashboard_title).strip():
            raise ValueError("report and dashboard titles cannot be empty")
        for name in (
            "top_strategies",
            "top_risk_reports",
            "top_explanations",
            "top_backtests",
            "top_scenarios",
            "maximum_dashboard_assets",
        ):
            if not 1 <= int(getattr(self, name)) <= 100:
                raise ValueError(f"{name} must be in [1, 100]")
        if not 50 <= int(self.maximum_chart_points_per_series) <= 10_000:
            raise ValueError("maximum_chart_points_per_series must be in [50, 10000]")
        if not 3 <= int(self.minimum_trend_points) <= 100:
            raise ValueError("minimum_trend_points must be in [3, 100]")


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ReportDiagnostics:
    generated_at_utc: str
    evidence_fingerprint: str
    section_names: tuple[str, ...]
    evidence_counts: Mapping[str, int]
    warnings: tuple[str, ...]
    source_metadata: Mapping[str, Any]


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class TextReportArtifact:
    content: str
    diagnostics: ReportDiagnostics


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class SavedArtifact:
    path: Path
    size_bytes: int
    sha256: str


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ChartContract:
    chart_id: str
    section: str
    analytical_question: str
    takeaway: str
    family: str
    chart_type: str
    fields: tuple[str, ...]
    source_ids: tuple[str, ...]
    fallback: str


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class DashboardSpec:
    title: str
    generated_at_utc: str
    data_freshness: Optional[str]
    datasets: Mapping[str, tuple[Mapping[str, Any], ...]]
    charts: tuple[ChartContract, ...]
    sources: Mapping[str, Any]
    warnings: tuple[str, ...]
    fingerprint: str


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class DashboardArtifact:
    saved: SavedArtifact
    specification: DashboardSpec
    renderer: str
    offline: bool


class ReportGenerator:
    """Validate, summarize, format, and atomically save SmartInvest evidence."""

    def __init__(self, config: Optional[ReportingConfig] = None) -> None:
        self.config = config or ReportingConfig()
        self._io_lock = threading.RLock()

    def generate_text_report(
        self,
        regime: MarketRegime,
        strategies: Sequence[Strategy],
        agent_signals: Mapping[str, Any],
        risk_reports: Mapping[str, RiskReport],
        explanations: Mapping[str, XAIExplanation],
        backtest_results: Sequence[BacktestResult],
        scenario_results: Sequence[ScenarioResult],
        timestamp: Optional[datetime] = None,
        *,
        source_metadata: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None,
    ) -> str:
        """Return the backward-compatible plain-text report."""

        return self.generate_text_report_detailed(
            regime=regime,
            strategies=strategies,
            agent_signals=agent_signals,
            risk_reports=risk_reports,
            explanations=explanations,
            backtest_results=backtest_results,
            scenario_results=scenario_results,
            timestamp=timestamp,
            source_metadata=source_metadata,
        ).content

    def generate_text_report_detailed(
        self,
        regime: MarketRegime,
        strategies: Sequence[Strategy],
        agent_signals: Mapping[str, Any],
        risk_reports: Mapping[str, RiskReport],
        explanations: Mapping[str, XAIExplanation],
        backtest_results: Sequence[BacktestResult],
        scenario_results: Sequence[ScenarioResult],
        timestamp: Optional[datetime] = None,
        *,
        source_metadata: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None,
    ) -> TextReportArtifact:
        warnings = self._validate_report_inputs(
            regime,
            strategies,
            agent_signals,
            risk_reports,
            explanations,
            backtest_results,
            scenario_results,
        )
        generated_at, timestamp_warning = self._timestamp(timestamp)
        if timestamp_warning:
            warnings.append(timestamp_warning)
        sources = self._validated_source_metadata(source_metadata)

        ordered_strategies = sorted(
            strategies, key=lambda item: float(item.score), reverse=True
        )
        ordered_risks = sorted(
            risk_reports.values(),
            key=lambda item: (
                _RISK_RANK.get(str(item.risk_level).lower(), -1),
                float(item.volatility_annual),
            ),
            reverse=True,
        )
        ordered_explanations = sorted(
            explanations.values(),
            key=lambda item: float(item.confidence),
            reverse=True,
        )
        ordered_backtests = sorted(
            backtest_results,
            key=lambda item: (
                _finite_or_default(item.sharpe_ratio, -math.inf),
                _finite_or_default(item.cagr, -math.inf),
            ),
            reverse=True,
        )
        ordered_scenarios = sorted(
            scenario_results, key=lambda item: float(item.portfolio_return)
        )
        section_names = (
            "Technical summary",
            "Scope and metric definitions",
            "Market regime evidence",
            "Strategy candidates",
            "Multi-agent evidence and consensus",
            "Historical risk evidence",
            "Explainability evidence",
            "Backtest evidence",
            "Scenario stress evidence",
            "Limitations and robustness",
            "Recommended next checks",
        )
        summary = self._technical_summary(
            regime,
            ordered_strategies,
            agent_signals,
            ordered_risks,
            ordered_backtests,
            ordered_scenarios,
        )
        lines = [
            "=" * 78,
            self.config.report_title,
            "=" * 78,
            f"Generated (UTC): {generated_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}",
            "",
            "TECHNICAL SUMMARY",
            "-" * 78,
            *summary,
            "",
            "SCOPE AND METRIC DEFINITIONS",
            "-" * 78,
            (
                "This report combines one market-regime estimate, ranked strategy "
                "templates, multi-agent evidence, historical asset risk, post-hoc "
                "explanations, historical backtests, and hypothetical stress scenarios."
            ),
            (
                "VaR and CVaR are historical daily return estimates; annual volatility "
                "is sample volatility scaled to a trading year; drawdown is peak-to-"
                "trough loss; model and explanation confidence scores are quality "
                "indicators, not probabilities of profit."
            ),
            (
                f"Evidence coverage: {len(strategies)} strategies, "
                f"{len(risk_reports)} risk reports, {len(explanations)} explanations, "
                f"{len(backtest_results)} backtests, and "
                f"{len(scenario_results)} scenarios."
            ),
            "",
            "MARKET REGIME EVIDENCE",
            "-" * 78,
            f"Detected regime: {regime.name.replace('_', ' ').upper()}",
            f"Regime confidence: {regime.confidence:.1%} (classification quality score)",
        ]
        if regime.characteristics:
            lines.append("Observed characteristics:")
            for name, value in sorted(regime.characteristics.items()):
                lines.append(f"  - {name}: {_format_number(value)}")
        if regime.recommended_adjustments:
            lines.append("Model-proposed allocation adjustments:")
            for name, value in sorted(regime.recommended_adjustments.items()):
                lines.append(f"  - {name}: {float(value):+.1%}")

        lines.extend(["", "STRATEGY CANDIDATES", "-" * 78])
        if not ordered_strategies:
            lines.append("No strategy candidates were supplied.")
        for rank, strategy in enumerate(
            ordered_strategies[: self.config.top_strategies], start=1
        ):
            allocations = ", ".join(
                f"{asset} {weight:.1%}"
                for asset, weight in sorted(
                    strategy.allocations.items(),
                    key=lambda item: item[1],
                    reverse=True,
                )
            )
            lines.extend(
                [
                    f"{rank}. {strategy.name} | score {strategy.score:.3f}",
                    (
                        f"   Type: {strategy.strategy_type}; regime target: "
                        f"{strategy.regime_target}; expected return/volatility: "
                        f"{strategy.expected_return}/{strategy.expected_volatility}."
                    ),
                    f"   Allocation: {allocations or 'not specified'}.",
                ]
            )
            if strategy.rationale:
                lines.append("   Evidence rationale: " + "; ".join(strategy.rationale))

        lines.extend(
            ["", "MULTI-AGENT EVIDENCE AND CONSENSUS", "-" * 78]
        )
        lines.extend(self._agent_section(agent_signals))

        lines.extend(["", "HISTORICAL RISK EVIDENCE", "-" * 78])
        if not ordered_risks:
            lines.append("No historical risk reports were supplied.")
        for report in ordered_risks[: self.config.top_risk_reports]:
            beta = (
                f"{report.beta:.3f}"
                if math.isfinite(float(report.beta))
                else "N/A"
            )
            lines.extend(
                [
                    (
                        f"{report.ticker}: {report.risk_level.upper()} | annual "
                        f"volatility {report.volatility_annual:.1%} | maximum "
                        f"drawdown {report.max_drawdown:.1%}"
                    ),
                    (
                        f"  Historical VaR 95/99: {report.var_95:.2%} / "
                        f"{report.var_99:.2%}; CVaR 95: {report.cvar_95:.2%}; "
                        f"beta: {beta}; Sortino: {report.sortino_ratio:.3f}."
                    ),
                ]
            )
            for source in report.main_risk_sources[:3]:
                lines.append(f"  - {source}")

        lines.extend(["", "EXPLAINABILITY EVIDENCE", "-" * 78])
        if not ordered_explanations:
            lines.append("No post-hoc explanations were supplied.")
        for explanation in ordered_explanations[: self.config.top_explanations]:
            lines.append(
                f"{explanation.ticker}: {explanation.action.upper()} | target "
                f"{explanation.allocation_pct:.1f}% | explanation-quality "
                f"{explanation.confidence:.1%}"
            )
            lines.append(
                f"  Momentum {explanation.momentum_score:+.3f}; fundamental value "
                f"{explanation.value_score:+.3f}; independent sentiment "
                f"{explanation.sentiment_score:+.3f}; risk "
                f"{explanation.risk_score:.3f}."
            )
            for reason in explanation.reasons[:4]:
                lines.append(f"  - {reason}")

        lines.extend(["", "BACKTEST EVIDENCE", "-" * 78])
        if not ordered_backtests:
            lines.append("No historical backtests were supplied.")
        for result in ordered_backtests[: self.config.top_backtests]:
            lines.append(
                f"{result.strategy_name} ({result.period}): total return "
                f"{result.total_return:+.1%}, CAGR {result.cagr:+.1%}, maximum "
                f"drawdown {result.max_drawdown:.1%}, Sharpe "
                f"{result.sharpe_ratio:.3f}, Sortino {result.sortino_ratio:.3f}."
            )
            lines.append(
                f"  Positive-month share {result.win_rate:.1%}; executed orders "
                f"{result.trades}; trailing-stop triggers "
                f"{result.stop_loss_triggers if result.stop_loss_enabled else 'disabled'}."
            )

        lines.extend(["", "SCENARIO STRESS EVIDENCE", "-" * 78])
        if not ordered_scenarios:
            lines.append("No hypothetical stress scenarios were supplied.")
        for scenario in ordered_scenarios[: self.config.top_scenarios]:
            lines.append(
                f"{scenario.scenario_name}: central portfolio return "
                f"{scenario.portfolio_return:+.1%}; configured adverse return "
                f"{scenario.max_loss:+.1%}."
            )
            lines.append(f"  {scenario.description}")
            if scenario.affected_positions:
                affected = ", ".join(
                    f"{ticker} {value:+.1%}"
                    for ticker, value in sorted(
                        scenario.affected_positions.items(),
                        key=lambda item: item[1],
                    )[:5]
                )
                lines.append(f"  Main modeled contributions: {affected}.")

        lines.extend(
            [
                "",
                "LIMITATIONS AND ROBUSTNESS",
                "-" * 78,
                (
                    "- Regime, prediction, sentiment, optimization, and XAI outputs "
                    "are model-dependent estimates and may be wrong."
                ),
                (
                    "- Historical risk and backtest statistics do not include every "
                    "live-trading cost, tax, liquidity shock, delisting, or structural break."
                ),
                (
                    "- Scenario results are hypothetical sensitivities, not forecasts "
                    "or calibrated event probabilities."
                ),
                (
                    "- Strategy scores and agent agreement do not establish suitability "
                    "for a specific investor."
                ),
            ]
        )
        if warnings:
            lines.append("Input and coverage warnings:")
            lines.extend(f"  - {warning}" for warning in warnings)

        lines.extend(
            [
                "",
                "RECOMMENDED NEXT CHECKS",
                "-" * 78,
                "- Confirm data freshness, adjustment policy, and benchmark alignment.",
                "- Compare candidate allocations out of sample and after realistic costs.",
                "- Stress-test estimation choices, turnover limits, and concentration bounds.",
                "- Review conflicts between directional evidence and RiskAgent constraints.",
                "- Treat any allocation change as requiring investor-specific suitability review.",
                "",
                "=" * 78,
                "Decision-support analysis only; not individualized financial advice.",
                "=" * 78,
            ]
        )
        content = "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
        fingerprint = self._evidence_fingerprint(
            regime,
            strategies,
            agent_signals,
            risk_reports,
            explanations,
            backtest_results,
            scenario_results,
            sources,
        )
        diagnostics = ReportDiagnostics(
            generated_at_utc=generated_at.isoformat(),
            evidence_fingerprint=fingerprint,
            section_names=section_names,
            evidence_counts=MappingProxyType(
                {
                    "strategies": len(strategies),
                    "risk_reports": len(risk_reports),
                    "explanations": len(explanations),
                    "backtests": len(backtest_results),
                    "scenarios": len(scenario_results),
                }
            ),
            warnings=tuple(dict.fromkeys(warnings)),
            source_metadata=MappingProxyType(dict(sources)),
        )
        return TextReportArtifact(content=content, diagnostics=diagnostics)

    def _technical_summary(
        self,
        regime: MarketRegime,
        strategies: Sequence[Strategy],
        agents: Mapping[str, Any],
        risks: Sequence[RiskReport],
        backtests: Sequence[BacktestResult],
        scenarios: Sequence[ScenarioResult],
    ) -> list[str]:
        consensus = agents.get("consensus", {}) if isinstance(agents, Mapping) else {}
        portfolio = agents.get("portfolio", {}) if isinstance(agents, Mapping) else {}
        lines = [
            (
                f"- Market state: {regime.name.replace('_', ' ')} at "
                f"{regime.confidence:.0%} classification confidence."
            ),
            (
                f"- Multi-agent stance: "
                f"{str(consensus.get('stance', 'unavailable')).replace('_', ' ')}; "
                f"risk veto {'active' if consensus.get('risk_veto') else 'inactive'}."
            ),
        ]
        if strategies:
            lines.append(
                f"- Highest-ranked strategy: {strategies[0].name} "
                f"(score {strategies[0].score:.3f})."
            )
        if portfolio and portfolio.get("weights"):
            leader = max(portfolio["weights"].items(), key=lambda item: item[1])
            lines.append(
                f"- Proposed portfolio's largest weight: {leader[0]} "
                f"at {float(leader[1]):.1%}."
            )
        if risks:
            lines.append(
                f"- Highest classified asset risk: {risks[0].ticker} "
                f"({risks[0].risk_level}, volatility "
                f"{risks[0].volatility_annual:.1%})."
            )
        if backtests:
            lines.append(
                f"- Best supplied historical Sharpe: {backtests[0].strategy_name} "
                f"at {backtests[0].sharpe_ratio:.3f}; this is not a forecast."
            )
        if scenarios:
            lines.append(
                f"- Most adverse supplied central scenario: "
                f"{scenarios[0].scenario_name} at "
                f"{scenarios[0].portfolio_return:+.1%}."
            )
        return lines

    @staticmethod
    def _agent_section(signals: Mapping[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
        if not signals:
            return ["No multi-agent signals were supplied."]
        consensus = signals.get("consensus", {})
        macro = signals.get("macro", {})
        alpha = signals.get("alpha", {})
        sentiment = signals.get("sentiment", {})
        risk = signals.get("risk", {})
        portfolio = signals.get("portfolio", {})
        lines = [
            (
                f"Consensus: {str(consensus.get('stance', 'unavailable')).upper()} | "
                f"score {float(consensus.get('score', 0)):+.3f} | agreement "
                f"{float(consensus.get('agreement', 0)):.1%} | risk veto "
                f"{'yes' if consensus.get('risk_veto') else 'no'}."
            ),
            (
                f"Macro: {macro.get('regime', 'N/A')} / "
                f"{macro.get('equity_bias', 'N/A')}."
            ),
            (
                f"Alpha: {alpha.get('bullish_count', 0)} bullish, "
                f"{alpha.get('bearish_count', 0)} bearish; top picks "
                f"{', '.join(alpha.get('top_picks', [])) or 'none'}."
            ),
            (
                f"Independent text sentiment: "
                f"{sentiment.get('overall_sentiment', 'unavailable')} "
                f"(coverage {float(sentiment.get('coverage', 0)):.1%}); "
                "price momentum is not labeled as sentiment."
            ),
            (
                f"Portfolio risk action: {risk.get('risk_action', 'N/A')}; "
                f"historical volatility "
                f"{float(risk.get('portfolio_volatility', 0)):.1%}."
            ),
            (
                f"Portfolio optimizer: {portfolio.get('method', 'N/A')}; "
                f"expected return {float(portfolio.get('expected_return', 0)):.1%}; "
                f"expected volatility "
                f"{float(portfolio.get('expected_volatility', 0)):.1%}."
            ),
        ]
        conflicts = consensus.get("conflicts", [])
        if conflicts:
            lines.append("Material conflicts:")
            lines.extend(f"  - {conflict}" for conflict in conflicts)
        return lines

    def _validate_report_inputs(
        self,
        regime: MarketRegime,
        strategies: Sequence[Strategy],
        agent_signals: Mapping[str, Any],
        risk_reports: Mapping[str, RiskReport],
        explanations: Mapping[str, XAIExplanation],
        backtests: Sequence[BacktestResult],
        scenarios: Sequence[ScenarioResult],
    ) -> list[str]:
        if not isinstance(regime, MarketRegime):
            raise ReportingInputError("regime must be a MarketRegime")
        self._typed_sequence(strategies, Strategy, "strategies")
        self._typed_mapping(risk_reports, RiskReport, "risk_reports")
        self._typed_mapping(explanations, XAIExplanation, "explanations")
        self._typed_sequence(backtests, BacktestResult, "backtest_results")
        self._typed_sequence(scenarios, ScenarioResult, "scenario_results")
        if not isinstance(agent_signals, Mapping):
            raise ReportingInputError("agent_signals must be a mapping")
        warnings: list[str] = []
        for key, report in risk_reports.items():
            if str(key).strip().upper() != str(report.ticker).strip().upper():
                warnings.append(
                    f"Risk-report key {key!r} differs from ticker {report.ticker}."
                )
        for key, explanation in explanations.items():
            if str(key).strip().upper() != str(explanation.ticker).strip().upper():
                warnings.append(
                    f"Explanation key {key!r} differs from ticker "
                    f"{explanation.ticker}."
                )
        if not risk_reports:
            warnings.append("Historical asset-risk evidence is absent.")
        if not backtests:
            warnings.append("No out-of-sample or historical backtest evidence is present.")
        if not scenarios:
            warnings.append("No hypothetical scenario evidence is present.")
        return warnings

    @staticmethod
    def _typed_sequence(
        values: Sequence[Any], expected: type, name: str
    ) -> None:
        if isinstance(values, (str, bytes)) or not isinstance(values, Sequence):
            raise ReportingInputError(f"{name} must be a sequence")
        invalid = [index for index, value in enumerate(values) if not isinstance(value, expected)]
        if invalid:
            raise ReportingInputError(
                f"{name} contains invalid items at indexes {invalid[:5]}"
            )

    @staticmethod
    def _typed_mapping(
        values: Mapping[str, Any], expected: type, name: str
    ) -> None:
        if not isinstance(values, Mapping):
            raise ReportingInputError(f"{name} must be a mapping")
        invalid = [str(key) for key, value in values.items() if not isinstance(value, expected)]
        if invalid:
            raise ReportingInputError(
                f"{name} contains invalid values for keys {invalid[:5]}"
            )

    @staticmethod
    def _timestamp(
        supplied: Optional[datetime],
    ) -> tuple[datetime, Optional[str]]:
        value = supplied or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        warning = None
        if value.tzinfo is None:
            value = value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
            warning = "Naive report timestamp was interpreted as UTC."
        return value.astimezone(timezone.utc), warning

    @staticmethod
    def _validated_source_metadata(
        metadata: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]],
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if metadata is None:
            return {}
        if not isinstance(metadata, Mapping):
            raise ReportingInputError("source_metadata must be a mapping")
        return _json_safe(dict(metadata))

    def save_report(
        self,
        report_text: str,
        path: str | os.PathLike[str] = "smartinvest_4_8_report.txt",
    ) -> Path:
        return self.save_report_detailed(report_text, path).path

    def save_report_detailed(
        self,
        report_text: str,
        path: str | os.PathLike[str] = "smartinvest_4_8_report.txt",
    ) -> SavedArtifact:
        if not isinstance(report_text, str) or not report_text.strip():
            raise ReportingInputError("report_text must be a non-empty string")
        return self._atomic_save(report_text, path)

    def build_dashboard_spec(
        self,
        price_data: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame],
        regime: MarketRegime,
        strategies: Sequence[Strategy],
        backtest_results: Sequence[BacktestResult],
        risk_reports: Mapping[str, RiskReport],
        *,
        timestamp: Optional[datetime] = None,
        source_metadata: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None,
    ) -> DashboardSpec:
        if not isinstance(regime, MarketRegime):
            raise ReportingInputError("regime must be a MarketRegime")
        self._typed_sequence(strategies, Strategy, "strategies")
        self._typed_sequence(backtest_results, BacktestResult, "backtest_results")
        self._typed_mapping(risk_reports, RiskReport, "risk_reports")
        generated_at, timestamp_warning = self._timestamp(timestamp)
        sources = self._validated_source_metadata(source_metadata)
        warnings: list[str] = []
        if timestamp_warning:
            warnings.append(timestamp_warning)
        prepared_prices, price_warnings = self._dashboard_prices(price_data)
        warnings.extend(price_warnings)
        data_freshness = (
            max(frame.index[-1] for frame in prepared_prices.values()).isoformat()
            if prepared_prices
            else None
        )
        top_strategy = (
            max(strategies, key=lambda item: float(item.score))
            if strategies
            else None
        )
        datasets: dict[str, tuple[Mapping[str, Any], ...]] = {}
        datasets["regime"] = (
            MappingProxyType(
                {
                    "regime": regime.name,
                    "confidence": float(regime.confidence),
                }
            ),
        )
        risk_counts = Counter(
            str(report.risk_level).strip().lower().replace("_", "-")
            for report in risk_reports.values()
        )
        risk_order = ("low", "medium", "medium-high", "high", "critical")
        datasets["risk_distribution"] = tuple(
            MappingProxyType({"risk_level": level, "asset_count": risk_counts.get(level, 0)})
            for level in risk_order
        )
        price_rows: list[Mapping[str, Any]] = []
        for ticker, close in prepared_prices.items():
            sampled = self._downsample(close)
            indexed = sampled / float(sampled.iloc[0]) * 100.0
            price_rows.extend(
                MappingProxyType(
                    {
                        "date": index.isoformat(),
                        "ticker": ticker,
                        "indexed_value": float(value),
                    }
                )
                for index, value in indexed.items()
            )
        datasets["indexed_prices"] = tuple(price_rows)
        allocations = (
            sorted(top_strategy.allocations.items(), key=lambda item: item[1], reverse=True)
            if top_strategy is not None
            else []
        )
        datasets["allocation"] = tuple(
            MappingProxyType(
                {
                    "asset": asset,
                    "weight": float(weight),
                    "strategy": top_strategy.name,
                }
            )
            for asset, weight in allocations
        )
        equity_rows: list[Mapping[str, Any]] = []
        ordered_backtests = sorted(
            backtest_results,
            key=lambda item: (
                _finite_or_default(item.sharpe_ratio, -math.inf),
                _finite_or_default(item.cagr, -math.inf),
            ),
            reverse=True,
        )
        for result in ordered_backtests[: self.config.top_backtests]:
            curve = self._valid_series(result.equity_curve, f"{result.strategy_name}.equity")
            if len(curve) < self.config.minimum_trend_points:
                warnings.append(
                    f"Backtest {result.strategy_name} omitted from trend chart: "
                    "too few valid points."
                )
                continue
            sampled = self._downsample(curve)
            indexed = sampled / float(sampled.iloc[0]) * 100.0
            equity_rows.extend(
                MappingProxyType(
                    {
                        "date": index.isoformat(),
                        "strategy": result.strategy_name,
                        "indexed_equity": float(value),
                    }
                )
                for index, value in indexed.items()
            )
        datasets["backtest_equity"] = tuple(equity_rows)
        if len(prepared_prices) >= 2:
            returns = pd.DataFrame(prepared_prices).pct_change(fill_method=None).dropna()
            correlation = returns.corr()
            datasets["correlation"] = tuple(
                MappingProxyType(
                    {
                        "asset_x": x,
                        "asset_y": y,
                        "correlation": float(correlation.loc[y, x]),
                    }
                )
                for y in correlation.index
                for x in correlation.columns
            )
        else:
            datasets["correlation"] = ()
            warnings.append("Correlation matrix requires at least two valid price series.")
        charts = self._chart_contracts(
            has_allocation=bool(datasets["allocation"]),
            has_equity=bool(datasets["backtest_equity"]),
            has_correlation=bool(datasets["correlation"]),
            source_ids=tuple(sources),
        )
        if not sources:
            warnings.append(
                "Canonical source identity was not supplied; dashboard provenance "
                "is limited to in-memory inputs."
            )
        serializable = {
            "title": self.config.dashboard_title,
            "generated_at": generated_at.isoformat(),
            "freshness": data_freshness,
            "datasets": {
                key: [dict(row) for row in rows] for key, rows in datasets.items()
            },
            "sources": sources,
        }
        fingerprint = hashlib.sha256(
            json.dumps(serializable, sort_keys=True, default=str).encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest()
        return DashboardSpec(
            title=self.config.dashboard_title,
            generated_at_utc=generated_at.isoformat(),
            data_freshness=data_freshness,
            datasets=MappingProxyType(datasets),
            charts=charts,
            sources=MappingProxyType(sources),
            warnings=tuple(dict.fromkeys(warnings)),
            fingerprint=fingerprint,
        )

    def _dashboard_prices(
        self, price_data: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame]
    ) -> tuple[dict[str, pd.Series], list[str]]:
        if not isinstance(price_data, Mapping) or not price_data:
            raise ReportingInputError("price_data must be a non-empty mapping")
        candidates: list[tuple[str, pd.Series]] = []
        warnings: list[str] = []
        seen: set[str] = set()
        for raw_ticker, frame in price_data.items():
            ticker = str(raw_ticker).strip().upper()
            if not ticker or ticker in seen:
                raise ReportingInputError(
                    f"invalid or duplicate price ticker: {ticker!r}"
                )
            seen.add(ticker)
            if not isinstance(frame, pd.DataFrame) or "Close" not in frame.columns:
                warnings.append(f"{ticker}: omitted because Close is unavailable.")
                continue
            close_column = frame["Close"]
            if isinstance(close_column, pd.DataFrame):
                if close_column.shape[1] != 1:
                    warnings.append(
                        f"{ticker}: omitted because Close contains multiple columns."
                    )
                    continue
                close_column = close_column.iloc[:, 0]

            close = pd.to_numeric(close_column, errors="coerce").copy()
            if not isinstance(close.index, pd.DatetimeIndex):
                try:
                    close.index = pd.to_datetime(close.index, errors="raise")
                except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):
                    warnings.append(f"{ticker}: omitted because dates are invalid.")
                    continue
            close = close.loc[~close.index.duplicated(keep="last")].sort_index()
            if close.index.tz is not None:
                close.index = close.index.tz_convert("UTC").tz_localize(None)
            close = close.loc[close.notna() & np.isfinite(close) & (close > 0)]
            if len(close) < self.config.minimum_trend_points:
                warnings.append(
                    f"{ticker}: omitted because fewer than "
                    f"{self.config.minimum_trend_points} valid points are available."
                )
                continue
            candidates.append((ticker, close.astype(float)))
        candidates.sort(key=lambda item: len(item[1]), reverse=True)
        if len(candidates) > self.config.maximum_dashboard_assets:
            warnings.append(
                f"Dashboard limited to {self.config.maximum_dashboard_assets} "
                "assets with the greatest data coverage."
            )
        selected = dict(candidates[: self.config.maximum_dashboard_assets])
        if not selected:
            raise ReportingInputError("no price series is suitable for the dashboard")
        return selected, warnings

    def _downsample(self, series: pd.Series) -> pd.Series:
        maximum = self.config.maximum_chart_points_per_series
        if len(series) <= maximum:
            return series
        positions = np.linspace(0, len(series) - 1, maximum, dtype=int)
        return series.iloc[np.unique(positions)]

    @staticmethod
    def _valid_series(series: pd.Series, name: str) -> pd.Series:
        if not isinstance(series, pd.Series):
            raise ReportingInputError(f"{name} must be a pandas Series")

        numeric = pd.to_numeric(series, errors="coerce")
        numeric = numeric.loc[
            numeric.notna() & np.isfinite(numeric) & (numeric > 0)
        ].copy()

        if numeric.empty:
            return numeric.astype(float)

        if not isinstance(numeric.index, pd.DatetimeIndex):
            try:
                numeric.index = pd.to_datetime(numeric.index, errors="raise")
            except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError) as exc:
                raise ReportingInputError(
                    f"{name} must use a valid datetime-like index"
                ) from exc

        if numeric.index.tz is not None:
            numeric.index = numeric.index.tz_convert("UTC").tz_localize(None)

        numeric = numeric.loc[~numeric.index.duplicated(keep="last")].sort_index()
        return numeric.astype(float)

    @staticmethod
    def _chart_contracts(
        *,
        has_allocation: bool,
        has_equity: bool,
        has_correlation: bool,
        source_ids: tuple[str, ...],
    ) -> tuple[ChartContract, ...]:
        contracts = [
            ChartContract(
                "regime_confidence",
                "Current state",
                "How strongly does the detector support the active regime?",
                "Show only the observed active-regime confidence; do not invent alternatives.",
                "Tables & Scorecards",
                "indicator",
                ("regime", "confidence"),
                source_ids,
                "single KPI with exact confidence",
            ),
            ChartContract(
                "risk_distribution",
                "Current state",
                "How are covered assets distributed across risk classifications?",
                "Counts show coverage and concentration by discrete risk level.",
                "Comparison & Ranking",
                "bar",
                ("risk_level", "asset_count"),
                source_ids,
                "compact count table",
            ),
            ChartContract(
                "indexed_prices",
                "Market movement",
                "How did supplied assets perform from a common base?",
                "Indexed lines compare path and relative movement, not absolute price.",
                "Trend",
                "line",
                ("date", "ticker", "indexed_value"),
                source_ids,
                "table of first, last, and indexed change",
            ),
        ]
        if has_allocation:
            contracts.append(
                ChartContract(
                    "allocation",
                    "Portfolio composition",
                    "What are the largest weights in the top-ranked strategy?",
                    "Sorted bars expose concentration and the full-investment denominator.",
                    "Composition",
                    "horizontal_bar",
                    ("asset", "weight", "strategy"),
                    source_ids,
                    "sorted allocation table",
                )
            )
        if has_equity:
            contracts.append(
                ChartContract(
                    "backtest_equity",
                    "Historical validation",
                    "How did supplied backtests evolve from a common starting value?",
                    "Indexed equity compares historical paths; it is not a forecast.",
                    "Trend",
                    "line",
                    ("date", "strategy", "indexed_equity"),
                    source_ids,
                    "backtest summary table",
                )
            )
        if has_correlation:
            contracts.append(
                ChartContract(
                    "correlation",
                    "Diversification structure",
                    "Which supplied assets moved together historically?",
                    "The matrix shows pairwise historical return correlation.",
                    "Matrix & Cohort",
                    "heatmap",
                    ("asset_x", "asset_y", "correlation"),
                    source_ids,
                    "correlation table",
                )
            )
        return tuple(contracts)

    def generate_plotly_dashboard(
        self,
        price_data: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame],
        regime: MarketRegime,
        strategies: Sequence[Strategy],
        backtest_results: Sequence[BacktestResult],
        risk_reports: Mapping[str, RiskReport],
        *,
        output_path: str | os.PathLike[str] = "smartinvest_4_8_dashboard.html",
        timestamp: Optional[datetime] = None,
        source_metadata: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None,
    ) -> bool:
        """Compatibility wrapper returning False when optional rendering fails."""

        try:
            self.generate_plotly_dashboard_detailed(
                price_data,
                regime,
                strategies,
                backtest_results,
                risk_reports,
                output_path=output_path,
                timestamp=timestamp,
                source_metadata=source_metadata,
            )
            return True
        except ReportingError as exc:
            LOGGER.error("Dashboard generation failed: %s", exc)
            return False

    def generate_plotly_dashboard_detailed(
        self,
        price_data: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame],
        regime: MarketRegime,
        strategies: Sequence[Strategy],
        backtest_results: Sequence[BacktestResult],
        risk_reports: Mapping[str, RiskReport],
        *,
        output_path: str | os.PathLike[str] = "smartinvest_4_8_dashboard.html",
        timestamp: Optional[datetime] = None,
        source_metadata: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None,
    ) -> DashboardArtifact:
        if not PLOTLY_AVAILABLE:
            raise ReportingDependencyError(
                "Plotly is not installed. Install the optional 'plotly' dependency "
                "or use build_dashboard_spec() for a renderer-independent contract."
            )
        spec = self.build_dashboard_spec(
            price_data,
            regime,
            strategies,
            backtest_results,
            risk_reports,
            timestamp=timestamp,
            source_metadata=source_metadata,
        )
        figure = self._plotly_figure(spec)
        html = figure.to_html(
            full_html=True,
            include_plotlyjs=self.config.include_plotlyjs,
            config={
                "responsive": True,
                "displaylogo": False,
                "scrollZoom": False,
            },
        )
        saved = self._atomic_save(html, output_path)
        return DashboardArtifact(
            saved=saved,
            specification=spec,
            renderer="plotly",
            offline=self.config.include_plotlyjs == "inline",
        )

    def _plotly_figure(self, spec: DashboardSpec) -> Any:
        figure = make_subplots(
            rows=3,
            cols=2,
            subplot_titles=(
                "Active regime confidence",
                "Assets by historical risk level",
                "Indexed asset performance",
                "Top strategy allocation",
                "Indexed backtest equity",
                "Historical return correlation",
            ),
            specs=(
                ({"type": "indicator"}, {"type": "bar"}),
                ({"type": "scatter"}, {"type": "bar"}),
                ({"type": "scatter"}, {"type": "heatmap"}),
            ),
            vertical_spacing=0.12,
            horizontal_spacing=0.10,
        )
        regime_row = dict(spec.datasets["regime"][0])
        figure.add_trace(
            go.Indicator(
                mode="number+gauge",
                value=float(regime_row["confidence"]),
                number={"valueformat": ".1%", "font": {"color": "#20252B"}},
                title={"text": str(regime_row["regime"]).replace("_", " ").upper()},
                gauge={
                    "axis": {"range": [0, 1], "tickformat": ".0%"},
                    "bar": {"color": _PALETTE[0]},
                    "bgcolor": "#EEF2F6",
                    "steps": [
                        {"range": [0, 0.5], "color": "#F5F7FA"},
                        {"range": [0.5, 1], "color": "#DCE7FF"},
                    ],
                },
            ),
            row=1,
            col=1,
        )
        risk_rows = [dict(row) for row in spec.datasets["risk_distribution"]]
        figure.add_trace(
            go.Bar(
                x=[row["risk_level"] for row in risk_rows],
                y=[row["asset_count"] for row in risk_rows],
                marker={
                    "color": ["#D8DEE6", "#AEB9C6", _PALETTE[1], _PALETTE[2], "#8A4B2A"],
                    "line": {"color": "#3D4650", "width": 0.8},
                },
                text=[row["asset_count"] for row in risk_rows],
                textposition="outside",
                name="Asset count",
                showlegend=False,
                hovertemplate="%{x}: %{y} assets<extra></extra>",
            ),
            row=1,
            col=2,
        )
        price_frame = pd.DataFrame([dict(row) for row in spec.datasets["indexed_prices"]])
        for index, (ticker, group) in enumerate(price_frame.groupby("ticker", sort=False)):
            figure.add_trace(
                go.Scatter(
                    x=group["date"],
                    y=group["indexed_value"],
                    mode="lines",
                    name=str(ticker),
                    line={
                        "color": _PALETTE[index % len(_PALETTE)],
                        "dash": _DASH_PATTERNS[index % len(_DASH_PATTERNS)],
                        "width": 2,
                    },
                    hovertemplate=(
                        "%{x}<br>Indexed value %{y:.1f}"
                        f"<extra>{ticker}</extra>"
                    ),
                ),
                row=2,
                col=1,
            )
        allocation_rows = [dict(row) for row in spec.datasets["allocation"]]
        if allocation_rows:
            allocation_rows.sort(key=lambda row: row["weight"])
            figure.add_trace(
                go.Bar(
                    x=[row["weight"] for row in allocation_rows],
                    y=[row["asset"] for row in allocation_rows],
                    orientation="h",
                    marker={"color": _PALETTE[0], "line": {"color": "#203A70", "width": 0.8}},
                    text=[f"{row['weight']:.1%}" for row in allocation_rows],
                    textposition="outside",
                    name="Weight",
                    showlegend=False,
                    hovertemplate="%{y}: %{x:.1%}<extra></extra>",
                ),
                row=2,
                col=2,
            )
        equity_frame = pd.DataFrame(
            [dict(row) for row in spec.datasets["backtest_equity"]]
        )
        if not equity_frame.empty:
            for index, (strategy, group) in enumerate(
                equity_frame.groupby("strategy", sort=False)
            ):
                figure.add_trace(
                    go.Scatter(
                        x=group["date"],
                        y=group["indexed_equity"],
                        mode="lines",
                        name=str(strategy),
                        line={
                            "color": _PALETTE[index % len(_PALETTE)],
                            "dash": _DASH_PATTERNS[index % len(_DASH_PATTERNS)],
                            "width": 2,
                        },
                        hovertemplate=(
                            "%{x}<br>Indexed equity %{y:.1f}"
                            f"<extra>{strategy}</extra>"
                        ),
                    ),
                    row=3,
                    col=1,
                )
        correlation_rows = [dict(row) for row in spec.datasets["correlation"]]
        if correlation_rows:
            correlation_frame = pd.DataFrame(correlation_rows)
            matrix = correlation_frame.pivot(
                index="asset_y", columns="asset_x", values="correlation"
            )
            figure.add_trace(
                go.Heatmap(
                    z=matrix.values,
                    x=matrix.columns,
                    y=matrix.index,
                    zmin=-1,
                    zmax=1,
                    zmid=0,
                    colorscale=(
                        (0.0, "#2F6BFF"),
                        (0.5, "#F7F8FA"),
                        (1.0, "#E67E22"),
                    ),
                    colorbar={"title": "Correlation"},
                    hovertemplate="%{y} / %{x}: %{z:.2f}<extra></extra>",
                ),
                row=3,
                col=2,
            )
        freshness = spec.data_freshness or "unavailable"
        figure.update_layout(
            title={
                "text": (
                    f"{spec.title}<br><sup>Snapshot generated "
                    f"{_human_datetime(spec.generated_at_utc)}; data through "
                    f"{_human_datetime(freshness)}</sup>"
                ),
                "x": 0.02,
                "xanchor": "left",
            },
            template="plotly_white",
            height=1_250,
            margin={"l": 80, "r": 60, "t": 120, "b": 90},
            font={"family": "Arial, sans-serif", "color": "#20252B"},
            paper_bgcolor="#FFFFFF",
            plot_bgcolor="#FFFFFF",
            legend={"orientation": "h", "yanchor": "bottom", "y": -0.10},
            meta={
                "fingerprint": spec.fingerprint,
                "sources": dict(spec.sources),
                "warnings": list(spec.warnings),
            },
        )
        figure.update_xaxes(showgrid=False, linecolor="#9AA5B1")
        figure.update_yaxes(gridcolor="#E6EAF0", linecolor="#9AA5B1")
        figure.update_yaxes(title_text="Indexed value (start = 100)", row=2, col=1)
        figure.update_xaxes(tickformat=".0%", title_text="Portfolio weight", row=2, col=2)
        figure.update_yaxes(title_text="Indexed equity (start = 100)", row=3, col=1)
        figure.add_annotation(
            text=(
                "Historical and hypothetical decision support only. "
                "Confidence scores are not probabilities of profit."
            ),
            xref="paper",
            yref="paper",
            x=0,
            y=-0.16,
            showarrow=False,
            align="left",
            font={"size": 11, "color": "#5B6570"},
        )
        return figure

    def _atomic_save(
        self,
        content: str,
        path: str | os.PathLike[str],
    ) -> SavedArtifact:
        target = Path(path).expanduser()
        if not target.name:
            raise ReportingIOError("output path must include a filename")
        parent = target.parent
        if self.config.create_parent_directories:
            parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        if not parent.exists() or not parent.is_dir():
            raise ReportingIOError(f"output directory does not exist: {parent}")
        if target.exists() and target.is_dir():
            raise ReportingIOError(f"output path is a directory: {target}")
        if target.exists() and not self.config.overwrite_existing:
            raise ReportingIOError(f"output already exists: {target}")
        encoded = content.encode("utf-8")
        temporary_path: Optional[Path] = None
        with self._io_lock:
            try:
                with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(
                    mode="wb",
                    dir=parent,
                    prefix=f".{target.name}.",
                    suffix=".tmp",
                    delete=False,
                ) as handle:
                    handle.write(encoded)
                    handle.flush()
                    os.fsync(handle.fileno())
                    temporary_path = Path(handle.name)
                os.replace(temporary_path, target)
            except OSError as exc:
                if temporary_path is not None:
                    try:
                        temporary_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
                    except OSError:
                        pass
                raise ReportingIOError(f"failed to save {target}: {exc}") from exc
        resolved = target.resolve()
        LOGGER.info("Artifact saved atomically to %s", resolved)
        return SavedArtifact(
            path=resolved,
            size_bytes=len(encoded),
            sha256=hashlib.sha256(encoded).hexdigest(),
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _evidence_fingerprint(*values: Any) -> str:
        payload = _json_safe(values)
        return hashlib.sha256(
            json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, default=str).encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest()


def _json_safe(value: Any) -> Any:
    if value is None or isinstance(value, (str, int, bool)):
        return value
    if isinstance(value, float):
        return value if math.isfinite(value) else None
    if isinstance(value, np.generic):
        return _json_safe(value.item())
    if isinstance(value, datetime):
        return value.isoformat()
    if isinstance(value, pd.Timestamp):
        return value.isoformat()
    if isinstance(value, pd.Series):
        return {
            "index": [str(item) for item in value.index],
            "values": [_json_safe(item) for item in value.tolist()],
        }
    if isinstance(value, pd.DataFrame):
        return {
            "columns": [str(column) for column in value.columns],
            "records": [
                {str(key): _json_safe(item) for key, item in row.items()}
                for row in value.to_dict(orient="records")
            ],
        }
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return {str(key): _json_safe(item) for key, item in value.items()}
    if isinstance(value, set):
        return sorted(
            (_json_safe(item) for item in value),
            key=lambda item: json.dumps(item, sort_keys=True, default=str),
        )
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
        return [_json_safe(item) for item in value]
    if hasattr(value, "__dataclass_fields__"):
        return {
            name: _json_safe(getattr(value, name))
            for name in value.__dataclass_fields__
        }
    return str(value)


def _format_number(value: Any) -> str:
    number = _finite_or_default(value, math.nan)
    return f"{number:.4f}" if math.isfinite(number) else "N/A"


def _finite_or_default(value: Any, default: float) -> float:
    try:
        number = float(value)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return default
    return number if math.isfinite(number) else default


def _human_datetime(value: str) -> str:
    try:
        parsed = pd.Timestamp(value)
        return parsed.strftime("%d %b %Y %H:%M UTC")
    except Exception:
        return str(value)

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import logging
import math
import signal
import sys
import threading
import time
import uuid
