# This distribution is a single-file build. All components are defined above.


LOGGER = logging.getLogger(__name__)
_RISK_TOLERANCES = frozenset({"low", "medium", "high"})
_OPTIMIZERS = frozenset(
    {"auto", "max_sharpe", "min_volatility", "risk_parity", "black_litterman"}
)
_STAGE_SUCCESS = frozenset({"success", "partial", "skipped", "failed"})


class OrchestratorError(RuntimeError):
    """Base error for orchestration failures."""


class PipelineBusyError(OrchestratorError):
    """Raised when the same orchestrator is already running."""


class PipelineCancelledError(OrchestratorError):
    """Raised after cooperative cancellation was requested."""


class PipelineStageError(OrchestratorError):
    """A required pipeline stage failed."""

    def __init__(self, stage: str, message: str) -> None:
        self.stage = stage
        super().__init__(f"{stage}: {message}")


def _orchestrator_normalise_tickers(values: Sequence[str]) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    if isinstance(values, (str, bytes)):
        raise ValueError("tickers must be a sequence, not one string")
    tickers: list[str] = []
    for value in values:
        if not isinstance(value, str):
            raise ValueError("every ticker must be a string")
        ticker = value.strip().upper()
        if not ticker or any(char.isspace() for char in ticker):
            raise ValueError(f"invalid ticker: {value!r}")
        if ticker not in tickers:
            tickers.append(ticker)
    if not tickers:
        raise ValueError("at least one ticker is required")
    return tuple(tickers)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class OrchestratorConfig:
    """Validated controls for one or more repeatable pipeline runs."""

    tickers: tuple[str, ...]
    benchmark: str = "SPY"
    lookback_days: int = 365 * 3
    prediction_horizon: int = 5
    risk_tolerance: str = "medium"
    opt_method: str = "max_sharpe"
    run_backtests: bool = True
    run_scenarios: bool = True
    generate_dashboard: bool = True
    save_report: bool = True
    persist_results: bool = True
    strict_data: bool = False
    strict_stages: bool = False
    allow_synthetic: bool = False
    force_refresh: bool = False
    min_price_rows: int = 80
    min_feature_rows: int = 50
    max_workers: int = 8
    max_backtests: int = 3
    history_limit: int = 20
    use_trailing_stop: bool = True
    sl_vol_multiplier: float = 2.0
    volatility_window: int = 20
    max_turnover: Optional[float] = None
    scenario_keys: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    report_path: Path = Path("smartinvest_4_8_report.txt")
    dashboard_path: Path = Path("smartinvest_4_8_dashboard.html")

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        object.__setattr__(self, "tickers", _orchestrator_normalise_tickers(self.tickers))
        benchmark = self.benchmark.strip().upper()
        if not benchmark or any(char.isspace() for char in benchmark):
            raise ValueError("benchmark must be a valid ticker")
        object.__setattr__(self, "benchmark", benchmark)
        object.__setattr__(
            self,
            "scenario_keys",
            tuple(dict.fromkeys(str(key).strip() for key in self.scenario_keys if str(key).strip())),
        )
        object.__setattr__(self, "report_path", Path(self.report_path))
        object.__setattr__(self, "dashboard_path", Path(self.dashboard_path))

        if not 30 <= self.lookback_days <= 365 * 50:
            raise ValueError("lookback_days must be between 30 and 18,250")
        if not 1 <= self.prediction_horizon <= 252:
            raise ValueError("prediction_horizon must be between 1 and 252")
        if self.risk_tolerance not in _RISK_TOLERANCES:
            raise ValueError(f"risk_tolerance must be one of {sorted(_RISK_TOLERANCES)}")
        if self.opt_method not in _OPTIMIZERS:
            raise ValueError(f"opt_method must be one of {sorted(_OPTIMIZERS)}")
        if self.min_price_rows < 30 or self.min_feature_rows < 20:
            raise ValueError("minimum row requirements are too small")
        if self.min_feature_rows >= self.min_price_rows:
            raise ValueError("min_feature_rows must be smaller than min_price_rows")
        if not 1 <= self.max_workers <= 64:
            raise ValueError("max_workers must be between 1 and 64")
        if self.max_backtests < 0 or self.history_limit < 1:
            raise ValueError("max_backtests must be non-negative and history_limit positive")
        if not math.isfinite(self.sl_vol_multiplier) or self.sl_vol_multiplier <= 0:
            raise ValueError("sl_vol_multiplier must be positive and finite")
        if self.volatility_window < 2:
            raise ValueError("volatility_window must be at least 2")
        if self.max_turnover is not None and not 0.0 <= self.max_turnover <= 2.0:
            raise ValueError("max_turnover must be between 0 and 2")

    @classmethod
    def from_secure_config(
        cls, secure_config: SecureConfig, **overrides: Any
    ) -> "OrchestratorConfig":
        values: dict[str, Any] = {
            "tickers": tuple(secure_config.default_tickers),
            "benchmark": secure_config.benchmark,
            "lookback_days": secure_config.lookback_days,
        }
        values.update(overrides)
        return cls(**values)

    def fingerprint(self) -> str:
        payload = asdict(self)
        payload["report_path"] = str(self.report_path)
        payload["dashboard_path"] = str(self.dashboard_path)
        return hashlib.sha256(
            json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")).encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest()[:16]


@dataclass(slots=True)
class SmartInvestComponents:
    """Dependency bundle; useful for tests, alternate providers and deployments."""

    db: Any
    data_engine: Any
    regime_detector: Any
    ensemble_ai: Any
    strategy_engine: Any
    scenario_simulator: Any
    risk_intelligence: Any
    xai: Any
    backtest_lab: Any
    optimizer: Any
    mas: Any
    reporter: Any

    @classmethod
    def build_default(cls, secure_config: SecureConfig) -> "SmartInvestComponents":
        db = Database(secure_config.database_path)
        data_engine = MarketDataEngine(db)
        regime_detector = MarketRegimeDetector(n_components=5)
        ensemble_ai = EnsembleAI()
        strategy_engine = StrategyEngine()
        scenario_simulator = ScenarioSimulator()
        risk_intelligence = RiskIntelligence()
        xai = ExplainableAI()
        backtest_lab = BacktestingLab(risk_intelligence=risk_intelligence)
        optimizer = PortfolioOptimizer()
        mas = MultiAgentSystem(
            data_engine,
            regime_detector,
            ensemble_ai,
            risk_intelligence,
            optimizer,
        )
        return cls(
            db=db,
            data_engine=data_engine,
            regime_detector=regime_detector,
            ensemble_ai=ensemble_ai,
            strategy_engine=strategy_engine,
            scenario_simulator=scenario_simulator,
            risk_intelligence=risk_intelligence,
            xai=xai,
            backtest_lab=backtest_lab,
            optimizer=optimizer,
            mas=mas,
            reporter=ReportGenerator(),
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class StageStatus:
    name: str
    status: str
    duration_ms: float
    records: int = 0
    error: Optional[str] = None
    warnings: tuple[str, ...] = ()

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.status not in _STAGE_SUCCESS:
            raise ValueError(f"invalid stage status: {self.status}")


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PipelineResult:
    run_id: str
    started_at: datetime
    finished_at: datetime
    config_fingerprint: str
    regime: MarketRegime
    predictions: Mapping[str, EnsemblePrediction]
    strategies: tuple[Strategy, ...]
    risk_reports: Mapping[str, RiskReport]
    explanations: Mapping[str, XAIExplanation]
    agent_signals: Mapping[str, Any]
    backtest_results: tuple[BacktestResult, ...]
    scenario_results: tuple[ScenarioResult, ...]
    report_text: str
    report_path: Optional[Path]
    dashboard_path: Optional[Path]
    stages: tuple[StageStatus, ...]
    warnings: tuple[str, ...]

    @property
    def elapsed_seconds(self) -> float:
        return max(0.0, (self.finished_at - self.started_at).total_seconds())

    @property
    def partial(self) -> bool:
        return any(stage.status in {"partial", "failed"} for stage in self.stages)


class _StageRecorder:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.items: list[StageStatus] = []

    def add(
        self,
        name: str,
        started: float,
        *,
        status: str = "success",
        records: int = 0,
        error: Optional[str] = None,
        warnings: Sequence[str] = (),
    ) -> None:
        self.items.append(
            StageStatus(
                name=name,
                status=status,
                duration_ms=round((time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0, 3),
                records=records,
                error=error,
                warnings=tuple(warnings),
            )
        )


class SmartInvest48:
    """Run the complete SmartInvest workflow with explicit failure semantics."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        tickers: Optional[Sequence[str]] = None,
        lookback_days: Optional[int] = None,
        risk_tolerance: str = "medium",
        opt_method: str = "max_sharpe",
        run_backtest: bool = True,
        run_scenarios: bool = True,
        generate_dashboard: bool = True,
        use_trailing_stop: bool = True,
        sl_vol_multiplier: float = 2.0,
        volatility_window: int = 20,
        *,
        config: Optional[OrchestratorConfig] = None,
        secure_config: Optional[SecureConfig] = None,
        components: Optional[SmartInvestComponents] = None,
    ) -> None:
        secure = secure_config or SecureConfig.from_env()
        if config is None:
            config = OrchestratorConfig.from_secure_config(
                secure,
                tickers=tuple(tickers) if tickers is not None else tuple(secure.default_tickers),
                lookback_days=(
                    secure.lookback_days if lookback_days is None else lookback_days
                ),
                risk_tolerance=risk_tolerance,
                opt_method=opt_method,
                run_backtests=run_backtest,
                run_scenarios=run_scenarios,
                generate_dashboard=generate_dashboard,
                use_trailing_stop=use_trailing_stop,
                sl_vol_multiplier=sl_vol_multiplier,
                volatility_window=volatility_window,
            )
        self.config = config
        self.components = components or SmartInvestComponents.build_default(secure)
        self._run_lock = threading.Lock()
        self._history_lock = threading.RLock()
        self._history: list[PipelineResult] = []

        # Backward-compatible component attributes.
        for name in SmartInvestComponents.__dataclass_fields__:
            setattr(self, name, getattr(self.components, name))

        LOGGER.info(
            "SmartInvest orchestrator initialized (tickers=%d, config=%s)",
            len(config.tickers),
            config.fingerprint(),
        )

    @property
    def last_result(self) -> Optional[PipelineResult]:
        with self._history_lock:
            return self._history[-1] if self._history else None

    def history(self) -> tuple[PipelineResult, ...]:
        with self._history_lock:
            return tuple(self._history)

    def run(
        self,
        *,
        cancellation_event: Optional[threading.Event] = None,
        news_texts: Optional[Mapping[str, Sequence[str]]] = None,
        current_weights: Optional[Mapping[str, float]] = None,
    ) -> str:
        """Backward-compatible convenience API returning the generated report."""
        return self.run_detailed(
            cancellation_event=cancellation_event,
            news_texts=news_texts,
            current_weights=current_weights,
        ).report_text

    def run_detailed(
        self,
        *,
        cancellation_event: Optional[threading.Event] = None,
        news_texts: Optional[Mapping[str, Sequence[str]]] = None,
        current_weights: Optional[Mapping[str, float]] = None,
    ) -> PipelineResult:
        """Execute one non-reentrant, cooperatively cancellable analysis run."""
        if not self._run_lock.acquire(blocking=False):
            raise PipelineBusyError("this SmartInvest instance is already running")

        started_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        run_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
        stages = _StageRecorder()
        warnings: list[str] = []
        try:
            self._check_cancelled(cancellation_event)
            price_data = self._fetch_data(stages, warnings)
            self._check_cancelled(cancellation_event)
            features = self._compute_features(price_data, stages, warnings)
            self._check_cancelled(cancellation_event)
            predictions = self._train_and_predict(price_data, features, stages, warnings)
            self._check_cancelled(cancellation_event)
            regime, regime_ticker = self._detect_regime(price_data, stages, warnings)
            strategies = self._generate_strategies(regime, predictions, stages)
            self._check_cancelled(cancellation_event)
            risk_reports = self._analyze_risk(
                price_data, regime_ticker, stages, warnings
            )
            explanations = self._explain(
                predictions, risk_reports, features, strategies, stages, warnings
            )
            self._check_cancelled(cancellation_event)
            agent_signals = self._run_agents(
                price_data,
                regime,
                predictions,
                stages,
                warnings,
                news_texts,
                current_weights,
            )
            backtests = self._run_backtests(strategies, price_data, stages, warnings)
            scenarios = self._run_scenarios(strategies, stages, warnings)
            self._persist(regime, strategies, backtests, scenarios, stages, warnings)

            source_metadata = self._source_metadata(price_data, run_id)
            report_text = self._build_report(
                regime,
                strategies,
                agent_signals,
                risk_reports,
                explanations,
                backtests,
                scenarios,
                source_metadata,
                started_at,
                stages,
            )
            report_path = self._save_report(report_text, stages, warnings)
            dashboard_path = self._create_dashboard(
                price_data,
                regime,
                strategies,
                backtests,
                risk_reports,
                source_metadata,
                started_at,
                stages,
                warnings,
            )
            finished_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
            result = PipelineResult(
                run_id=run_id,
                started_at=started_at,
                finished_at=finished_at,
                config_fingerprint=self.config.fingerprint(),
                regime=regime,
                predictions=MappingProxyType(dict(predictions)),
                strategies=tuple(strategies),
                risk_reports=MappingProxyType(dict(risk_reports)),
                explanations=MappingProxyType(dict(explanations)),
                agent_signals=MappingProxyType(dict(agent_signals)),
                backtest_results=tuple(backtests),
                scenario_results=tuple(scenarios),
                report_text=report_text,
                report_path=report_path,
                dashboard_path=dashboard_path,
                stages=tuple(stages.items),
                warnings=tuple(warnings),
            )
            with self._history_lock:
                self._history.append(result)
                del self._history[:-self.config.history_limit]
            LOGGER.info(
                "Analysis %s completed in %.2fs (partial=%s)",
                run_id,
                result.elapsed_seconds,
                result.partial,
            )
            return result
        finally:
            self._run_lock.release()

    def _fetch_data(
        self, stages: _StageRecorder, warnings: list[str]
    ) -> dict[str, pd.DataFrame]:
        started = time.perf_counter()
        universe = list(self.config.tickers)
        if self.config.benchmark not in universe:
            universe.append(self.config.benchmark)
        try:
            raw = self.data_engine.fetch_multiple(
                universe,
                self.config.lookback_days,
                self.config.max_workers,
                force_refresh=self.config.force_refresh,
                allow_synthetic=self.config.allow_synthetic,
                strict=self.config.strict_data,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            stages.add("market_data", started, status="failed", error=str(exc))
            raise PipelineStageError("market_data", str(exc)) from exc

        accepted: dict[str, pd.DataFrame] = {}
        rejected: list[str] = []
        for ticker in universe:
            frame = raw.get(ticker)
            source = str(getattr(frame, "attrs", {}).get("source", "")).lower()
            valid = (
                isinstance(frame, pd.DataFrame)
                and "Close" in frame.columns
                and len(frame) >= self.config.min_price_rows
                and (self.config.allow_synthetic or source != "synthetic")
            )
            if valid:
                accepted[ticker] = frame.copy(deep=False)
            else:
                rejected.append(ticker)
        if not accepted:
            message = "no market series passed validation"
            stages.add("market_data", started, status="failed", error=message)
            raise PipelineStageError("market_data", message)
        if rejected and self.config.strict_data:
            message = f"rejected market data for: {', '.join(rejected)}"
            stages.add("market_data", started, status="failed", error=message)
            raise PipelineStageError("market_data", message)
        stage_warnings = (
            [f"Rejected market data for: {', '.join(rejected)}"] if rejected else []
        )
        warnings.extend(stage_warnings)
        stages.add(
            "market_data",
            started,
            status="partial" if rejected else "success",
            records=len(accepted),
            warnings=stage_warnings,
        )
        return accepted

    def _compute_features(
        self,
        price_data: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame],
        stages: _StageRecorder,
        warnings: list[str],
    ) -> dict[str, pd.DataFrame]:
        started = time.perf_counter()
        features: dict[str, pd.DataFrame] = {}
        failures: list[str] = []
        for ticker, frame in price_data.items():
            try:
                value = self.data_engine.compute_features(frame, drop_incomplete=True)
                if not isinstance(value, pd.DataFrame) or len(value) < self.config.min_feature_rows:
                    raise ValueError("insufficient complete feature rows")
                features[ticker] = value
            except Exception as exc:
                failures.append(f"{ticker}: {exc}")
        if not features:
            stages.add("features", started, status="failed", error="; ".join(failures))
            raise PipelineStageError("features", "no usable feature matrices")
        if failures and self.config.strict_stages:
            stages.add("features", started, status="failed", error="; ".join(failures))
            raise PipelineStageError("features", "one or more feature jobs failed")
        warnings.extend(failures)
        stages.add(
            "features",
            started,
            status="partial" if failures else "success",
            records=len(features),
            warnings=failures,
        )
        return features

    def _train_and_predict(
        self,
        price_data: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame],
        features: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame],
        stages: _StageRecorder,
        warnings: list[str],
    ) -> dict[str, EnsemblePrediction]:
        started = time.perf_counter()
        predictions: dict[str, EnsemblePrediction] = {}
        failures: list[str] = []
        for ticker in self.config.tickers:
            if ticker not in features or ticker not in price_data:
                continue
            try:
                trained = self.ensemble_ai.train(
                    ticker,
                    features[ticker],
                    close=price_data[ticker]["Close"],
                    horizon=self.config.prediction_horizon,
                )
                if not trained:
                    raise RuntimeError("training returned False")
                predictions[ticker] = self.ensemble_ai.predict(
                    ticker,
                    features[ticker],
                    horizon=self.config.prediction_horizon,
                    strict=True,
                )
            except Exception as exc:
                failures.append(f"{ticker}: {exc}")
        if not predictions:
            message = "no model produced a verified prediction"
            stages.add("ensemble", started, status="failed", error=message, warnings=failures)
            raise PipelineStageError("ensemble", message)
        if failures and self.config.strict_stages:
            stages.add("ensemble", started, status="failed", error="; ".join(failures))
            raise PipelineStageError("ensemble", "one or more model jobs failed")
        warnings.extend(failures)
        stages.add(
            "ensemble",
            started,
            status="partial" if failures else "success",
            records=len(predictions),
            warnings=failures,
        )
        return predictions

    def _detect_regime(
        self,
        price_data: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame],
        stages: _StageRecorder,
        warnings: list[str],
    ) -> tuple[MarketRegime, str]:
        started = time.perf_counter()
        stage_warnings: list[str] = []
        ticker = self.config.benchmark
        if ticker not in price_data:
            ticker = max(price_data, key=lambda key: len(price_data[key]))
            stage_warnings.append(
                f"Benchmark {self.config.benchmark} unavailable; regime uses {ticker}."
            )
        try:
            regime = self.regime_detector.fit_detect(price_data[ticker])
        except Exception as exc:
            stages.add("regime", started, status="failed", error=str(exc))
            raise PipelineStageError("regime", str(exc)) from exc
        warnings.extend(stage_warnings)
        stages.add(
            "regime",
            started,
            status="partial" if stage_warnings else "success",
            records=1,
            warnings=stage_warnings,
        )
        return regime, ticker

    def _generate_strategies(
        self,
        regime: MarketRegime,
        predictions: Mapping[str, EnsemblePrediction],
        stages: _StageRecorder,
    ) -> list[Strategy]:
        started = time.perf_counter()
        try:
            strategies = self.strategy_engine.generate_strategies(
                regime, predictions, self.config.risk_tolerance
            )
            if not strategies:
                raise RuntimeError("strategy engine returned no eligible strategy")
        except Exception as exc:
            stages.add("strategies", started, status="failed", error=str(exc))
            raise PipelineStageError("strategies", str(exc)) from exc
        stages.add("strategies", started, records=len(strategies))
        return list(strategies)

    def _analyze_risk(
        self,
        price_data: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame],
        benchmark_ticker: str,
        stages: _StageRecorder,
        warnings: list[str],
    ) -> dict[str, RiskReport]:
        started = time.perf_counter()
        reports: dict[str, RiskReport] = {}
        failures: list[str] = []
        benchmark = price_data[benchmark_ticker]
        for ticker in self.config.tickers:
            if ticker not in price_data:
                continue
            try:
                reports[ticker] = self.risk_intelligence.analyze(
                    ticker, price_data[ticker], benchmark
                )
            except Exception as exc:
                failures.append(f"{ticker}: {exc}")
        if not reports:
            stages.add("risk", started, status="failed", error="; ".join(failures))
            raise PipelineStageError("risk", "no verified risk report")
        if failures and self.config.strict_stages:
            stages.add("risk", started, status="failed", error="; ".join(failures))
            raise PipelineStageError("risk", "one or more risk jobs failed")
        warnings.extend(failures)
        stages.add(
            "risk",
            started,
            status="partial" if failures else "success",
            records=len(reports),
            warnings=failures,
        )
        return reports

    def _explain(
        self,
        predictions: Mapping[str, EnsemblePrediction],
        risk_reports: Mapping[str, RiskReport],
        features: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame],
        strategies: Sequence[Strategy],
        stages: _StageRecorder,
        warnings: list[str],
    ) -> dict[str, XAIExplanation]:
        started = time.perf_counter()
        explanations: dict[str, XAIExplanation] = {}
        failures: list[str] = []
        top_allocations = strategies[0].allocations if strategies else {}
        for ticker in predictions.keys() & risk_reports.keys() & features.keys():
            try:
                explanations[ticker] = self.xai.explain(
                    ticker,
                    predictions[ticker],
                    risk_reports[ticker],
                    features[ticker],
                    allocation_pct=100.0 * float(top_allocations.get(ticker, 0.0)),
                )
            except Exception as exc:
                failures.append(f"{ticker}: {exc}")
        if not explanations and not failures:
            failures.append("No ticker had prediction, risk and feature evidence together.")
        if failures and self.config.strict_stages:
            stages.add(
                "explanations", started, status="failed", error="; ".join(failures)
            )
            raise PipelineStageError("explanations", "explanation evidence is incomplete")
        warnings.extend(failures)
        status = "success" if explanations and not failures else "partial"
        stages.add(
            "explanations",
            started,
            status=status,
            records=len(explanations),
            warnings=failures,
        )
        return explanations

    def _run_agents(
        self,
        price_data: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame],
        regime: MarketRegime,
        predictions: Mapping[str, EnsemblePrediction],
        stages: _StageRecorder,
        warnings: list[str],
        news_texts: Optional[Mapping[str, Sequence[str]]],
        current_weights: Optional[Mapping[str, float]],
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        started = time.perf_counter()
        tickers = [ticker for ticker in self.config.tickers if ticker in price_data]
        if len(tickers) < 2:
            warning = "Multi-agent portfolio stage requires at least two usable assets."
            warnings.append(warning)
            stages.add("agents", started, status="skipped", warnings=[warning])
            return {}
        try:
            signals = self.mas.run(
                tickers,
                price_data,
                regime,
                predictions,
                self.config.opt_method,
                news_texts=news_texts,
                current_weights=current_weights,
                max_turnover=self.config.max_turnover,
                strict=self.config.strict_stages,
            )
            stages.add("agents", started, records=len(signals))
            return dict(signals)
        except Exception as exc:
            return self._optional_failure("agents", started, exc, stages, warnings, {})

    def _run_backtests(
        self,
        strategies: Sequence[Strategy],
        price_data: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame],
        stages: _StageRecorder,
        warnings: list[str],
    ) -> list[BacktestResult]:
        started = time.perf_counter()
        if not self.config.run_backtests or self.config.max_backtests == 0:
            stages.add("backtests", started, status="skipped")
            return []
        results: list[BacktestResult] = []
        failures: list[str] = []
        for strategy in strategies[: self.config.max_backtests]:
            try:
                results.append(
                    self.backtest_lab.run(
                        strategy,
                        price_data,
                        use_trailing_stop=self.config.use_trailing_stop,
                        sl_vol_multiplier=self.config.sl_vol_multiplier,
                        volatility_window=self.config.volatility_window,
                    )
                )
            except Exception as exc:
                failures.append(f"{strategy.name}: {exc}")
        if failures and self.config.strict_stages:
            stages.add("backtests", started, status="failed", error="; ".join(failures))
            raise PipelineStageError("backtests", "one or more backtests failed")
        warnings.extend(failures)
        stages.add(
            "backtests",
            started,
            status="partial" if failures else "success",
            records=len(results),
            warnings=failures,
        )
        return results

    def _run_scenarios(
        self,
        strategies: Sequence[Strategy],
        stages: _StageRecorder,
        warnings: list[str],
    ) -> list[ScenarioResult]:
        started = time.perf_counter()
        if not self.config.run_scenarios:
            stages.add("scenarios", started, status="skipped")
            return []
        portfolio = dict(strategies[0].allocations)
        try:
            if self.config.scenario_keys:
                results = [
                    self.scenario_simulator.simulate(key, portfolio)
                    for key in self.config.scenario_keys
                ]
            else:
                results = self.scenario_simulator.run_all(portfolio)
            stages.add("scenarios", started, records=len(results))
            return list(results)
        except Exception as exc:
            return self._optional_failure(
                "scenarios", started, exc, stages, warnings, []
            )

    def _persist(
        self,
        regime: MarketRegime,
        strategies: Sequence[Strategy],
        backtests: Sequence[BacktestResult],
        scenarios: Sequence[ScenarioResult],
        stages: _StageRecorder,
        warnings: list[str],
    ) -> None:
        started = time.perf_counter()
        if not self.config.persist_results:
            stages.add("persistence", started, status="skipped")
            return
        failures: list[str] = []
        operations: list[tuple[str, Callable[[], Any]]] = [
            ("regime", lambda: self.db.log_regime(regime)),
            *[
                (
                    f"strategy:{strategy.name}",
                    lambda strategy=strategy: self.db.log_strategy(strategy, regime.name),
                )
                for strategy in strategies[:3]
            ],
            *[
                (
                    f"backtest:{result.strategy_name}",
                    lambda result=result: self.db.log_backtest(result),
                )
                for result in backtests
            ],
            *[
                (
                    f"scenario:{result.scenario_name}",
                    lambda result=result: self.db.log_scenario(result),
                )
                for result in scenarios
            ],
        ]
        saved = 0
        for label, operation in operations:
            try:
                operation()
                saved += 1
            except Exception as exc:
                failures.append(f"{label}: {exc}")
        if failures and self.config.strict_stages:
            stages.add("persistence", started, status="failed", error="; ".join(failures))
            raise PipelineStageError("persistence", "one or more writes failed")
        warnings.extend(failures)
        stages.add(
            "persistence",
            started,
            status="partial" if failures else "success",
            records=saved,
            warnings=failures,
        )

    def _build_report(
        self,
        regime: MarketRegime,
        strategies: Sequence[Strategy],
        agent_signals: Mapping[str, Any],
        risk_reports: Mapping[str, RiskReport],
        explanations: Mapping[str, XAIExplanation],
        backtests: Sequence[BacktestResult],
        scenarios: Sequence[ScenarioResult],
        source_metadata: Mapping[str, Any],
        timestamp: datetime,
        stages: _StageRecorder,
    ) -> str:
        started = time.perf_counter()
        try:
            report = self.reporter.generate_text_report(
                regime,
                strategies,
                agent_signals,
                risk_reports,
                explanations,
                backtests,
                scenarios,
                timestamp,
                source_metadata=source_metadata,
            )
            if not isinstance(report, str) or not report.strip():
                raise RuntimeError("report generator returned empty text")
        except Exception as exc:
            stages.add("report", started, status="failed", error=str(exc))
            raise PipelineStageError("report", str(exc)) from exc
        stages.add("report", started, records=1)
        return report

    def _save_report(
        self,
        report_text: str,
        stages: _StageRecorder,
        warnings: list[str],
    ) -> Optional[Path]:
        started = time.perf_counter()
        if not self.config.save_report:
            stages.add("report_save", started, status="skipped")
            return None
        try:
            path = Path(self.reporter.save_report(report_text, self.config.report_path))
            stages.add("report_save", started, records=1)
            return path
        except Exception as exc:
            return self._optional_failure(
                "report_save", started, exc, stages, warnings, None
            )

    def _create_dashboard(
        self,
        price_data: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame],
        regime: MarketRegime,
        strategies: Sequence[Strategy],
        backtests: Sequence[BacktestResult],
        risk_reports: Mapping[str, RiskReport],
        source_metadata: Mapping[str, Any],
        timestamp: datetime,
        stages: _StageRecorder,
        warnings: list[str],
    ) -> Optional[Path]:
        started = time.perf_counter()
        if not self.config.generate_dashboard:
            stages.add("dashboard", started, status="skipped")
            return None
        try:
            ok = self.reporter.generate_plotly_dashboard(
                price_data,
                regime,
                strategies,
                backtests,
                risk_reports,
                output_path=self.config.dashboard_path,
                timestamp=timestamp,
                source_metadata=source_metadata,
            )
            if not ok:
                raise RuntimeError("dashboard renderer returned False")
            stages.add("dashboard", started, records=1)
            return self.config.dashboard_path
        except Exception as exc:
            return self._optional_failure(
                "dashboard", started, exc, stages, warnings, None
            )

    def backtest_strategy(
        self,
        strategy_name: str,
        tickers: Optional[Sequence[str]] = None,
        **backtest_options: Any,
    ) -> BacktestResult:
        """Backtest one exact strategy; never substitutes an arbitrary strategy."""
        name = strategy_name.strip().casefold()
        if not name:
            raise ValueError("strategy_name is required")
        symbols = _orchestrator_normalise_tickers(tickers or self.config.tickers)
        data = self.data_engine.fetch_multiple(
            list(symbols),
            self.config.lookback_days,
            self.config.max_workers,
            allow_synthetic=self.config.allow_synthetic,
            strict=True,
        )
        regime_source = data.get(self.config.benchmark)
        if regime_source is None:
            regime_source = max(data.values(), key=len)
        regime = self.regime_detector.fit_detect(regime_source)
        features = {
            ticker: self.data_engine.compute_features(frame)
            for ticker, frame in data.items()
        }
        predictions: dict[str, EnsemblePrediction] = {}
        for ticker, feature_frame in features.items():
            if self.ensemble_ai.train(
                ticker,
                feature_frame,
                close=data[ticker]["Close"],
                horizon=self.config.prediction_horizon,
            ):
                predictions[ticker] = self.ensemble_ai.predict(
                    ticker,
                    feature_frame,
                    self.config.prediction_horizon,
                    strict=True,
                )
        strategies = self.strategy_engine.generate_strategies(
            regime, predictions, self.config.risk_tolerance
        )
        matches = [strategy for strategy in strategies if strategy.name.casefold() == name]
        if len(matches) != 1:
            available = ", ".join(strategy.name for strategy in strategies) or "none"
            raise LookupError(
                f"strategy {strategy_name!r} was not found; available: {available}"
            )
        options = {
            "use_trailing_stop": self.config.use_trailing_stop,
            "sl_vol_multiplier": self.config.sl_vol_multiplier,
            "volatility_window": self.config.volatility_window,
            **backtest_options,
        }
        return self.backtest_lab.run(matches[0], data, **options)

    def simulate_scenario(
        self,
        scenario_key: str,
        portfolio: Mapping[str, float],
        custom_shocks: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None,
    ) -> ScenarioResult:
        """Run one explicit scenario without console or persistence side effects."""
        if not portfolio:
            raise ValueError("portfolio cannot be empty")
        return self.scenario_simulator.simulate(
            scenario_key, portfolio, custom_shocks
        )

    def quick_predict(
        self, ticker: str, *, force_refresh: bool = False
    ) -> EnsemblePrediction:
        """Fetch, train and predict one ticker, failing loudly on weak evidence."""
        symbol = _orchestrator_normalise_tickers([ticker])[0]
        frame = self.data_engine.fetch_ticker(
            symbol,
            self.config.lookback_days,
            force_refresh=force_refresh,
            allow_synthetic=self.config.allow_synthetic,
        )
        if not isinstance(frame, pd.DataFrame) or len(frame) < self.config.min_price_rows:
            raise PipelineStageError("quick_predict", "insufficient market history")
        features = self.data_engine.compute_features(frame)
        if len(features) < self.config.min_feature_rows:
            raise PipelineStageError("quick_predict", "insufficient feature history")
        if not self.ensemble_ai.train(
            symbol,
            features,
            close=frame["Close"],
            horizon=self.config.prediction_horizon,
        ):
            raise PipelineStageError("quick_predict", "model training failed")
        return self.ensemble_ai.predict(
            symbol,
            features,
            self.config.prediction_horizon,
            strict=True,
        )

    def _optional_failure(
        self,
        stage: str,
        started: float,
        exc: Exception,
        stages: _StageRecorder,
        warnings: list[str],
        fallback: Any,
    ) -> Any:
        if self.config.strict_stages:
            stages.add(stage, started, status="failed", error=str(exc))
            raise PipelineStageError(stage, str(exc)) from exc
        warning = f"{stage}: {exc}"
        warnings.append(warning)
        stages.add(stage, started, status="partial", error=str(exc), warnings=[warning])
        return fallback

    @staticmethod
    def _check_cancelled(event: Optional[threading.Event]) -> None:
        if event is not None and event.is_set():
            raise PipelineCancelledError("analysis was cancelled")

    @staticmethod
    def _source_metadata(
        price_data: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame], run_id: str
    ) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
        sources: dict[str, Any] = {}
        for ticker, frame in price_data.items():
            last_index = frame.index[-1] if len(frame) else None
            sources[ticker] = {
                "source": str(frame.attrs.get("source", "unknown")),
                "rows": int(len(frame)),
                "as_of": None if last_index is None else str(last_index),
            }
        return {"run_id": run_id, "market_data": sources}


def _ticker_argument(value: str) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    """Parse a comma-separated ticker list for argparse."""
    try:
    return _orchestrator_normalise_tickers(value.split(","))
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError(str(exc)) from exc


class _ArgumentParser(argparse.ArgumentParser):
    """Argument parser with a stable configuration-error exit code."""

    def error(self, message: str) -> None:
        self.print_usage(sys.stderr)
        self.exit(3, f"{self.prog}: configuration error: {message}\n")


def build_argument_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    """Build the command-line contract without starting the application."""
    parser = _ArgumentParser(
        prog="smartinvest",
        description=(
            "Run the SmartInvest 4.8 analysis pipeline. API keys and the default "
            "database/log paths are loaded from environment variables by Module 1."
        ),
        formatter_class=argparse.ArgumentDefaultsHelpFormatter,
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--tickers",
        type=_ticker_argument,
        help="Comma-separated ticker universe; defaults to SMARTINVEST_TICKERS.",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--benchmark", help="Market benchmark ticker.")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--lookback-days", type=int, help="Number of calendar days to request."
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--horizon", type=int, default=5, help="Prediction horizon in trading days."
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--risk-tolerance",
        choices=sorted(_RISK_TOLERANCES),
        default="medium",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--optimizer",
        choices=sorted(_OPTIMIZERS),
        default="max_sharpe",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--max-workers", type=int, default=8, help="Concurrent market-data workers."
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--max-backtests", type=int, default=3, help="Maximum ranked strategies to test."
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--backtests",
        action=argparse.BooleanOptionalAction,
        default=True,
        help="Enable or disable strategy backtests.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--scenarios",
        action=argparse.BooleanOptionalAction,
        default=True,
        help="Enable or disable the scenario suite.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--dashboard",
        action=argparse.BooleanOptionalAction,
        default=True,
        help="Enable or disable the HTML dashboard.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--save-report",
        action=argparse.BooleanOptionalAction,
        default=True,
        help="Enable or disable saving the text report.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--persist",
        action=argparse.BooleanOptionalAction,
        default=True,
        help="Enable or disable SQLite audit persistence.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--trailing-stop",
        action=argparse.BooleanOptionalAction,
        default=True,
        help="Enable volatility-scaled trailing stops in backtests.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--stop-vol-multiplier",
        type=float,
        default=2.0,
        help="Trailing-stop distance as a volatility multiple.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--volatility-window",
        type=int,
        default=20,
        help="Rolling volatility window used by trailing stops.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--strict-data",
        action="store_true",
        help="Fail if any requested market series is unavailable or rejected.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--strict-stages",
        action="store_true",
        help="Fail when any analytical or optional stage is incomplete.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--allow-synthetic",
        action="store_true",
        help="Explicitly permit synthetic fallback data.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--force-refresh",
        action="store_true",
        help="Bypass fresh cached market data.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--report-path",
        type=Path,
        default=Path("smartinvest_4_8_report.txt"),
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--dashboard-path",
        type=Path,
        default=Path("smartinvest_4_8_dashboard.html"),
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--print-report",
        action="store_true",
        help="Print the complete report after the run.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--demo-scenario",
        metavar="KEY",
        help="After a successful run, execute one demonstration scenario.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--demo-prediction",
        metavar="TICKER",
        help="After a successful run, execute one single-ticker prediction.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--quiet",
        action="store_true",
        help="Suppress the banner and normal progress summary.",
    )
    return parser


def _install_cancellation_handlers(event: threading.Event) -> dict[int, Any]:
    """Install cooperative stop handlers and return handlers to restore."""
    previous: dict[int, Any] = {}
    if threading.current_thread() is not threading.main_thread():
        return previous

    def request_stop(signum: int, _frame: Any) -> None:
        LOGGER.warning("Received signal %s; cancellation requested.", signum)
        event.set()

    for signum in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM):
        try:
            previous[signum] = signal.getsignal(signum)
            signal.signal(signum, request_stop)
        except (OSError, RuntimeError, ValueError):
            LOGGER.debug("Signal %s is not configurable on this platform.", signum)
    return previous


def _restore_signal_handlers(previous: Mapping[int, Any]) -> None:
    if threading.current_thread() is not threading.main_thread():
        return
    for signum, handler in previous.items():
        try:
            signal.signal(signum, handler)
        except (OSError, RuntimeError, ValueError):
            LOGGER.debug("Could not restore signal %s.", signum)


def _print_run_summary(result: PipelineResult) -> None:
    """Print a concise operational summary, not uncontrolled internal output."""
    outcome = "PARTIAL" if result.partial else "SUCCESS"
    print()
    print(f"Run ID       : {result.run_id}")
    print(f"Outcome      : {outcome}")
    print(
        f"Market regime: {result.regime.name} "
        f"({result.regime.confidence:.1%} confidence)"
    )
    print(f"Predictions  : {len(result.predictions)}")
    print(f"Strategies   : {len(result.strategies)}")
    print(f"Elapsed      : {result.elapsed_seconds:.2f} s")
    if result.report_path is not None:
        print(f"Report       : {result.report_path.resolve()}")
    if result.dashboard_path is not None:
        print(f"Dashboard    : {result.dashboard_path.resolve()}")
    if result.warnings:
        print(f"Warnings     : {len(result.warnings)}")


def main(argv: Optional[Sequence[str]] = None) -> int:
    """Command-line entry point.

    Return codes:
        0 - complete success
        2 - completed with partial/optional-stage failures
        3 - invalid configuration or command-line input
        4 - cancelled by the user or operating system
        5 - pipeline/runtime failure
    """
    parser = build_argument_parser()
    try:
        args = parser.parse_args(argv)
        secure = SecureConfig.from_env()
        config = OrchestratorConfig.from_secure_config(
            secure,
            tickers=args.tickers or tuple(secure.default_tickers),
            benchmark=args.benchmark or secure.benchmark,
            lookback_days=(
                secure.lookback_days
                if args.lookback_days is None
                else args.lookback_days
            ),
            prediction_horizon=args.horizon,
            risk_tolerance=args.risk_tolerance,
            opt_method=args.optimizer,
            run_backtests=args.backtests,
            run_scenarios=args.scenarios,
            generate_dashboard=args.dashboard,
            save_report=args.save_report,
            persist_results=args.persist,
            strict_data=args.strict_data,
            strict_stages=args.strict_stages,
            allow_synthetic=args.allow_synthetic,
            force_refresh=args.force_refresh,
            max_workers=args.max_workers,
            max_backtests=args.max_backtests,
            use_trailing_stop=args.trailing_stop,
            sl_vol_multiplier=args.stop_vol_multiplier,
            volatility_window=args.volatility_window,
            report_path=args.report_path,
            dashboard_path=args.dashboard_path,
        )
    except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
        parser.error(str(exc))
        return 3  # pragma: no cover - argparse exits before this line

    if not args.quiet:
        print(
            "\n"
            "SmartInvest 4.8 - Dynamic Risk Control Edition\n"
            f"Starting analysis for {len(config.tickers)} assets "
            f"(configuration {config.fingerprint()})..."
        )

    cancellation_event = threading.Event()
    previous_handlers = _install_cancellation_handlers(cancellation_event)
    try:
        engine = SmartInvest48(
            config=config,
            secure_config=secure,
        )
        result = engine.run_detailed(cancellation_event=cancellation_event)

        if args.print_report:
            print("\n" + result.report_text.rstrip())
        if not args.quiet:
            _print_run_summary(result)

        if args.demo_scenario:
            demo = engine.simulate_scenario(
                args.demo_scenario,
                portfolio={
                    "equities": 0.50,
                    "bonds": 0.25,
                    "gold": 0.15,
                    "cash": 0.10,
                },
            )
            if not args.quiet:
                print(
                    f"\nScenario {demo.scenario_name}: "
                    f"portfolio return {demo.portfolio_return:+.2%}, "
                    f"max loss {demo.max_loss:.2%}"
                )

        if args.demo_prediction:
            prediction = engine.quick_predict(args.demo_prediction)
            if not args.quiet:
                print(
                    f"\nPrediction {prediction.ticker}: "
                    f"{prediction.direction}, "
                    f"return {prediction.ensemble_pred:+.2%}, "
                    f"confidence {prediction.confidence:.1%}"
                )
        return 2 if result.partial else 0
    except PipelineCancelledError:
        LOGGER.warning("SmartInvest analysis cancelled.")
        return 4
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        LOGGER.warning("SmartInvest analysis interrupted by the user.")
        return 4
    except (OrchestratorError, OSError) as exc:
        LOGGER.error("SmartInvest analysis failed: %s", exc)
        return 5
    except Exception:
        LOGGER.exception("Unexpected SmartInvest failure.")
        return 5
    finally:
        _restore_signal_handlers(previous_handlers)


__all__ = [
    "build_argument_parser",
    "main",
    "OrchestratorConfig",
    "OrchestratorError",
    "PipelineBusyError",
    "PipelineCancelledError",
    "PipelineResult",
    "PipelineStageError",
    "SmartInvest48",
    "SmartInvestComponents",
    "StageStatus",
]


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
